============== START FILE: modAccChartOfAccounts.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Function FindGLAccountID(glAccountNumber As String, Optional continue As Boolean) As Long
Dim ID

  ID = DLookup("GLAccountID", "tblAccAcc_GLAccounts", "companyID=" & currentCompanyID() & " And glAccountNumber='" & glAccountNumber & "'")
  
  If IsNull(ID) Then
    If continue Then
      FindGLAccountID = 0
    Else
      MsgBox "Ne najdem konta " & glAccountNumber & ". Program bo prekinjen.", vbExclamation
      End
    End If
  Else
    FindGLAccountID = ID
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function FindCountryGAccountID(partnerID As Long, PR As String) As Long
Dim ID
  
  Call CreateQry(121, partnerID)
  
  If PR = "R" Then
    ID = DLookup("receivablesGLAccountID", "qryAccAcc_countryGLAccounts")
  Else
    ID = DLookup("payablesGLAccountID", "qryAccAcc_countryGLAccounts")
  End If
  
  If IsNull(ID) Then
    'MsgBox "Ne najdem konta.", vbExclamation
    End
  Else
    FindCountryGAccountID = ID
  End If
  
End Function

============== END FILE: modAccChartOfAccounts.txt ================



============== START FILE: modAccFS.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Function Indent(howMuch As Integer) As String
Dim i As Integer

    i = 0
    Do While i < howMuch
        Indent = "    " & Indent
        i = i + 1
    Loop

End Function

Public Sub CalculateFSReport()
'On Error Resume Next
Dim frm As Form, maxLevel, db As Database, q1 As QueryDef, q2 As QueryDef, i As Integer

  Set frm = Forms!frmAccFSRpts!frmAccFSRpts_Reports.Form

  If IsNull(frm!reportID) Then Beep: MsgBox "Izberi finan no poro ilo!": Exit Sub
  If IsNull(frm!fromMonth) Then Beep: MsgBox "Manjka za etni mesec!": Exit Sub
  If IsNull(frm!toMonth) Then Beep: MsgBox "Manjka kon ni mesec!": Exit Sub
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblAccFS_ReportDetails WHERE tblAccFS_ReportDetails.reportID=" & frm!reportID)

  If frm!isOfficialStatement = 1 Then
    Call CreateQry1(90, currentCompanyID(), frm!fiscalYear, frm!fromMonth, frm!toMonth)
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccFSReport_01"
  Else
    Call CreateQry1(91, currentCompanyID(), frm!fiscalYear, frm!fromMonth, frm!toMonth)
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccFSReport_01b"
  End If
  
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdAccFSReport_02"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccFSReport_03"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  maxLevel = DMax("level", "tblAccFS_ReportDetails", "reportID=" & frm!reportID) - 2
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set q1 = db.QueryDefs("qappAccFSReport_04")
  q1.Parameters("repID") = frm!reportID
  q1.Parameters("statID") = frm!statementID
  Set q2 = db.QueryDefs("qappAccFSReport_05")
  q2.Parameters("repID") = frm!reportID
  q2.Parameters("statID") = frm!statementID
  
  For i = maxLevel To 0 Step -1
    q1.Parameters("i") = i
    q1.Execute
    q2.Parameters("i") = i
    q2.Execute
  Next i
  
End Sub

============== END FILE: modAccFS.txt ================



============== START FILE: modAccGLPostings.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Sub PostingErrorsFltr()
'On Error GoTo end_exit
Dim noOfRecords As Long, f As Form
  
  Set f = Forms!frmAccGL_Postings.Form

  noOfRecords = DCount("postingID", "qryAccGL_ErrNotZeroPostings")
  If noOfRecords > 0 Then
    f!cmdPostningsNotZero.Caption = "Temeljnice <> 0 (" & noOfRecords & ")"
    f!cmdPostningsNotZero.visible = True
  Else
    f!cmdPostningsNotZero.visible = False
  End If
  
  noOfRecords = DCount("postingName", "qryAccGL_ErrDuplicatePostings")
  If noOfRecords > 0 Then
    f!cmdDoublePostings.Caption = "Podvojene temeljnice (" & noOfRecords & ")"
    f!cmdDoublePostings.visible = True
  Else
    f!cmdDoublePostings.visible = False
  End If
end_exit:

End Sub

Public Sub CreatePosting(Optional compID As Long)
Dim cmpyID As Long, strSQL As String, resp As Integer, newPostingID As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset, rs2 As DAO.Recordset

  cmpyID = IIf(compID = 0, currentCompanyID(), compID)
  
  strSQL = "SELECT postingName, fiscalDate, parentID, parentTableID, postingTypeID, fiscalYear " & _
           "FROM tblTempAccGLPostings " & _
           "GROUP BY postingName, fiscalDate, parentID, parentTableID, postingTypeID, fiscalYear;"
  
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Ni zapisov! Temeljnice ni mogo e ustvariti!", vbCritical)
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF

    If Not IsNull(DFirst("closedAt", "tblAccFP_CmpyFiscalYears", "(companyID=" & cmpyID & ") AND (fiscalYear=" & rs("fiscalYear") & ")")) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Napaka pri knji enju dokumenta: " & rs("postingName") & "! Knji ba za leto " & rs("fiscalYear") & " ni ve  mo na, ker je leto zaklju eno! Izvajanje programa bo prekinjeno!", vbExclamation
      Exit Sub
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF

    If IsNull(DFirst("companyID", "tblAccFP_CmpyFiscalYears", "(companyID=" & cmpyID & ") AND (fiscalYear=" & rs("fiscalYear") & ")")) Then
      Beep
      resp = MsgBox("Pri knji enju dokumenta: " & rs("postingName") & ", je potrebno ustvariti temeljnico v letu " & rs("fiscalYear") & _
      ", ki  e ni odprto. Knji ba v glavni knjigi ne bo vidna dokler leto ne bo odprto. Ali nadaljujem s knji enjem? ", vbYesNo + vbExclamation)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  
  Loop
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccGL_PostingTemp"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qdelAccGL_PostingTemp"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  strSQL = "SELECT fiscalYear, Sum([debitAmount]-[creditAmount]) AS diff " & _
           "FROM tblTempAccGLPostings " & _
           "GROUP BY fiscalYear " & _
           "HAVING (((Sum([debitAmount]-[creditAmount]))<>0));"
           
  Set rs2 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs2.RecordCount <> 0 Then
    Beep
    rs.MoveFirst
    resp = MsgBox("Napaka pri knji enju dokumenta " & rs("postingName") & ". Vsota temeljnice ne bo enaka 0 - razlika=" & rs2("diff") & "! Ali kljub temu ustvarim temeljnico?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo)
    If resp <> vbYes Then GoTo exit_CreatePosting
  End If
 
  Call PostingSequence
    
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF
    If Not IsNull(DLookup("postingID", "tblAccGL_Postings", "companyID=" & cmpyID & " And postingName='" & rs("postingName") & "' And fiscalYear=" & rs("fiscalYear"))) Then
      resp = MsgBox("Temeljnica z nazivom " & rs("postingName") & " v letu " & rs("fiscalYear") & "  e obstaja! Ali naj nadaljujem in ustvarim podvojeno temeljnico? " & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
      "YES = podvojena temeljnica bo ustvarjena, program bo nadaljeval z knji enjem (morebitnih) ostalih dokumentov" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
      "NO = podvojena temeljnica ne bo ustvarjena, program bo nadaljeval z knji enjem (morebitnih) ostalih dokumentov" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
      "CANCEL = podvojena temeljnica ne bo ustvarjena, program bo prekinjen " _
      , vbCritical + vbYesNoCancel)
      If resp <> vbYes Then GoTo exit_CreatePosting
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF
    Set rs1 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblAccGL_Postings", dbOpenDynaset, dbSeeChanges)
    rs1.AddNew
    rs1("postingName") = rs("postingName")
    rs1("fiscalDate") = Int(rs("fiscalDate"))
    rs1("parentID") = IIf(IsNull(rs("parentID")), 0, rs("parentID"))
    rs1("parentTableID") = IIf(IsNull(rs("parentTableID")), 0, rs("parentTableID"))
    rs1("postingTypeID") = rs("postingTypeID")
    rs1("fiscalYear") = rs("fiscalYear")
    rs1("companyID") = cmpyID
    rs1.Update
    rs1.Bookmark = rs1.LastModified
    newPostingID = rs1("postingID")
      
    strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblAccGL_PostingSubs (postingID, sequence, postingDescription, GLAccountID, partnerID, fiscalMonth, dueDate, " & _
             "costCenterID, debitAmount, creditAmount, linkName, bpartnerID, bGLAccountID, bdebitAmount, bcreditAmount, documentID ) " & _
             "SELECT " & newPostingID & " AS postID, sequence, postingDescription, GLAccountID, partnerID, " & _
             "fiscalMonth, dueDate, costCenterID, " & _
             "IIf([toBeBalanced], IIf([debitAmount]>[creditAmount],[debitAmount]-[creditAmount],0), debitAmount), " & _
             "IIf([toBeBalanced], IIf([creditAmount]>[debitAmount],[creditAmount]-[debitAmount],0), creditAmount), " & _
             "linkName, bpartnerID, bGLAccountID, bdebitAmount, bcreditAmount, documentID " & _
             "FROM tblTempAccGLPostings " & _
             "WHERE ([fiscalYear]=" & rs("fiscalYear") & ") And ((debitAmount-creditAmount)<>0);"
    Call RunSQL(strSQL)
    rs.MoveNext
    rs1.Close
  Loop

exit_CreatePosting:
  rs.Close
  rs2.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  Set rs1 = Nothing
  Set rs2 = Nothing
  If resp = vbCancel Then
    DoCmd.Hourglass False
    End
  End If

End Sub

Sub PostingSequence()
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, i As Integer, Yr As Long

  strSQL = "SELECT fiscalYear, tblTempAccGLPostings.sequence, tblTempAccGLPostings.dueDate FROM tblTempAccGLPostings " & _
           "ORDER BY fiscalYear, tblTempAccGLPostings.txtSequence, tblTempAccGLPostings.dueDate"

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL, dbOpenDynaset)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
  rs.MoveFirst
  Yr = rs("fiscalYear")
  i = 1
  
  Do Until rs.EOF
    rs.Edit
    rs("sequence") = i
    rs.Update
    
    rs.MoveNext
    If rs.EOF Then Exit Do
    
    If rs("fiscalYear") <> Yr Then
      Yr = rs("fiscalYear")
      i = 1
    Else
      i = i + 1
    End If
  Loop

End Sub
Public Sub CreatePostingMySQL(Optional compyID As Long, Optional keepSequence As Boolean)
Dim cmpyID As Long, cmptrID As Long, strSQL As String, strWhere As String, resp As Integer, newPostingID As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset, rs2 As DAO.Recordset

  cmptrID = computerID()
  cmpyID = IIf(compyID = 0, currentCompanyID(), compyID)
  
  strSQL = "SELECT postingName, fiscalDate, parentID, parentTableID, postingTypeID, fiscalYear " & _
           "FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting " & _
           "WHERE tblTempAccGL_Posting.computerID= " & cmptrID & vbCrLf & _
           "GROUP BY postingName, fiscalDate, parentID, parentTableID, postingTypeID, fiscalYear;"
  
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Ni zapisov! Temeljnice ni mogo e ustvariti!", vbCritical)
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF

    If Not IsNull(DFirst("closedAt", "tblAccFP_CmpyFiscalYears", "(companyID=" & cmpyID & ") AND (fiscalYear=" & rs("fiscalYear") & ")")) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Napaka pri knji enju dokumenta: " & rs("postingName") & "! Knji ba za leto " & rs("fiscalYear") & " ni ve  mo na, ker je leto zaklju eno! Izvajanje programa bo prekinjeno!", vbExclamation
      Exit Sub
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  
  Loop
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF

    If YearIsOpened(rs("fiscalYear")) = 0 Then
      Beep
      resp = MsgBox("Pri knji enju dokumenta: " & rs("postingName") & ", je potrebno ustvariti temeljnico v letu " & rs("fiscalYear") & _
      ", ki  e ni odprto. Knji ba v glavni knjigi ne bo vidna dokler leto ne bo odprto. Ali nadaljujem s knji enjem? ", vbYesNo + vbExclamation)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  
  Loop
  
  Call CreateQry(77, cmptrID)
  Call RunMySQL

  strWhere = "WHERE ((debitAmount-creditAmount=0) AND (computerID= " & cmptrID & ")) OR ((toBeDeleted=1) AND (computerID= " & cmptrID & "));"
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting " & strWhere)
  
  If DCount("ID", "tblTempAccGL_Posting") = 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Ni zapisov! Temeljnice ni mogo e ustvariti!", vbCritical)
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  strSQL = "SELECT fiscalYear, Sum([debitAmount]-[creditAmount]) AS diff " & _
           "FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting " & _
           "WHERE tblTempAccGL_Posting.computerID= " & cmptrID & vbCrLf & _
           "GROUP BY fiscalYear " & vbCrLf & _
           "HAVING (((Sum([debitAmount]-[creditAmount]))<>0));"
           
  Set rs2 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs2.RecordCount <> 0 Then
    Beep
    rs.MoveFirst
    resp = MsgBox("Napaka pri knji enju dokumenta " & rs("postingName") & ". Vsota temeljnice ne bo enaka 0 (razlika: " & rs2("diff") & ") ! Ali kljub temu ustvarim temeljnico?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo)
    If resp <> vbYes Then GoTo exit_CreatePosting
  End If
 
  If keepSequence = False Then
    Call PostingSequenceMySQL(cmptrID)
  End If
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF
    If Not IsNull(DLookup("postingID", "tblAccGL_Postings", "companyID=" & cmpyID & " And postingName='" & rs("postingName") & "' And fiscalYear=" & rs("fiscalYear"))) Then
      resp = MsgBox("Temeljnica z nazivom " & rs("postingName") & " v letu " & rs("fiscalYear") & "  e obstaja! Ali naj nadaljujem in ustvarim podvojeno temeljnico? " & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
      "YES = podvojena temeljnica bo ustvarjena, program bo nadaljeval z knji enjem (morebitnih) ostalih dokumentov" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
      "NO = podvojena temeljnica ne bo ustvarjena, program bo nadaljeval z knji enjem (morebitnih) ostalih dokumentov" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
      "CANCEL = podvojena temeljnica ne bo ustvarjena, program bo prekinjen " _
      , vbCritical + vbYesNoCancel)
      If resp <> vbYes Then GoTo exit_CreatePosting
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF
    Set rs1 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblAccGL_Postings", dbOpenDynaset, dbSeeChanges)
    rs1.AddNew
    rs1("postingName") = rs("postingName")
    rs1("fiscalDate") = rs("fiscalDate")
    rs1("parentID") = IIf(IsNull(rs("parentID")), 0, rs("parentID"))
    rs1("parentTableID") = IIf(IsNull(rs("parentTableID")), 0, rs("parentTableID"))
    rs1("postingTypeID") = rs("postingTypeID")
    rs1("fiscalYear") = rs("fiscalYear")
    rs1("companyID") = cmpyID
    rs1.Update
    'rs1.Bookmark = rs1.LastModified
    newPostingID = DMax("postingID", "tblAccGL_Postings", "companyID=" & cmpyID)
    
      
    strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblAccGL_PostingSubs (postingID, sequence, postingDescription, GLAccountID, partnerID, fiscalMonth, dueDate, " & _
             "costCenterID, debitAmount, creditAmount, documentID, bpartnerID, bGLAccountID, bdebitAmount, bcreditAmount) " & _
             "SELECT " & newPostingID & " AS postID, sequence, postingDescription, GLAccountID, partnerID, " & _
             "fiscalMonth, dueDate, costCenterID, " & _
             "If(toBeBalanced=1, If(debitAmount>creditAmount,debitAmount-creditAmount,0), debitAmount), " & _
             "If(toBeBalanced=1, If(creditAmount>debitAmount,creditAmount-debitAmount,0), creditAmount), " & _
             "documentID, bpartnerID, bGLAccountID, bdebitAmount, bcreditAmount " & _
             "FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting " & _
             "WHERE (fiscalYear=" & rs("fiscalYear") & ") AND ((debitAmount-creditAmount)<>0) AND (computerID= " & cmptrID & ");"
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
    rs.MoveNext
    rs1.Close
  Loop

exit_CreatePosting:
  rs.Close
  rs2.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  Set rs1 = Nothing
  Set rs2 = Nothing
  If resp = vbCancel Then
    DoCmd.Hourglass False
    End
  End If
'  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting WHERE tblTempAccGL_Posting.computerID=" & cmptrID)
End Sub

Sub PostingSequenceMySQL(cmptrID As Long)
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, i As Integer, Yr As Long

  strSQL = "SELECT fiscalYear, sequence FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting WHERE computerID=" & cmptrID & vbCrLf & _
           "ORDER BY fiscalYear, txtSequence, dueDate;"

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL, dbOpenDynaset)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
  rs.MoveFirst
  Yr = rs("fiscalYear")
  i = 1
  
  Do Until rs.EOF
    rs.Edit
    rs("sequence") = i
    rs.Update
    
    rs.MoveNext
    If rs.EOF Then Exit Do
    
    If rs("fiscalYear") <> Yr Then
      Yr = rs("fiscalYear")
      i = 1
    Else
      i = i + 1
    End If
  Loop

End Sub

Public Sub CloseFormPosting(f As Form)
'On Error Resume Next
Dim frmName As String, frm As Form, ID As Long, resp As Integer

  frmName = "frmAccGL_Postings"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    resp = MsgBox("Ali osve im GK? (traja lahko nekaj sekund)", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
    If resp = vbYes Then
      Set frm = Forms(frmName)
      Call PostingErrorsFltr
      If Not IsNull(f!postingID) Then Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm!frmAccGL_PostingsSub.Form, "postingID", f!postingID)
    End If
  End If
  
  frmName = "frmAccPR"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Call RefreshPostingSub
  End If
    
  frmName = "frmAccARAP"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm.lstDocuments.Requery
    frm.lstPayments.Requery
  End If

  frmName = "frmAccPmtJournals"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    ID = frm!paymentJournalID
    frm!frmAccPmtJournals_Journals.Form.RecordSource = "qryAccPmtJournals_Journals"
    Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm!frmAccPmtJournals_Journals.Form, "paymentJournalID", ID)
  End If

  frmName = "frmEmpWageCalc"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm!frmEmpWageCalcWageCalculations.Requery
  End If

  frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoiceGGPostings"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    Set frm = frm!frmPurchaseInvoiceGGPostingsSub.Form
    Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm, "purchaseInvoiceID", (frm!purchaseInvoiceID))
  End If
  
  frmName = "frmFADepreciationCalcMain"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    Set frm = frm!frmFADepreciationCalcCalcs.Form
    Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm, "depreciationCalcID", (frm!depreciationCalcID))
  End If
  
  frmName = "frmTourSales_ggInvoices"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm, "ggInvoiceID", frm!ggInvoiceID)
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub ShowBusinessData(frm As Form, showData As Boolean, size As Integer)

  frm!bGLAccountID.ColumnHidden = Not showData
  frm!bPartnerID.ColumnHidden = Not showData
  frm!bDebitAmount.ColumnHidden = Not showData
  frm!bCreditAmount.ColumnHidden = Not showData

  If showData Then
    frm!bGLAccountID.RowSource = "qryAccAcc_GLAccounts"
    frm!bPartnerID.RowSource = "qryPtr_ptrCmpyPartners"
    frm!bGLAccountID.ColumnWidth = size
    frm!bPartnerID.ColumnWidth = size - 1000
    frm!bDebitAmount.ColumnWidth = size - 1900
    frm!bCreditAmount.ColumnWidth = size - 1900
  Else
    frm!bGLAccountID.RowSource = ""
    frm!bPartnerID.RowSource = ""
  End If

End Sub

Public Sub DeletePosting(frm As Form)
Dim strSQL As String, resp As Integer
  
  Beep
  resp = MsgBox("Ali resni no  eli  brisati knji bo " & frm!postingName & "?", vbCritical + vbYesNo)
  If Not resp = vbYes Then End
  strSQL = "DELETE FROM tblAccGL_Postings WHERE postingID=" & frm!postingID & ";"
  Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
  
End Sub

Public Sub OpenPosting(postID As Long, postSubID As Long)
Dim frm As Form

  DoCmd.OpenForm "frmAccGL_Posting", , , "postingID=" & postID
  Set frm = Forms!frmAccGL_Posting!frmAccGL_PostingSub.Form
  
  Forms!frmAccGL_Posting!frmAccGL_PostingSub.SetFocus
  
  With frm.RecordsetClone
    .FindFirst "postingSubID=" & postSubID

    If Not .NoMatch Then
      frm.Bookmark = .Bookmark
    End If
  End With
  
End Sub

Public Sub PurchDetailsTemp()
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, i As Integer, purchaseInvoiceID As Long

  strSQL = "SELECT tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.purchaseInvoiceID, tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.lockedAt " & _
           " FROM tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices LEFT JOIN tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails ON tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.purchaseInvoiceID = tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails.purchaseInvoiceID " & _
           " WHERE (((tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.companyID)=1004) AND ((tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.documentTypeID)=6) AND ((tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails.purchaseInvoiceDetailID) Is Null)) GROUP BY tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.purchaseInvoiceID, tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.lockedAt;"

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  rs.MoveFirst
  i = 0
  Do While Not rs.EOF
  i = i + 1
  purchaseInvoiceID = rs("purchaseInvoiceID")
      Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails WHERE purchaseInvoiceID=" & purchaseInvoiceID)
      If IsNull(rs("lockedAt")) Then
        GoTo nadaljuj:
      End If
        
'      If Not (Abs(n(DLookup("diff", "qryTourSales_unlockedPurchaseInvoicesSub2", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & purchaseInvoiceID))) < 1) Then
'        GoTo nadaljuj:
'      End If
  
      Call CreateQry(126, purchaseInvoiceID)
      Call RunMySQL
      Call CreateQry(210, purchaseInvoiceID)
      Call RunMySQL
nadaljuj:
      rs.MoveNext
      
  Loop
Beep
MsgBox "I=10"

End Sub

Public Sub TurismPostingTemp()
Dim frm As Form, resp As Integer
Dim qDef As QueryDef, frmName As String
Dim db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, NoOfDays As Integer, i As Integer, fiscalDate As Date, GLAccID As String, totalCost As Double
Dim strSQL As String, purchaseInvoiceID As Long

  strSQL = "SELECT tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.purchaseInvoiceID " & _
           " FROM tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices LEFT JOIN tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails ON tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.purchaseInvoiceID = tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails.purchaseInvoiceID " & _
           " WHERE (((tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.companyID)=1004) AND ((tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.documentTypeID)=6)) GROUP BY tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.purchaseInvoiceID, tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices.lockedAt;"

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  rs.MoveFirst
  i = 0
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    purchaseInvoiceID = rs("purchaseInvoiceID")
    Set qDef = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("0_qappPurchaseInv_Posting_41_sub1")
    qDef.Parameters("invID") = purchaseInvoiceID
    qDef.Execute
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
      
      
End Sub

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit
Public Function NewPOID(Optional parentID, Optional parentTableID, Optional pCode, Optional pDescr, Optional execDate, Optional pAmount, Optional bpID, Optional crName, Optional crStreet, Optional crPlace, Optional crBIC, Optional crIBAN, Optional crRef, Optional crRefInf, Optional GLAccountID, Optional relationshipName, Optional docID, Optional permanent) As Long
Dim errMsg As String
  
  NewPOID = 0
  errMsg = "Napaka pri vhodnih podatkih: " & vbCrLf & vbCrLf
  If Not IsNumeric(parentID) Then errMsg = errMsg & "- parentID" & vbCrLf
  If Not IsNumeric(parentTableID) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - parentTableID" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(pCode) Or IsNull(pCode) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - paymentCode" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(pDescr) Or IsNull(pDescr) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - paymentDescription" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(execDate) Or IsNull(execDate) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - paymentExecutionDate" & vbCrLf
  If Not IsNumeric(pAmount) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - paymentAmount" & vbCrLf
  If Not IsNumeric(bpID) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - partnerID" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(crName) Or IsNull(crName) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - creditorName" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(crStreet) Or IsNull(crStreet) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - creditorStreet" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(crPlace) Or IsNull(crPlace) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - creditorPlace" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(crBIC) Or IsNull(crBIC) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - creditorBIC" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(crIBAN) Or IsNull(crIBAN) Then errMsg = errMsg & " - creditorIBAN" & vbCrLf
  If IsMissing(crRef) Or IsNull(crRef) Then crRef = "SI00"
  If IsMissing(crRefInf) Or IsNull(crRefInf) Then crRefInf = "1"
  If Not IsNumeric(GLAccountID) Then GLAccountID = 0
  If IsMissing(relationshipName) Or IsNull(relationshipName) Then relationshipName = ""
  If IsMissing(docID) Or IsNull(docID) Then docID = 0
  If IsMissing(permanent) Or IsNull(permanent) Then permanent = 0
  'Debug.Print Len(errMsg)
  
  If Len(errMsg) = 33 Then
    NewPOID = CreatePO(parentID, parentTableID, pCode, pDescr, execDate, pAmount, bpID, crName, crStreet, crPlace, crBIC, crIBAN, crRef, crRefInf, GLAccountID, relationshipName, docID, permanent)
  Else
    Beep
    MsgBox errMsg & vbCrLf & "Program bo prekinjen.", vbExclamation
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function CreatePO(parentID, parentTableID, pCode, pDescr, execDate, pAmount, bpID, crName, crStreet, crPlace, crBIC, crIBAN, Optional crRef, Optional crRefInf, Optional GLAccountID, Optional relationshipName, Optional docID, Optional permanent) As Long
Dim db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, poID As Long

  poID = 0
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders", dbOpenDynaset, dbSeeChanges)

    rs.AddNew
    rs("companyID") = currentCompanyID
    rs("parentID") = parentID
    rs("paymentCode") = pCode
    rs("parentTableID") = parentTableID
    rs("paymentDescription") = pDescr
    If weekDay(execDate, vbMonday) = 6 Then
      If CLng(execDate) <= CLng(Date) Then execDate = Date + 2 Else execDate = Date - 1
    End If
    If weekDay(execDate, vbMonday) = 7 Then
      If CLng(execDate) <= CLng(Date) Then execDate = Date + 1 Else execDate = Date - 2
    End If
    rs("paymentExecutionDate") = CDate(execDate)
'    rs("currency") = "EUR"
    rs("paymentAmount") = Round(pAmount, 2)
    rs("debtorRef") = "SI00"
    rs("PartnerID") = bpID
    rs("creditorName") = Left(crName, 70)
    rs("creditorStreet") = crStreet
    rs("creditorPlace") = crPlace
    rs("creditorBIC") = UCase(crBIC)
    rs("creditorIBAN") = Replace(crIBAN, " ", "")
    rs("creditorRef") = crRef
    rs("creditorRefInformation") = crRefInf
    rs("GLAccountID") = GLAccountID
    rs("relationshipName") = relationshipName
    rs("documentID") = docID
    rs("isPermanentOrder") = permanent
    rs.Update
    
    rs.Bookmark = rs.LastModified
    poID = rs!paymentOrderID
    
    CreatePO = poID
    
'    rs.FindFirst "paymentOrderID=" & poid
    rs.Edit
      rs("debtorRefInformation") = PaymentReference(poID)
    rs.Update
  
    rs.Close
    
End Function

Public Function PaymentReference(poID As Long) As String
Dim i As Integer, ref As String
  
  ref = CStr(poID)
  If Len(ref) < 13 Then
    PaymentReference = ref
  Else
    PaymentReference = Left(ref, 12) & "-" & Mid(ref, 13, 8)
  End If

End Function

Function CreateSEPAXML() As Boolean
On Error GoTo err
Dim cmpyAccountName As String, strPath As String, f As Form, db As DAO.Database, strQuote As String, strCriteria As String, strSQL1 As String, strSQL2 As String, str1 As String, str2 As String, str3 As String, j As Integer, i As Integer, rs As DAO.Recordset, fileName As String, qDef As QueryDef
Dim ref As String

  CreateSEPAXML = True
  Set f = Forms!frmAccPmtPO_PaymentOrders.Form
  Set db = CurrentDb()
    
  strQuote = chr$(34)
  
  
  strSQL1 = "SELECT tblCmpy_RegistrationData.*, tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.*, PaymentReference(paymentOrderID) AS PmtInfId, Left([creditorIBAN],2) AS country " & _
            "FROM tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders INNER JOIN tblCmpy_RegistrationData ON tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.companyID = tblCmpy_RegistrationData.companyID " & _
            "WHERE "
            
 str1 = "SI"
 If f!chkSeparateFileForeignPO = True Then
    strSQL2 = "(Left([creditorIBAN],2)=" & strQuote & str1 & strQuote & ") AND (tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.companyID=CurrentCompanyID()) AND " & _
              "(tblCmpy_RegistrationData.validTo Is Null) AND (toBePayed=1);"
 Else
    strSQL2 = "(tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.companyID=CurrentCompanyID()) AND (tblCmpy_RegistrationData.validTo Is Null) AND (toBePayed=1);"
 End If
  
 cmpyAccountName = DLookup("cmpyName", "tblCmpy_Companies", "(companyID) = " & currentCompanyID) & "_" & f!BankAccountToBePaidFrom
 strPath = "c:\Companies\Applications\XMLFiles\PaymentOrders\" & cmpyAccountName & "_"
 
 For j = 0 To 1

   If f!chkSeparateFileForeignPO = True Then
     If j = 0 Then
        fileName = strPath & "DomaciPN.xml"
        Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL1 & strSQL2)
     Else
        fileName = strPath & "TujiPN.xml"
        Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL1 & "Not " & strSQL2)
     End If
   Else
       Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL1 & strSQL2)
       fileName = strPath & "VsiPN.xml"
       j = 1
   End If
      
      If rs.BOF And rs.EOF Then GoTo next_j:

      str1 = strQuote & "urn:iso:std:iso:20022:tech:xsd:pain.001.001.03" & strQuote & " xmlns:xsi="
      str2 = strQuote & "http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance" & strQuote & " xsi:schemaLocation="
      str3 = strQuote & "urn:iso:std:iso:20022:tech:xsd:pain.001.001.03 ./pain.001.001.03.xsd" & strQuote
      
      rs.MoveLast
      i = rs.RecordCount
      rs.MoveFirst
      Open fileName For Output As #1
      Print #1, "<?xml version='1.0' encoding='utf-8'?>"
      Print #1, "<Document xmlns=" & str1 & str2 & str3 & ">"
      Print #1, "  " & "<CstmrCdtTrfInitn>"
      Print #1, "    " & "<GrpHdr>"
      Print #1, "      " & HTMLLine("MsgId", Format(Now(), "yymmddhhnnss") & "_" & j)
      Print #1, "      " & HTMLLine("CreDtTm", XMLDate(Now) & "T" & Format(Now, "hh:nn:ss"))
      Print #1, "      " & HTMLLine("NbOfTxs", i)
      Print #1, "      " & "<InitgPty>"
      Print #1, "        " & HTMLLine("Nm", rs("cmpyShortName1"))
      Print #1, "        " & "<Id>"
      Print #1, "          " & "<OrgId>"
      Print #1, "            " & "<Othr>"
      Print #1, "              " & HTMLLine("Id", Right(rs("cmpyTaxNumber"), 8))
      Print #1, "              " & "<SchmeNm>"
      Print #1, "                " & HTMLLine("Cd", "TXID")
      Print #1, "              " & "</SchmeNm>"
      Print #1, "            " & "</Othr>"
      Print #1, "          " & "</OrgId>"
      Print #1, "        " & "</Id>"
      Print #1, "      " & "</InitgPty>"
      Print #1, "    " & "</GrpHdr>"
      
      i = 0
      Do Until rs.EOF
        i = i + 1
        Print #1, "   " & "<PmtInf>"
        ref = rs("PmtInfId")
        Print #1, "     " & HTMLLine("PmtInfId", ref) '*****
        Print #1, "     " & HTMLLine("PmtMtd", "TRF")
        Print #1, "     " & "<PmtTpInf>"
        Print #1, "       " & HTMLLine("InstrPrty", "NORM")
        Print #1, "       " & "<SvcLvl>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Cd", "SEPA")
        Print #1, "       " & "</SvcLvl>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<LclInstrm>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Prtry", "SEPA")
        Print #1, "       " & "</LclInstrm>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<CtgyPurp>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Cd", "SUPP")
        Print #1, "       " & "</CtgyPurp>"
        Print #1, "     " & "</PmtTpInf>"
        Print #1, "     " & HTMLLine("ReqdExctnDt", XMLDate(rs("paymentExecutionDate")))
        Print #1, "     " & "<Dbtr>"
        Print #1, "       " & HTMLLine("Nm", rs("cmpyShortName1"))
        Print #1, "       " & "<PstlAdr>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Ctry", Left(f!BankAccountToBePaidFrom, 2))
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("AdrLine", rs("cmpyStreet"))
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("AdrLine", rs("cmpyPlace"))
        Print #1, "       " & "</PstlAdr>"
        Print #1, "     " & "</Dbtr>"
        Print #1, "     " & "<DbtrAcct>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<Id>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("IBAN", f!BankAccountToBePaidFrom.Column(0))
        Print #1, "       " & "</Id>"
        Print #1, "     " & "</DbtrAcct>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<DbtrAgt>"
        Print #1, "         " & "<FinInstnId>"
        Print #1, "           " & HTMLLine("BIC", f!BankAccountToBePaidFrom.Column(2))
        Print #1, "         " & "</FinInstnId>"
        Print #1, "       " & "</DbtrAgt>"
        Print #1, "     " & "<CdtTrfTxInf>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<PmtId>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("InstrId", ref) '*****
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("EndToEndId", rs("debtorRef") & rs("debtorRefInformation"))  '*****rs("debtorRef") &
        Print #1, "       " & "</PmtId>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<Amt>"
        Print #1, "         " & "<InstdAmt Ccy='" & rs("currency") & "'>" & XMLNumber(rs("paymentAmount")) & "</InstdAmt>"
        Print #1, "       " & "</Amt>"
        Print #1, "       " & HTMLLine("ChrgBr", "SLEV")
        Print #1, "       " & "<UltmtDbtr>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Nm", rs("cmpyShortName1"))
        Print #1, "       " & "</UltmtDbtr>"
        Print #1, "         " & "<CdtrAgt>"
        Print #1, "           " & "<FinInstnId>"
        Print #1, "             " & HTMLLine("BIC", UCase(rs("creditorBIC")))
        Print #1, "           " & "</FinInstnId>"
        Print #1, "         " & "</CdtrAgt>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<Cdtr>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Nm", rs("creditorName"))
        Print #1, "         " & "<PstlAdr>"
        Print #1, "           " & HTMLLine("Ctry", Left(rs("creditorIBAN"), 2))
        Print #1, "           " & HTMLLine("AdrLine", rs("creditorStreet"))
        Print #1, "           " & HTMLLine("AdrLine", rs("creditorPlace"))
        Print #1, "         " & "</PstlAdr>"
        Print #1, "       " & "</Cdtr>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<CdtrAcct>"
        Print #1, "         " & "<Id>"
        Print #1, "           " & HTMLLine("IBAN", Replace(rs("creditorIBAN"), " ", ""))
        Print #1, "         " & "</Id>"
        Print #1, "       " & "</CdtrAcct>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<UltmtCdtr>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Nm", rs("creditorName"))
        Print #1, "       " & "</UltmtCdtr>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<Purp>"
        Print #1, "         " & HTMLLine("Cd", rs("paymentCode"))
        Print #1, "       " & "</Purp>"
        Print #1, "       " & "<RmtInf>"
        Print #1, "         " & "<Strd>"
        Print #1, "           " & "<CdtrRefInf>"
        Print #1, "             " & "<Tp>"
        Print #1, "               " & "<CdOrPrtry>"
        Print #1, "                 " & HTMLLine("Cd", "SCOR")
        Print #1, "               " & "</CdOrPrtry>"
        Print #1, "             " & "</Tp>"
        Print #1, "             " & HTMLLine("Ref", rs("creditorRef") & rs("creditorRefInformation"))
        Print #1, "           " & "</CdtrRefInf>"
        Print #1, "           " & HTMLLine("AddtlRmtInf", rs("paymentDescription"))
        Print #1, "         " & "</Strd>"
        Print #1, "       " & "</RmtInf>"
        Print #1, "     " & "</CdtTrfTxInf>"
        Print #1, "   " & "</PmtInf>"
        rs.MoveNext
      Loop
      Print #1, "  " & "</CstmrCdtTrfInitn>"
      Print #1, "</Document>"
      Close #1
next_j:
  Next j
  
  Beep
  MsgBox ("Pla ilni nalogi so bili uspe no izvo eni v datoteko: " & strPath & "...")
Exit Function

err:
  MsgBox ("Napaka pri kreiranju datoteke!! (" & Error & ")")
  Close #1
  CreateSEPAXML = False

End Function

Public Function ImportBankSepaXml(strLongFileName As String, strBankStatementFileID As String)
On Error Resume Next
Dim resp As Integer, strSQL As String, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, XMLDoc As New DOMDocument, strWhere As String
Dim strBankStatementId As String, strBankStatementNo As String, paymentJournalNameID As Long, strDate As String, BankStatementDate As Date
Dim cmpyID As Long, strIBAN As String, strCcy As String, dblExchangeRate As Double
Dim dblEndBalance As Double, paymentJournalID As Long, i As Integer
Dim dblAmount As Double, sumCrdtAmount As Double, sumDbitAmount As Double, CdtDbt As String
Dim nd As IXMLDOMElement, nd1 As IXMLDOMElement, nd2 As IXMLDOMElement, nd3 As IXMLDOMElement, nd4 As IXMLDOMElement, nd5 As IXMLDOMElement, nd6 As IXMLDOMElement, nd7 As IXMLDOMElement
  
  strSQL = "DELETE * FROM tblTempAccPmt_PaymentTransactions;"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  strCcy = "EUR"
  
  XMLDoc.Load strLongFileName
  For Each nd In XMLDoc.selectNodes("//Stmt")
    For Each nd1 In nd.childNodes
      Select Case nd1.nodeName
        Case "Id": strBankStatementId = nd.selectSingleNode("Id").Text
        Case "LglSeqNb": strBankStatementNo = nd.selectSingleNode("LglSeqNb").Text
        Case "Acct"
          For Each nd2 In nd1.childNodes
            If nd2.nodeName = "Id" Then
              strIBAN = nd2.selectSingleNode("IBAN").Text
              cmpyID = DLookup("companyID", "tblAccPmt_MeansOfPayments", "IBAN = '" & nd2.selectSingleNode("IBAN").Text & "'")
            End If
            If nd2.nodeName = "Ccy" Then
              strCcy = nd1.selectSingleNode("Ccy").Text
            End If
            paymentJournalNameID = DLookup("paymentJournalNameID", "qryAccPmtIBANCcy", "IBANCcy = '" & strIBAN & strCcy & "'")
            dblExchangeRate = DLookup("exchangeRate", "tblAccPmt_PaymentJournalNames", "paymentJournalNameID=" & paymentJournalNameID)
          Next nd2
        Case "Bal"
          For Each nd2 In nd1.childNodes
            Select Case nd2.nodeName
              Case "Amt": dblEndBalance = Replace(nd1.selectSingleNode("Amt").Text, ".", ",")
              Case "Dt":
                strDate = nd1.selectSingleNode("Dt").Text
                BankStatementDate = CDate(Right(strDate, 2) & "/" & Mid(strDate, 6, 2) & "/" & Left(strDate, 4))
            End Select
          Next nd2
        Case "TxsSummry"
          For Each nd2 In nd1.childNodes
            If nd2.nodeName = "TtlCdtNtries" Then
              For Each nd3 In nd2.childNodes
                If nd3.nodeName = "Sum" Then sumCrdtAmount = Replace(nd2.selectSingleNode("Sum").Text, ".", ",")
              Next nd3
            End If
            If nd2.nodeName = "TtlDbtNtries" Then
              For Each nd3 In nd2.childNodes
                If nd3.nodeName = "Sum" Then sumDbitAmount = Replace(nd2.selectSingleNode("Sum").Text, ".", ",")
              Next nd3
            End If
          Next nd2
          
        strWhere = "companyID=" & cmpyID
        strWhere = strWhere & " And (fiscalYear=" & Year(BankStatementDate)
        strWhere = strWhere & ") And (bankStatementID='" & strBankStatementId & "')"
        strWhere = strWhere & " And (paymentJournalNameId=" & paymentJournalNameID & ")"
        
        If Not IsNull(DFirst("paymentJournalNameID", "qryAccPmt_ImportedPaymentJournals", strWhere)) Then
          Beep
          resp = MsgBox("Ban ni izpisek za dan " & BankStatementDate & " je  e uvo en! Naj nadaljujem z uvozom ostalih izpiskov?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
          If resp = vbYes Then
            GoTo nextStatement
          Else
            GoTo addPaymentJournals
          End If
        End If
        
        Set db = CurrentDb()
        Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblAccPmt_PaymentJournals", dbOpenDynaset, dbSeeChanges)
        rs.AddNew
        rs("paymentJournalNumber") = Right(Year(BankStatementDate), 2) & "-" & Format(strBankStatementNo, "000")
        rs("paymentJournalDate") = BankStatementDate
        rs("paymentJournalNameID") = paymentJournalNameID
        rs("bankStatementID") = strBankStatementId
        rs("sumCreditAmount") = sumCrdtAmount
        rs("sumDebitAmount") = sumDbitAmount
        rs("endBalance") = dblEndBalance
        rs("fiscalYear") = Year(BankStatementDate)
        rs("companyID") = cmpyID
        rs("exchangeRate") = dblExchangeRate
        rs.Update
        rs.Bookmark = rs.LastModified
        paymentJournalID = rs("paymentJournalID")
        rs.Close
          
        i = 0
        Case "Ntry"
          Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblTempAccPmt_PaymentTransactions")
          For Each nd2 In nd1.childNodes
            Select Case nd2.nodeName
              Case "Amt": dblAmount = Replace(nd1.selectSingleNode("Amt").Text, ".", ",")
              Case "CdtDbtInd": If nd1.selectSingleNode("CdtDbtInd").Text = "DBIT" Then dblAmount = -dblAmount
              Case "NtryDtls"
                For Each nd3 In nd2.childNodes
                  i = i + 1
                  rs.AddNew
                  rs("sequence") = i
                  rs("paymentJournalID") = paymentJournalID
                  rs("ptAmount") = Round(dblAmount / dblExchangeRate, 2)
                  rs("ptAmountInCurrency") = dblAmount

                  Select Case nd3.nodeName
                    Case "TxDtls"
                      For Each nd4 In nd3.childNodes
                        Select Case nd4.nodeName
                          
                          Case "Refs"
                            If dblAmount < 0 Then
                              If Left(nd4.selectSingleNode("EndToEndId").Text, 2) = "SI" And IsNumeric(Mid(nd4.selectSingleNode("EndToEndId").Text, 3, 2)) Then
                                rs("ourRefInformation") = Mid(nd4.selectSingleNode("EndToEndId").Text, 5)
                              Else
                                rs("ourRefInformation") = nd4.selectSingleNode("EndToEndId").Text
                              End If
                            End If
                          
                          Case "RltdPties"
                            For Each nd5 In nd4.childNodes
                              If dblAmount > 0 Then
                                Select Case nd5.nodeName
                                  Case "Dbtr"
                                    rs("bpName") = nd5.selectSingleNode("Nm").Text
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "Pty" '06.04.2024
                                          If IsNull(rs("bpName")) Then rs("bpName") = nd6.selectSingleNode("Nm").Text
                                        Case "PstlAdr"
                                          rs("bpStreet") = nd6.childNodes(1).Text
                                          If Not nd6.childNodes(2) Is Nothing Then rs("bpPlace") = nd6.childNodes(2).Text
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                  Case "DbtrAcct"
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "Id"
                                          For Each nd7 In nd6.childNodes
                                            If nd7.nodeName = "IBAN" Then rs("bpIBAN") = nd6.selectSingleNode("IBAN").Text
                                          Next nd7
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                End Select
                              End If
                              If dblAmount < 0 Then
                                Select Case nd5.nodeName
                                  Case "Cdtr"
                                    rs("bpName") = nd5.selectSingleNode("Nm").Text
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "Pty" '06.04.2024
                                          If IsNull(rs("bpName")) Then rs("bpName") = nd6.selectSingleNode("Nm").Text
                                        Case "PstlAdr"
                                          rs("bpStreet") = nd6.childNodes(1).Text
                                          If Not nd6.childNodes(2) Is Nothing Then rs("bpPlace") = nd6.childNodes(2).Text
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                  Case "CdtrAcct"
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "Id"
                                          For Each nd7 In nd6.childNodes
                                            If nd7.nodeName = "IBAN" Then rs("bpIBAN") = nd6.selectSingleNode("IBAN").Text
                                          Next nd7
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                End Select
                              End If
                            Next nd5
                          
                          Case "RltdAgts"
                            If dblAmount > 0 Then
                              For Each nd5 In nd4.childNodes
                                Select Case nd5.nodeName
                                  Case "DbtrAgt"
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "FinInstnId"
                                          For Each nd7 In nd6.childNodes
                                            If nd7.nodeName = "BIC" Then rs("bpBIC") = nd6.selectSingleNode("BIC").Text
                                          Next nd7
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                End Select
                              Next nd5
                            End If
                            If dblAmount < 0 Then
                              For Each nd5 In nd4.childNodes
                                Select Case nd5.nodeName
                                  Case "CdtrAgt"
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "FinInstnId"
                                          For Each nd7 In nd6.childNodes
                                            If nd7.nodeName = "BIC" Then rs("bpBIC") = nd6.selectSingleNode("BIC").Text
                                          Next nd7
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                End Select
                              Next nd5
                            End If
                          
                          Case "RmtInf"
                              For Each nd5 In nd4.childNodes
                                Select Case nd5.nodeName
                                  Case "Strd"
                                    For Each nd6 In nd5.childNodes
                                      Select Case nd6.nodeName
                                        Case "CdtrRefInf"
                                          If dblAmount > 0 Then
                                            For Each nd7 In nd6.childNodes
                                              If nd7.nodeName = "Ref" Then
                                                If Left(nd6.selectSingleNode("Ref").Text, 2) = "SI" And IsNumeric(Mid(nd6.selectSingleNode("Ref").Text, 3, 2)) Then
                                                  rs("ourRefInformation") = Mid(nd6.selectSingleNode("Ref").Text, 5)
                                                Else
                                                  If Left(strIBAN, 2) = "SI" Then rs("ourRefIformation") = nd6.selectSingleNode("Ref").Text
                                                End If
                                              End If
                                            Next nd7
                                          End If
                                        Case "AddtlRmtInf": rs("ptDescription") = nd5.selectSingleNode("AddtlRmtInf").Text
                                      End Select
                                    Next nd6
                                  Case "Ustrd"
                                    rs("ptDescription") = nd4.selectSingleNode("Ustrd").Text
                                End Select
                              Next nd5
                        End Select
                      Next nd4
                  End Select
                rs.Update
              Next nd3
            End Select
          Next nd2
          rs.Close
      End Select
    Next nd1
nextStatement:
  Next nd
  
  rs.Close
  db.Close
  
addPaymentJournals:

  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappAccPmtBankStImport_PaymentOrderSngl")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappAccPmtBankStImport_PaymentOrderUni")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappAccPmtBankStImport_AutoCorrect_1")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappAccPmtBankStImport_AutoCorrect_2")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappAccPmtBankStImport_AutoCorrect_3")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappAccPmtBankStImport_RestTransactions")
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
End Function

Public Sub CopyTransaction(paymentTransactionID As Long, txtRef, ptAmount As Double, ptAmountInCurrency As Double, accAmount As Double, docID As Long, Optional docNumber As String)
Dim db As Database, qDef As QueryDef

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qappAccPmtJournals_CopyTransaction")
  qDef.Parameters("transactionID") = paymentTransactionID
  qDef.Parameters("txtRef") = txtRef
  qDef.Parameters("amount") = ptAmount
  qDef.Parameters("amountCurrency") = ptAmountInCurrency
  qDef.Parameters("accAm") = accAmount
  qDef.Parameters("docID") = docID
  qDef.Parameters("docNumber") = docNumber
  qDef.Execute
  
End Sub

Public Function DeletePaymentOrder(poID As Long) As Boolean
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset, strSQL As String, resp As Long

  DeletePaymentOrder = False
  
  strSQL = "SELECT tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.* FROM tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders WHERE paymentOrderID=" & poID & ";"
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  Beep
  
  If rs.NoMatch Then
    MsgBox ("Napaka. Ne najdem pla ilnega naloga.")
    Exit Function
  End If
  
  If rs("unitedPaymentOrderID") = 0 Or IsNull(rs("unitedPaymentOrderID")) Then
  
    If Not IsNull(rs("exportedAt")) Then
      resp = MsgBox("Pla ilni nalog je  e bil izvo en.  e bo izbrisan lahko nastopijo te ave pri knji enju ban nega izpiska! Ali naj pla ilni nalog vseeno izbri em?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Function
    Else
      resp = MsgBox("Ali naj izbri em izbrani pla ilni nalog?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Function
    End If
    Beep
    strSQL = "DELETE FROM tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders WHERE paymentOrderID=" & poID & ";"
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
    DeletePaymentOrder = True
    
  Else
    resp = MsgBox("Zdru eni pla ilni nalog ni mogo e izbrisati! Izbrisati ga je mo no preko forme Zdru evanje pla ilnih nalogov.", vbExclamation)
  End If

End Function

Public Sub RequeryFrmPaymentOrders(frm As Form)
  
  frm!lstNotExecutedPO.Requery
  frm!lstToBeExecutedPO.Requery
  frm!lstPermanentPO.Requery
  frm!lstExecutedPO.Requery
  frm!lstNotExecutedPOSum = 0
  frm!lstToBeExecutedPOSUM = 0
  frm!poID = Null
  
End Sub

  DoCmd.OpenForm "frmAccPmtPO_PaymentOrder", acNormal, , "paymentOrderID= " & paymentOrderID, acFormEdit
End Sub

Public Sub CopyPaymentOrder(paymentOrderID As Long)
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset, strSQL As String

  strSQL = "SELECT tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.* FROM tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders WHERE (tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders.paymentOrderID=" & paymentOrderID & ");"
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  Call NewPOID(rs("parentID"), rs("parentTableID"), rs("paymentCode"), rs("paymentDescription") & " (c)", rs("paymentExecutionDate"), rs("paymentAmount"), rs("partnerID"), rs("creditorName"), rs("creditorStreet"), rs("creditorPlace"), rs("creditorBIC"), rs("creditorIBAN"), rs("creditorRef"), rs("creditorRefInformation"), rs("GLAccountID"), IIf(IsNull(rs("relationshipName")), "", rs("relationshipName")), rs("documentID"), rs("isPermanentOrder"))
  Beep
  
  Exit Sub
err_exit:
  Resume Next
  Beep
  MsgBox "Virmana ni mogo e ustvariti. Napaka: " & err.description
End Sub

Public Sub fLstPostingSubID_AfterUpdate(frm As Form)
On Error Resume Next
Dim varItm As Variant, frm1Name As String, frm1 As Form

  frm!debitSum = 0
  frm!creditSum = 0
  
  For Each varItm In frm!lstPostingSubID.ItemsSelected
    frm!debitSum = Round(frm!debitSum + n(frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(4, varItm)), 2)
    frm!creditSum = Round(frm!creditSum + n(frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(5, varItm)), 2)
  Next varItm

  If frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(3) = "" Then
    frm!linkLinkName = frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(1)
  Else
    frm!linkLinkName = frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(3)
  End If
  
  If frm!Difference < 0 Then
      frm!balanceGLAccountID = FindGLAccountID("789000")
  Else
      frm!balanceGLAccountID = FindGLAccountID("759000")
  End If

  frm1Name = "frmAccGL_Posting"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frm1Name) Then
    Call OpenPosting(frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(9), frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(0))
  End If
  
End Sub

  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccPR_AutoLink_5"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccPR_AutoLink_6"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdAccPR_AutoLink_7"

End Sub

Public Sub AutoLinkPRbyAmount()
Dim qDef As QueryDef, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset
Dim rs1 As DAO.Recordset

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("qryAccPR_AutoLinkByAmount_Documents", dbOpenDynaset)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Beep: Exit Sub
    
  rs.MoveFirst
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryAccPR_AutoLinkByAmount_Payments")
    qDef.Parameters("accID") = rs("GLAccountID")
    qDef.Parameters("ptrID") = rs("partnerID")
    qDef.Parameters("payAm") = rs("docAmount")
    Set rs1 = qDef.OpenRecordset()
     
    If rs1.RecordCount = 1 Then
      rs1.Edit
      rs1("linkName") = rs("linkName")
      rs1("isLinked") = 1
      rs1.Update
    
      rs.Edit
      rs("isLinked") = 1
      rs.Update

      rs1.Close
    End If
    
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  rs.Close

End Sub
Public Sub CheckLinkData(frm As Form, frm1 As Form)
Dim resp As Integer

  If IsNull(frm!linkLinkName) Or frm!linkLinkName = "" Then
    Beep
    frm!linkLinkName.BackColor = 255
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka naziv za zvezo!", vbInformation)
    frm!linkLinkName.BackColor = 16777215
    End
  End If

  If IsNull(frm1!partnerID) Or frm1!partnerID = 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka partner!", vbInformation)
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm1!GLAccountID) Or frm1!GLAccountID = 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka konto!", vbInformation)
    End
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub CreateBalancePosting(frm As Form, frmsum As Form)
Dim resp As Integer, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, docNumber As String

  Call CheckLinkData(frm, frmsum)
  
  If IsNull(frm!Difference) Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Razlika=0! Uskladitvena temeljnica ni potrebna.", vbInformation)
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!balanceGLAccountID) Then
    Beep
    frm!balanceGLAccountID.BackColor = 255
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka protikonto za uskladitveno temeljnico!", vbInformation)
    frm!balanceGLAccountID.BackColor = 16777215
    Exit Sub
  End If
    
  If IsNull(frm!balanceCostCenterID) Then
    Beep
    frm!balanceCostCenterID.BackColor = 255
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka stro kovno mesto! Ali naj nadaljujem brez njega?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
    frm!balanceCostCenterID.BackColor = 16777215
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  End If
    
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting")
  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblTempAccGL_Posting", dbOpenDynaset)
    
  docNumber = NewDocumentNumber("tblAccGL_Postings", "postingName", "SK", currentFiscalYear(), currentCompanyID(), True)
  rs.AddNew
    rs("postingName") = docNumber
    rs("parentTableID") = 19
    rs("fiscalDate") = Date
    rs("fiscalYear") = currentFiscalYear()
    rs("txtSequence") = 1
    rs("postingDescription") = "uskladitev SK"
    rs("GLAccountID") = frmsum!GLAccountID
    rs("partnerID") = frmsum!partnerID
    rs("fiscalMonth") = Month(Date)
    rs("dueDate") = Date
    If frm!Difference > 0 Then
      rs("debitAmount") = 0
      rs("creditAmount") = Round(frm!Difference, 2)
    Else
      rs("debitAmount") = Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2)
      rs("creditAmount") = 0
    End If
    rs("computerID") = computerID()
  rs.Update
    
  rs.AddNew
    rs("postingName") = docNumber
    rs("parentTableID") = 19
    rs("fiscalDate") = Date
    rs("fiscalYear") = currentFiscalYear()
    rs("txtSequence") = 2
    rs("postingDescription") = "uskladitev SK"
    rs("GLAccountID") = frm!balanceGLAccountID
    rs("costCenterID") = frm!balanceCostCenterID
    rs("fiscalMonth") = Month(Date)
    rs("dueDate") = Date
    If frm!Difference > 0 Then
      rs("debitAmount") = Round(frm!Difference, 2)
      rs("creditAmount") = 0
    Else
      rs("debitAmount") = 0
      rs("creditAmount") = Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2)
    End If
    rs("computerID") = computerID()
  rs.Update
  
  rs.Close
  db.Close

  Call CreatePostingMySQL
  
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, "frmAccPR") Then
    frm!lstPostingSubID.Requery
    frm!debitSum = 0
    frm!creditSum = 0
  End If

  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, "frmAccARAP") Then
    frm!lstDocuments.Requery
    frm!lstPayments.Requery
    frm!documentSum = 0
    frm!paymentSum_0 = 0
  End If

  Beep

End Sub

Public Sub CreateLink(frm As Form)
Dim varItm As Variant, m1 As Integer, strWhere As String
  
  If frm!lstPostingSubID.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Noben zapis ni izbran!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!OptPR = False Then
    m1 = frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(8)
    For Each varItm In frm!lstPostingSubID.ItemsSelected
        If m1 <> frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(8, varItm) Then
          Beep
          MsgBox "Prejeti ra un mora biti knji en v istem mesecu kot prevzem!", vbExclamation
          Exit Sub
        End If
    Next varItm
  End If
  
  If Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2) > Round(frm!MaxDiff, 2) Then
    Beep
    If frm!OptPR = True Then
      MsgBox "Razlika med vsoto knji b v breme in dobro mora biti enaka 0. Za nadaljevanje je potrebno ustvariti uskladitveno temeljnico!", vbExclamation
    Else
      MsgBox "Prejetega ra una ni mogo e povezati s prevzemom/i. Razlika med prejetim ra unom in prevzemom je prevelika (biti mora manj a od 5,00 ).", vbExclamation
    End If
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  Call CheckLinkData(frm, frm!frmAccPR_Sums.Form)
  
  For Each varItm In frm!lstPostingSubID.ItemsSelected
    
    strWhere = "WHERE postingSubID=" & frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(0, varItm)
    Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET linkName='" & frm!linkLinkName & "', isLinked=1 " & strWhere & ";")

  Next varItm
  
  frm!lstPostingSubID.Requery
  Beep
  
End Sub

Public Sub DeleteLink(frm As Form)
Dim strSQL As String, varItm As Variant, strWhere As String
  
  If frm!lstPostingSubID.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Noben zapis ni izbran!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked = 0 "
  For Each varItm In frm!lstPostingSubID.ItemsSelected

    strWhere = " WHERE tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID=" & frm!lstPostingSubID.Column(0, varItm)
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL & strWhere)

  Next varItm

  frm.lstPostingSubID.Requery

  Beep

End Sub

On Error Resume Next
Dim frm As Form

  f!GLAccID = f!GLAccountID

Public Sub ChangeLinkStatus()
Dim strSQL As String, frm As Form, isNotLinked As Integer, isLinked As Integer
  
  Set frm = Forms!frmAccPR.Form
  
    If frm!optIsNotLinked = 0 Then
      If frm!optIsLinked = 0 Then
        isNotLinked = 5
        isLinked = 5
      Else
        isNotLinked = 1
        isLinked = 1
      End If
    Else
      If frm!optIsLinked = 0 Then
        isNotLinked = 0
        isLinked = 0
      Else
        isNotLinked = 0
        isLinked = 1
      End If
    End If
    
 '   If frm!OptPR = True Then
      Call CreateQry(35, frm!frmAccPR_Sums!ptrID, isLinked, , , SQLDate(frm!dueDate))
 '   Else
 '     Call CreateQry(95, isNotLinked, isLinked)
 '   End If
End Sub

Public Sub RefreshPostingSub()
Dim strSQL As String, frm As Form, isNotLinked As Integer, isLinked As Integer
  
  Set frm = Forms!frmAccPR.Form
  
  If frm!OptPR = True Then
    strSQL = " AND (tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID<>-1) AND (tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear>=2019) AND (tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=-1)  "
  Else
    strSQL = " AND (tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID<>-1) AND (tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear>=2020) "
  End If
  
  Select Case frm!optOrderBy
    Case 1: strSQL = strSQL & "ORDER BY tblAccGL_Postings.postingName, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.linkName;"
    Case 2: strSQL = strSQL & "ORDER BY date(tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalDate), date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_Postings.postingName;"
    Case 3: strSQL = strSQL & "ORDER BY tblAccGL_PostingSubs.linkName, ABS(debitAmount-creditAmount);"
    Case 4: strSQL = strSQL & "ORDER BY ABS(debitAmount-creditAmount), tblAccGL_PostingSubs.linkName;"
    Case 5: strSQL = strSQL & "ORDER BY date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_PostingSubs.linkName, tblAccGL_Postings.postingName;"
  End Select
  
  Set frm = frm!frmAccPR_Sums.Form
  
  Call RunSQL("UPDATE tblShrdApp_qrys SET qryOrderBy='" & strSQL & "' WHERE qryID=36;")
  Call CreateQry(36, frm!isLinked, frm!GLAccountID, frm!partnerID)
  
  Forms!frmAccPR.Form!lstPostingSubID.RowSource = "qryAccPR_postingSub"
  
End Sub
