Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Sub ARAP_CreateLink(frm As Form, diff As Double, PR As Boolean)
Dim varItm As Variant, varItm1 As Variant, n As Integer, m1 As Integer, strWhere As String, frm1 As Form, resp As Integer
  
  If frm!docType <> 1 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Tip dokumenta mora biti dokument in ne preddokument!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
    
  If frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected.Count > 1 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi natan no en dokument!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi vsaj eno pla ilo!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If

  If Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2) > diff Then
    Beep
    If PR Then
      MsgBox "Razlika med dokumenti in pla ili mora biti enaka 0. Za nadaljevanje je potrebno ustvariti uskladitveno temeljnico!", vbExclamation
    Else
      MsgBox "Razlika med dokumenti in pla ili mora biti manj a od " & Format(diff, STANDARD) & " . Za nadaljevanje je potrebno ustvariti uskladitveno temeljnico!", vbExclamation
    End If
    Exit Sub
  End If
    
  If PR = False Then
    For Each varItm In frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected
      m1 = frm!lstDocuments.Column(4)
      For Each varItm1 In frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected
        If frm!lstPayments.Column(5) <> m1 Then
          MsgBox "Prevzem in ra un morata biti v istem mesecu!", vbExclamation
          Exit Sub
        End If
      Next varItm1
    Next varItm
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!linkID) Or frm!linkID = "" Or frm!linkID = 0 Then
    Beep
    frm!linkID.BackColor = 255
    MsgBox "Manjka naziv ID za zvezo!", vbInformation
    frm!linkID.BackColor = 16777215
    End
  End If
  
  Set frm1 = frm!frmAccARAP_Sums.Form
  
  If IsNull(frm1!partnerID) Or frm1!partnerID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Manjka poslovni partner!", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  If frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    resp = MsgBox("Noben dokument ni izbran. Nadaljujem?", vbYesNo + vbInformation)
    If resp <> vbYes Then End
  Else
    If IsNull(frm!lstDocuments.Column(9)) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Napaka! Ponovno izberi dokument.", vbInformation
      End
    End If
    strWhere = "WHERE postingSubID=" & frm!lstDocuments.Column(9)
    Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=" & frm!linkID & ", tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked=1 " & strWhere & ";")
  End If
  
  For Each varItm In frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected
    
    strWhere = "WHERE postingSubID=" & frm!lstPayments.Column(0, varItm)
    Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=" & frm!linkID & ", tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked=1 " & strWhere & ";")
    
  Next varItm
  
  Beep
  
End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_DeleteLink(frm As Form)
Dim strSQL As String
  
  If frm!docType <> 1 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Tip dokumenta mora biti dokument in ne preddokument!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
      
  If IsNull(frm!linkID) Or frm!linkID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Noben zapis ni izbran! (linkID mora imeti neko vrednost)", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked = 0 WHERE tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=" & frm!linkID
  Call RunMySQL(strSQL)

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_AutoLink(frm As Form)
Dim cmpyID As Long
  
    If frm!OptPR = True Then
      cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
      Call CreateQry1(201, cmpyID) ' poslovni partnerji razen partnerji ozna eni z all companies
      Call RunMySQL
    Else
      Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTemp WHERE computerID=" & computerID())
      DoCmd.SetWarnings False
      DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccARAP_AutoLink_1_prv"
      DoCmd.SetWarnings True
      If DCount("id", "tblTemp") = 0 Then Exit Sub
      Call CreateQry1(220, True)
    End If
 
  frm!linkID = Null
  frm.lstDocuments.Requery
  frm.lstPayments.Requery
  frm!documentSum = 0
  frm!paymentSum = 0
  Beep
  
End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_AttachPayment(frm As Form)
Dim varItm As Variant, m1 As Integer, strWhere As String, frm1 As Form, rowIndex As Integer

  If frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Nobeno pla ilo ni izbrano. Program bo prekinjen.", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!linkID) Or frm!linkID = "" Or frm!linkID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi dokument. Program bo prekinjen.", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  Set frm1 = frm!frmAccARAP_Sums.Form
    
  For Each varItm In frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected
    
    strWhere = " WHERE postingSubID=" & frm!lstPayments.Column(0, varItm)
    Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=" & frm!linkID & strWhere & ";")

  Next varItm
  
'  rowIndex = frm!lstDocuments.ListIndex
  frm!lstDocuments.Requery
'  frm!lstDocuments.Selected(rowIndex + 1) = True
  frm!lstPayments.Requery
  Beep

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_DeleteLinkErrors(PR As Boolean)
Dim companyID As Long, compID As Long

  companyID = currentCompanyID()
  
  If PR = True Then
    Call CreateQry1(218, companyID)
    Call RunMySQL
    Call CreateQry1(320)
    Call RunMySQL
  Else
    compID = computerID()
    Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTemp WHERE computerID=" & compID)
    Call CreateQry1(221, companyID, compID)
    RunMySQL
    If DCount("id", "tblTemp") = 0 Then Exit Sub
    Call CreateQry1(219, compID)
    Call RunMySQL
    Call CreateQry1(220, companyID)
    Call RunMySQL
  End If

End Sub
Public Sub ARAP_CheckPostingData(frm As Form, frm1 As Form)
Dim resp As Integer

  If YearIsOpened(frm!balanceYear) = 0 Then
    MsgBox "Leto ni odprto oz. ne obstaja!!"
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!balanceMonth) Or (frm!balanceMonth < 1) Or (frm!balanceMonth > 12) Then
    MsgBox "Mesec mora biti med 1 in 12!"
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm1!partnerID) Or frm1!partnerID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Manjka partner!", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm1!GLAccountID) Or frm1!GLAccountID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Manjka konto!", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!Difference) Or frm!Difference = "0" Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Razlika=0! Uskladitvena temeljnica ni potrebna.", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!balanceGLAccountID) Then
    Beep
    frm!balanceGLAccountID.BackColor = 255
    MsgBox "Manjka protikonto.", vbInformation
    frm!balanceGLAccountID.BackColor = 16777215
    End
  End If
  
  If frm!balanceGLAccountID.Column(2) = 1 And IsNull(frm!balancePartnerID) Then
    Beep
    frm!balancePartnerID.BackColor = 255
    MsgBox "Manjka nasprotni partner.", vbInformation
    frm!balancePartnerID.BackColor = 16777215
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!balanceDescriptionDebit) Then
    Beep
    frm!balanceDescriptionDebit.BackColor = 255
    MsgBox "Manjka opis breme.", vbInformation
    frm!balanceDescriptionDebit.BackColor = 16777215
    End
  End If
  
   If IsNull(frm!balanceDescriptionCredit) Then
    Beep
    frm!balanceDescriptionCredit.BackColor = 255
    MsgBox "Manjka opis dobro.", vbInformation
    frm!balanceDescriptionCredit.BackColor = 16777215
    End
  End If
  
  If frm!balanceGLAccountID.Column(2) = 1 Then
    frm!balanceCostCenterID = Null
  Else
    If IsNull(frm!balanceCostCenterID) Then
      Beep
      frm!balanceCostCenterID.BackColor = 255
      resp = MsgBox("Manjka stro kovno mesto! Ali naj nadaljujem brez njega?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
      frm!balanceCostCenterID.BackColor = 16777215
      If resp <> vbYes Then End
    End If
  End If
End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CreatePosting(frm As Form, frmsum As Form)
Dim resp As Integer, docID As Long, strWhere As String, postDescription, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, docNumber As String, cmptrID As Long, varItm As Variant
  
      
  If frm!postingType.Column(3) = 222 Then
    If frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
      Beep
      resp = MsgBox("Nobeno pla ilo pla ilo ni izbrano. Ali  eli  resni no ustvariti nov dokument?", vbYesNo + vbInformation)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
      docID = frm!lstDocuments.Column(0)
    Else
      docID = 0
    End If
  Else
    If frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
      Beep
      resp = MsgBox("Noben dokument ni izbran. Ali  eli  resni no ustvariti novo pla ilo?", vbYesNo + vbInformation)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
      docID = 0
    Else
      docID = frm!lstDocuments.Column(0)
    End If

  End If
        
  Call ARAP_CheckPostingData(frm, frmsum)
     
  cmptrID = computerID()
  strWhere = "WHERE computerID=" & cmptrID
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting " & strWhere)
  
  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("SELECT tblTempAccGL_Posting.* FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting " & strWhere, dbOpenDynaset)
    
  docNumber = NewDocumentNumber("tblAccGL_Postings", "postingName", "GP", frm!balanceYear, currentCompanyID(), True)

    rs.AddNew
      rs("postingName") = docNumber
      rs("postingTypeID") = frm!postingType.Column(0)
      rs("parentTableID") = frm!postingType.Column(2)
      rs("fiscalDate") = Date
      rs("fiscalYear") = frm!balanceYear
      rs("txtSequence") = 1
      rs("GLAccountID") = frmsum!GLAccountID
      rs("partnerID") = frmsum!partnerID
      rs("fiscalMonth") = frm!balanceMonth
      rs("dueDate") = frm!balanceDueDate
      If frm!Difference < 0 Then
        rs("postingDescription") = frm!balanceDescriptionDebit
        rs("debitAmount") = Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2)
        rs("creditAmount") = 0
      Else
        rs("postingDescription") = frm!balanceDescriptionCredit
        rs("debitAmount") = 0
        rs("creditAmount") = Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2)
      End If
      rs("documentID") = docID
      rs("computerID") = cmptrID
    rs.Update
    
    rs.AddNew
      rs("postingName") = docNumber
      rs("postingTypeID") = frm!postingType.Column(0)
      rs("parentTableID") = frm!postingType.Column(2)
      rs("fiscalDate") = Date
      rs("fiscalYear") = frm!balanceYear
      rs("txtSequence") = 2
      rs("GLAccountID") = frm!balanceGLAccountID
      rs("partnerID") = frm!balancePartnerID
      rs("costCenterID") = frm!balanceCostCenterID
      rs("fiscalMonth") = frm!balanceMonth
      rs("dueDate") = frm!balanceDueDate
      If frm!Difference > 0 Then
        rs("postingDescription") = frm!balanceDescriptionDebit
        rs("debitAmount") = Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2)
        rs("creditAmount") = 0
      Else
        rs("postingDescription") = frm!balanceDescriptionCredit
        rs("debitAmount") = 0
        rs("creditAmount") = Round(Abs(frm!Difference), 2)
      End If
      rs("documentID") = 0
      rs("computerID") = cmptrID
    rs.Update
  
  rs.Close
  db.Close

  Call CreatePostingMySQL
  
  frm!lstDocuments.Requery
  frm!lstPayments.Requery
  frm!documentSum = 0
  frm!documentPaymentsSum = 0
  frm!documentDiffSum = 0
  frm!paymentSum = 0
  frm!balanceYear = 0

  Beep

End Sub
Public Sub ARAP_SelectRecordFrmSums(frm As Form, frmSUMS As Form)
'On Error Resume Next
  
  frmSUMS!GLAccID = frmSUMS!GLAccountID
  frmSUMS!partID = frmSUMS!partnerID
  frmSUMS!isLnkd = frmSUMS!isLinked
  frmSUMS!lstPartnerCountry.Requery
  frmSUMS!lstPartnerData1.Requery
  frmSUMS!lstPartnerData2.Requery
  
  frm!balancePartnerID = frmSUMS!partnerID
  frm!balanceGLAccountID = Null
  'If frm!OptPR = 2 Then Exit Sub
  Call ARAP_CreateQryDocuments(frm, frmSUMS)
  Call ARAP_CreateQryPreDocuments(frm, frmSUMS)
  Call ARAP_CreateQryPayments(frm, frmSUMS)
  Call ARAP_docType(frm)
  Call ARAP_requery(frm)

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CreateQryDocuments(frmARAP As Form, frmARAPsum As Form)
Dim strSQL As String, ptrID

  
  'query 109 documents
  strSQL = " AND (tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID<>-1) AND (tblShrdDB_Tables.isDocument = 1) AND (CONCAT(tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear,LPAD(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth,2,0)) BETWEEN pmt01 AND pmt02) "
  
  Select Case frmARAP!optOrderBy
    Case 1: strSQL = strSQL & " ORDER BY tblAccGL_Postings.postingName"
    Case 2: strSQL = strSQL & " ORDER BY date(tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalDate), date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_Postings.postingName;"
    Case 3: strSQL = strSQL & " ORDER BY ABS(debitAmount-creditAmount), date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate);"
    Case 4: strSQL = strSQL & " ORDER BY date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_Postings.postingName;"
    Case 5: strSQL = strSQL & " ORDER BY tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID, tblAccGL_Postings.postingName;"
  End Select

  Call RunSQL("UPDATE tblShrdApp_qrys SET qryOrderBy='" & strSQL & "' WHERE qryID=109;")
  If IsNull(frmARAPsum!partnerID) Then
    ptrID = 0
  Else
    ptrID = frmARAPsum!partnerID
  End If
  Call CreateQry(109, frmARAPsum!isLinked, frmARAPsum!GLAccountID, ptrID, , frmARAPsum!Yr1 & Format(frmARAPsum!m1, "00"), frmARAPsum!Yr2 & Format(frmARAPsum!m2, "00"))

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CreateQryPreDocuments(frmARAP, frmARAPsum As Form)
Dim cmpyID As Long, strSQL As String, s As String, ptrID
  
  cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
  
  If IsNull(frmARAPsum!partnerID) Then
    ptrID = 0
  Else
    ptrID = frmARAPsum!partnerID
  End If
  
  If Left(frmARAPsum!glAccountNumber, 1) = "1" Then    'query 160 ggInvoices
    Select Case frmARAP!optOrderBy
        Case 1: strSQL = "ORDER BY IF(qryAccPmt_DocumentPayments.paymentAmount IS NULL,1,2), If(qryAccPmt_DocumentPayments.paymentAmount IS NULL,(dd1),(dd2)), tblTourSales_ggInvoices.ggInvoiceNumber;"
        Case 2: strSQL = "ORDER BY DATE(tblTourSales_ggInvoices.invoiceDate), tblTourSales_ggInvoices.ggInvoiceNumber;"
        Case 3: strSQL = "ORDER BY qryAccARAP_preDocs_ggInvoicesSub.invoiceAmount;"
        Case 4: strSQL = "ORDER BY date(qryAccARAP_preDocs_ggInvoices_paymentTerms.dd2), tblTourSales_ggInvoices.ggInvoiceNumber;"
        Case 5: strSQL = "ORDER BY tblTourSales_ggInvoices.ggInvoiceID;"
      End Select
    Call RunSQL("UPDATE tblShrdApp_qrys SET qryOrderBy='" & strSQL & "' WHERE qryID=160;")
    Call CreateQry1(160, cmpyID, frmARAPsum!Yr1 & Format(frmARAPsum!m1, "00"), frmARAPsum!Yr2 & Format(frmARAPsum!m2, "00"), IIf(IsNull(frmARAPsum!partnerID), 0, frmARAPsum!partnerID))
  Else                                                      '161 purchaseInvoices
    Call CreateQry1(161, frmARAPsum!Yr1 & Format(frmARAPsum!m1, "00"), frmARAPsum!Yr2 & Format(frmARAPsum!m2, "00"), cmpyID, ptrID)
  End If

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CreateQryPayments(frmARAP As Form, frmARAPsum As Form)
Dim pmt04, pmt07

'  pmt07 = " AND ((tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID = 19) OR (tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID = 69)) AND (tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID<>-1) AND (CONCAT(tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear,LPAD(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth,2,0)) BETWEEN pmt02 AND pmt03) "
  
  If IsNull(frmARAP!lstDocuments.Column(0)) Then
    pmt07 = "ORDER BY IF(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=0,-1, 1),"
  Else
    pmt07 = "ORDER BY IF(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=" & frmARAP!lstDocuments.Column(0) & ",-1, IF(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=0, 0, 1)), "
  End If
  
  Select Case frmARAP!optOrderBy
    Case 1: pmt07 = pmt07 & "tblAccGL_Postings.postingName, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID;"
    Case 2: pmt07 = pmt07 & "date(tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalDate), date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_Postings.postingName, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID;"
    Case 3: pmt07 = pmt07 & "ABS(debitAmount-creditAmount), date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID;"
    Case 4: pmt07 = pmt07 & "date(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate), tblAccGL_PostingSubs.linkName, tblAccGL_Postings.postingName, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID;"
    Case 5: pmt07 = pmt07 & "tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID, tblAccGL_Postings.postingName;"
  End Select
  
  If IsNull(frmARAPsum!partnerID) Then
    pmt04 = "(1 = 1)"
  Else
    pmt04 = "(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID=" & frmARAPsum!partnerID & ")"
  End If
  
  Call CreateQry1(110, currentCompanyID(), frmARAPsum!isLinked, frmARAPsum!GLAccountID, pmt04, frmARAPsum!Yr1 & Format(frmARAPsum!m1, "00"), frmARAPsum!Yr2 & Format(frmARAPsum!m2, "00"), pmt07)
End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CopyPayment(frm As Form, frmSUMS As Form)
Dim resp As Integer, rowIndex As Integer, docID As Long, docAm As Double, strAM As String, strWhere As String, strSQL As String, varItm As Variant, payAm As Double

  If IsNull(frm!lstDocuments.Column(0)) Or IsNull(frm!lstPayments.Column(7)) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Napaka. Ponovno izberi dokument in pla ilo.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
  End If

  If IsNull(frmSUMS!partnerID) Or frmSUMS!partnerID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Manjka poslovni partner!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected.Count <> 1 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi natan no ENO pla ilo. Program bo prekinjen?", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi enega ali ve  dokumentov. Program bo prekinjen?", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  
  payAm = n(frm!lstPayments.Column(6))
  
  For Each varItm In frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected                     'preverjanje da imajo vsi dokumenti nasproten predznak od pla ila
    
    If frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1 Then
      docAm = n(frm!lstDocuments.Column(5, varItm))
    Else
      docAm = n(frm!lstDocuments.Column(7, varItm))
    End If
    
    If docAm * payAm > 0 Then
      MsgBox "Pla ila morajo imeti nasprotni predznak od dokumenta.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
    End If

  Next varItm
  
  
  If frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1 Then
    If Abs(frm!documentSum) > Abs(frm!paymentSum) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Pla ilo mora biti ve je od vsote dokumentov.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
    End If
  Else
    If Abs(frm!documentDiffSum) > Abs(frm!paymentSum) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Pla ilo mora biti ve je od vsote dokumentov.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
    End If
  End If
  
  If frm!lstPayments.Column(7) <> 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Pla ilo je zasedeno (docID <>0). Nadaljujem?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  End If
     
  strWhere = " WHERE tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID=" & frm!lstPayments.Column(0)
     
  For Each varItm In frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected                     'dodajanje vrstic med pla ila za vsak izbrani dokument
 
    docID = frm!lstDocuments.Column(0, varItm)
    
    If frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1 Then
      docAm = n(frm!lstDocuments.Column(5, varItm))
    Else
      docAm = n(frm!lstDocuments.Column(7, varItm))
    End If
    
    If docAm > 0 Then

        strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblAccGL_PostingSubs (postingID, sequence, GLAccountID, postingDescription, partnerID, fiscalMonth, dueDate, documentID, creditAmount) " & _
                 "SELECT tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.sequence, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingDescription, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID, " & _
                 "tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate, " & docID & ", " & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & " FROM tblAccGL_PostingSubs " & strWhere
    Else
        docAm = Abs(docAm)
        strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblAccGL_PostingSubs (postingID, sequence, GLAccountID, postingDescription, partnerID, fiscalMonth, dueDate, documentID, debitAmount) " & _
                 "SELECT tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.sequence, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingDescription, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID, " & _
                 "tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate, " & docID & ", " & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & " FROM tblAccGL_PostingSubs " & strWhere
    End If
    
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)

  Next varItm
    
  docAm = frm!Difference                                                  'razlika med pla ilom in vsoto dokumentov
     
  If docAm <> 0 Then                                                      'dodamo razliko na obstoje o vrstico Replace(am2, ",", ".")
    If 0 < docAm Then
      Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.debitAmount=" & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & strWhere)
    Else
      docAm = Abs(docAm)
      Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.creditAmount=" & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & strWhere)
    End If
  Else
      Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblAccGL_PostingSubs" & strWhere)
  End If
    
  frm!lstPayments.Requery
  frm!lstPayments.RowSource = "qryAccARAP_Payments"
  rowIndex = frm!lstDocuments.ListIndex
  frm!lstDocuments.Requery
  frm!lstDocuments.Selected(rowIndex + 1) = True
  Beep

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CopyPaymentN(frm As Form, frmSUMS As Form)
Dim resp As Integer, rowIndex As Integer, docID As Long, docAm As Double, payAm As Double, strWhere As String, strSQL As String, varItm As Variant
  
  If IsNull(frm!lstDocuments.Column(0)) Or IsNull(frm!lstPayments.Column(7)) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Napaka. Ponovno izberi dokument in pla ilo.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
  End If
         
 If IsNull(frmSUMS!partnerID) Or frmSUMS!partnerID = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Manjka poslovni partner!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected.Count = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi vsaj ENO pla ilo. Program bo prekinjen.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!lstDocuments.ItemsSelected.Count <> 1 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izberi natan no EN dokument. Program bo prekinjen.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1 Then
    If Abs(frm!documentSum) > Abs(frm!paymentSum) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Vsota pla il mora biti enaka ali ve ja zneska dokumenta.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
    End If
  Else
    If Abs(frm!documentDiffSum) > Abs(frm!paymentSum) Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Vsota pla il mora biti enaka ali ve ja zneska dokumenta.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
    End If
  End If
  
  If frm!lstPayments.Column(7) <> 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Pla ilo je zasedeno (docID <>0). Nadaljujem?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  End If

  If frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1 Then
    docAm = n(frm!lstDocuments.Column(5, varItm))
  Else
    docAm = n(frm!lstDocuments.Column(7, varItm))
  End If
 
  For Each varItm In frm!lstPayments.ItemsSelected                     'preverjanje da imajo vsi dokumenti nasproten predznak od pla ila
    payAm = n(frm!lstPayments.Column(6, varItm))
  
    If docAm * payAm > 0 Then
      MsgBox "Pla ila morajo imeti nasprotni predznak od dokumenta.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
    End If

  Next varItm

  strWhere = " WHERE tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingSubID=" & frm!lstPayments.Column(0)
     
    docID = frm!lstDocuments.Column(0)
    docAm = frm!Difference
    
    If docAm < 0 Then
        docAm = Abs(docAm)
        strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblAccGL_PostingSubs (postingID, sequence, GLAccountID, postingDescription, partnerID, fiscalMonth, dueDate, documentID, creditAmount) " & _
                 "SELECT tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.sequence, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingDescription, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID, " & _
                 "tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate, 0, " & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & " FROM tblAccGL_PostingSubs " & strWhere
    Else
        strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblAccGL_PostingSubs (postingID, sequence, GLAccountID, postingDescription, partnerID, fiscalMonth, dueDate, documentID, debitAmount) " & _
                 "SELECT tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.sequence, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingDescription, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID, " & _
                 "tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.dueDate, 0, " & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & " FROM tblAccGL_PostingSubs " & strWhere
    End If
    
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
   
  docAm = -frm!Difference + n(frm!lstPayments.Column(6))
     
  If docAm <> 0 Then
    If 0 < docAm Then
      Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.debitAmount=" & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & ", documentID=" & frm!linkID & strWhere)
    Else
      docAm = Abs(docAm)
      Call RunMySQL("UPDATE tblAccGL_PostingSubs SET tblAccGL_PostingSubs.creditAmount=" & Replace(docAm, ",", ".") & ", documentID=" & frm!linkID & strWhere)
    End If
  Else
      Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblAccGL_PostingSubs" & strWhere)
  End If
    
  frm!lstPayments.Requery
  frm!lstPayments.RowSource = "qryAccARAP_Payments"
  rowIndex = frm!lstDocuments.ListIndex
  frm!lstDocuments.Requery
  'frm!lstDocuments.Selected(rowIndex + 1) = True
  Beep

End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_CreatePO(frm As Form)
Dim resp As Integer, rs As DAO.Recordset, paymentDescription As String, frm1 As Form, lastPOID As Long
  
  If Not (-frm!Difference > 0) Then
    resp = MsgBox("Znesek pla ilnega naloga je manj i od ni  oz. enak ni . Nadaljujem?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  End If
  
  paymentDescription = "Vra ilo denarja"
  
  Set frm1 = frm!frmAccARAP_Sums.Form
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("SELECT tblPtr_Partners.* FROM tblPtr_Partners WHERE tblPtr_Partners.partnerID=" & frm1!partnerID)
  lastPOID = NewPOID(0, 0, "REFU", paymentDescription, Date, -frm!Difference, frm1!partnerID, rs("name1") & " " & rs("name2"), rs("street"), rs("place"), rs("BIC"), rs("IBAN"), "SI00", "1", frm1!GLAccountID, "", frm!linkID)
  If lastPOID = 0 Then Exit Sub
  DoCmd.OpenForm "frmaccPmtPO_paymentOrder", acNormal, , "paymentOrderID=" & lastPOID
End Sub


Public Sub ARAP_requery(frm As Form)

  frm!lstDocuments.Requery
  frm!lstPayments.Requery
  frm!lstDocuments = ""
  frm!lstPayments = ""
  frm!linkID = Null
  frm!documentSum = 0
  frm!documentPaymentsSum = 0
  frm!documentDiffSum = 0
  frm!paymentSum = 0
  frm!balanceMonth = Null
  frm!balanceYear = 0
  
End Sub

Public Sub ARAP_docType(frm As Form)
  
  If frm!docType = 1 Then
    frm!lstDocuments.RowSource = "qryAccARAP_Documents"
    frm!lstDocuments.ColumnCount = 10
    frm!lstDocuments.ColumnWidths = "2cm;3cm;2cm;10cm;1cm;2.2cm;2.2cm;2.2cm;2cm;0cm" '"2cm;3cm;2cm;10cm;1cm;2,2cm;2,2cm;2,2cm;2cm;0cm"
    frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1
    'frm!OptLinkByAmount.visible = False
  Else
    frm!lstDocuments.RowSource = "qryAccARAP_preDocuments"
    frm!lstDocuments.ColumnCount = 9
    frm!lstDocuments.ColumnWidths = "2cm;9.8cm;2.2cm;2cm;2cm;2.2cm;2.2cm;2.2cm" '"2cm;9,8cm;2,2cm;2cm;2cm;2,2cm;2,2cm;2,2cm"
    frm!OptLinkByAmount = 1
    frm!OptLinkByAmount.visible = True
  End If
  
End Sub


Public Sub CreateQry_ARAPSums()
Dim db As Database, qDef As QueryDef
Dim strSQL As String, strSQL1 As String, strSQL2 As String, strSQL2_0 As String, strSQL2_1 As String, strSQL2_2 As String, strSQL2_3 As String, strSQL3 As String
Dim cmpyID As Long, isLinked1 As Integer, isLinked2 As Integer, m1 As Integer, m2 As Integer, Yr1 As Integer, Yr2 As Integer

cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
isLinked1 = 0
isLinked2 = 0
m1 = 0
Yr1 = 2000
m2 = 12
Yr2 = 2021


  strSQL1 = "SELECT tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID, tblAccAcc_GLAccounts.glAccountNumber, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID, " & _
            "CONCAT_WS(' ',tblPtr_Partners.Name1,tblPtr_Partners.Name2, tblPtr_Partners.Name3) AS partnerName, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked, " & _
            "tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth, MIN(0) AS preDocAm, MIN(0) AS preDocPayments, MIN(0) AS preDocBalance, " & _
            "round(100*Sum(If(tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID=19,0,debitAmount-creditAmount))) AS rpDocAm, MIN(0) AS rpPayments, MIN(0) AS rpBalance, " & _
            "round(100*Sum(IF(tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID=0 AND tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID=19,debitAmount-creditAmount,0))) AS notAssignedPayments, " & _
            "ROUND(100*Sum(IF(tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID<>19,0,debitAmount-creditAmount))) AS totalPayments " & _
            "FROM ((tblAccGL_Postings INNER JOIN (tblAccAcc_GLAccounts INNER JOIN tblAccGL_PostingSubs ON tblAccAcc_GLAccounts.GLAccountID = tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID) " & _
            "ON tblAccGL_Postings.postingID = tblAccGL_PostingSubs.postingID) LEFT JOIN tblPtr_Partners ON tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID = tblPtr_Partners.partnerID) "
  
  strSQL2_0 = "((tblAccGL_Postings.companyID=" & cmpyID & ") AND ((tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked=" & isLinked1 & ")  OR (tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked=" & isLinked2 & ")) AND " & _
              "(tblAccAcc_GLAccounts.isPRAccount=1) AND (tblAccGL_PostingSubs.documentID<>-1) AND (tblAccGL_Postings.parentTableID<>-1) "
  strSQL2_1 = "AND ((tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth>=" & m1 & ") AND (tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear=" & Yr1 & ")))"
  strSQL2_2 = "AND ((tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear>" & Yr1 & ") AND (tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear<" & Yr2 & ")))"
  strSQL2_3 = "AND ((tblAccGL_PostingSubs.fiscalMonth<=" & m2 & ") AND (tblAccGL_Postings.fiscalYear=" & Yr2 & ")))"
  strSQL2 = "WHERE " & strSQL2_0 & strSQL2_1 & " OR " & strSQL2_0 & strSQL2_2 & " OR " & strSQL2_0 & strSQL2_3
  
  
  strSQL3 = "GROUP BY tblAccGL_PostingSubs.GLAccountID, tblAccAcc_GLAccounts.glAccountNumber, tblAccGL_PostingSubs.partnerID, CONCAT_WS(' ',tblPtr_Partners.Name1,tblPtr_Partners.Name2, tblPtr_Partners.Name3) , tblAccGL_PostingSubs.isLinked;"

  strSQL = strSQL1 & strSQL2 & strSQL3

  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryAccARAP_SumsSub_GL")
  qDef.SQL = strSQL

End Sub

Public Sub OpenFormARAP()
Dim frm As Form, frmSUMS As Form, compID As Long, strSQL As String

  If YearIsOpened(currentFiscalYear()) = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Saldakonte je mo no odpreti samo v letu, ki je odprto.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  compID = currentCompanyID()
  Call ARAP_DeleteLinkErrors(True)
  Call CreateQry(111, 0, 0, compID, , 200001, 205012)
  Call CreateQry(162, , , , , compID, 200001, 205012)
  Call CreateQry(163, , , , , compID, 200001, 205012)
  Call CreateQry(236, , , , , compID, 200001, 205012)
  DoCmd.OpenForm "frmAccARAP"
  Set frm = Forms!frmAccARAP.Form
  Set frmSUMS = frm!frmAccARAP_Sums.Form
  frmSUMS.RecordSource = "qryAccARAP_Sums"
  frmSUMS.Detail.visible = True
  frm!OptPR = True
End Sub

============== START FILE: modAcc_FiscalPeriods.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Function YearIsOpened(Yr As Long) As Integer
Dim strWhere As String

  strWhere = "fiscalYear=" & Yr
  
  
  If IsNull(DLookup("fiscalYear", "qryAccFiscalPeriods_YrIsOpened", strWhere)) Then
    YearIsOpened = 0
  Else
    YearIsOpened = 1
  End If

End Function

Public Function MonthIsOpened(DT As Date, Optional cmpyID As Long) As Integer
Dim strWhere As String

  If cmpyID = 0 Then
    cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
  End If
  
  Call CreateQry(108, cmpyID, Month(DT), Year(DT))
  
  MonthIsOpened = DCount("fiscalMonthID", "qryAccFiscalPeriods_MnthIsOpened")

End Function

Public Function GetFiscalMonthID(d As Date) As Long
Dim m

  m = DLookup("fiscalMonthID", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "(fiscalYear=" & Year(d) & ") And (fiscalMonth=" & Month(d) & ")")
  
  If IsNull(m) Then
    GetFiscalMonthID = 0
  Else
    GetFiscalMonthID = m
  End If
    
End Function

============== END FILE: modAcc_FiscalPeriods.txt ================



============== START FILE: modAcc_VAT.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Sub Create_KIRKPR_XML(frm As Form)
Dim newLine As String
Dim strXML As String
Dim rs_glava As DAO.Recordset
Dim rs_KIR As DAO.Recordset
Dim rs_KPR As DAO.Recordset
Dim KIR_exists As String
Dim KPR_exists As String
Dim i As Integer
Dim strField As String
Dim strFileName As String

DoCmd.Hourglass True
newLine = chr(13) & chr(10)

Set rs_glava = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("SELECT * FROM tblAccVat_DDVO WHERE DDVOID=" & frm!ddvoID)

Set rs_KIR = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("SELECT * FROM tblAccVat_DDVO_KIR WHERE DDVOID=" & frm!ddvoID & " ORDER BY ZAPST")
If rs_KIR.RecordCount = 0 Then
  KIR_exists = "false"
Else
  KIR_exists = "true"
End If

Set rs_KPR = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("SELECT * FROM tblAccVat_DDVO_KPR WHERE DDVOID=" & frm!ddvoID & " ORDER BY ZAPST")
If rs_KPR.RecordCount = 0 Then
  KPR_exists = "false"
Else
  KPR_exists = "true"
End If

'**************************************
strXML = "<?xml version = '1.0' encoding='UTF-8'?>" & newLine

'****************START DV_KIR_KPR*****************
strXML = strXML & "<DDV_KIR_KPR xmlns='http://edavki.durs.si/Documents/Schemas/DDV_KIR_KPR_1.xsd' " & _
                  "xmlns:xsi='http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema-instance'>" & newLine

'****************GLAVA*****************
strXML = strXML & "<Glava>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "<TaxPayerID>" & Mid(companyTaxNumber(frm!companyID), 3) & "</TaxPayerID>" & newLine
'        <TUJEC1>AB</TUJEC1>
'        <TUJEC2>string</TUJEC2>
strXML = strXML & "<OBDOBJE_OD>" & XMLDate(rs_glava("OBDOBJE_OD")) & "</OBDOBJE_OD>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "<OBDOBJE_DO>" & XMLDate(rs_glava("OBDOBJE_DO")) & "</OBDOBJE_DO>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "<KIR>" & KIR_exists & "</KIR>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "<KPR>" & KPR_exists & "</KPR>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "<VRACILO>" & IIf(rs_glava("VRACILO") = 0, "false", "true") & "</VRACILO>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "<ODBDELEZ>false</ODBDELEZ>" & newLine
If Not IsNull(rs_glava("NACIN")) Then strXML = strXML & "<NACIN>" & rs_glava("NACIN") & "</NACIN>" & newLine
'        <INSPOS>false</INSPOS>
'        <PREDLODO>false</PREDLODO>
If Not IsNull(rs_glava("NACIN")) Then strXML = strXML & "<OPOMBA> " & rs_glava("OPOMBA") & "</OPOMBA>" & newLine
strXML = strXML & "</Glava>" & newLine
'****************END GLAVA*****************
  
    '****************START KIR*****************
    If KIR_exists = "true" Then
      rs_KIR.MoveFirst
      strXML = strXML & "<Lista_KIR>" & newLine
      Do Until rs_KIR.EOF
        strXML = strXML & "<KIR>" & newLine
        strXML = strXML & "<ZAPST>" & rs_KIR("ZAPST") & "</ZAPST>" & newLine
        strXML = strXML & "<OBDOBJE>" & rs_KIR("OBDOBJE") & "</OBDOBJE>" & newLine
        For i = 2 To 28
          If i <> 12 And i <> 13 And i <> 27 Then
            strField = "P" & i
            If hasValue(rs_KIR(strField)) Then
                Select Case i
                Case 2, 4
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & XMLDate(rs_KIR(strField)) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                'Case 3, 5, 28
                '  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KIR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine 'UTF8Encode(Replace(rs_KIR(strField), "&", ""))
                Case 6
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KIR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                  strField = "P" & i & "DS"
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KIR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                Case 7 To 27
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & XMLNumber(rs_KIR(strField)) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                Case Else:
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KIR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                End Select
            End If
          End If
        Next i
        strXML = strXML & "<OBRAVNAVA>" & rs_KIR("OBRAVNAVA") & "</OBRAVNAVA>" & newLine
        If Not IsNull(rs_KIR("OBDOBJE88")) Then
          strXML = strXML & "<OBDOBJE88>" & rs_KIR("OBDOBJE88") & "</OBDOBJE88>" & newLine
          strXML = strXML & "<DAVEK88>" & XMLNumber(rs_KIR("DAVEK88")) & "</DAVEK88>" & newLine
        End If
        strXML = strXML & "</KIR>" & newLine
        rs_KIR.MoveNext
      Loop
      strXML = strXML & "</Lista_KIR>" & newLine
    End If
    '****************END KIR*****************
  
  
    '****************START KPR*****************
    If KPR_exists = "true" Then
        rs_KPR.MoveFirst
        strXML = strXML & "<Lista_KPR>" & newLine
        Do Until rs_KPR.EOF
          strXML = strXML & "<KPR>" & newLine
          strXML = strXML & "<ZAPST>" & rs_KPR("ZAPST") & "</ZAPST>" & newLine
          strXML = strXML & "<OBDOBJE>" & rs_KPR("OBDOBJE") & "</OBDOBJE>" & newLine
          For i = 2 To 22
            If i <> 12 And i <> 13 Then
              strField = "P" & i
              If hasValue(rs_KPR(strField)) Then
                Select Case i
                Case 2, 4, 5                                                                                                'date fields
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & XMLDate(rs_KPR(strField)) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                'Case 3, 6, 22                                                                                               'text fields
                '  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & UTF8Encode(Replace(rs_KPR(strField), "&", "")) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                Case 7
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KPR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                  strField = "P" & i & "DS"
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KPR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                Case 8 To 21                                                                                                'number
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & XMLNumber(rs_KPR(strField)) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                Case Else
                  strXML = strXML & "<" & strField & ">" & rs_KPR(strField) & "</" & strField & ">" & newLine
                End Select
              End If
            End If
          Next i
          strXML = strXML & "<OBRAVNAVA>" & rs_KPR("OBRAVNAVA") & "</OBRAVNAVA>" & newLine
          If Not IsNull(rs_KPR("OBDOBJE88")) Then
            strXML = strXML & "<OBDOBJE88>" & rs_KPR("OBDOBJE88") & "</OBDOBJE88>" & newLine
            strXML = strXML & "<DAVEK88>" & XMLNumber(rs_KPR("DAVEK88")) & "</DAVEK88>" & newLine
          End If
          strXML = strXML & "</KPR>" & newLine
          rs_KPR.MoveNext
        Loop
        strXML = strXML & "</Lista_KPR>"
    End If
    '****************END KPR*****************

strXML = strXML & "</DDV_KIR_KPR>"
'******************************************

'CREATE xml File
    
    strFileName = "c:\Companies\Applications\XMLFiles\" & "KIR_KPR_" & Mid(companyTaxNumber(frm!companyID), 3) & ".xml"
    
    Open strFileName For Output As #1
    
    Print #1, strXML
    Close #1
DoCmd.Hourglass False
End Sub

============== END FILE: modAcc_VAT.txt ================



============== START FILE: modBusinessPartner.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Sub CheckBPData(bpID As Long)
'doda podatke v tabelo poslovni partnerji podjetja,  e  e ne obstajajo
Dim cmpyID As Long, strWhere As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, strSQL As String
   
    cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
   
    strWhere = "(partnerID=" & bpID & ") AND (companyID=" & cmpyID & ")"

    If DCount("*", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere) = 0 Then
      Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblPtr_PartnersCmpys")
      rs.AddNew
      rs("partnerID") = bpID
      rs("companyID") = cmpyID
      rs("bpRef") = "SI00"
      rs("meansOfPaymentGroupID") = DFirst("meansOfPaymentGroupID", "tblAccPmt_MeansOfPaymentGroups", "meansOfPaymentGroupType='isBankAccount'")
      rs.Update
      rs.Close
      Set rs = Nothing
    End If
       
End Sub

Public Function CheckBPDataTaxNumber(taxNumber As String) As Boolean
'doda podatke v tabelo poslovni partnerji podjetja,  e  e ne obstajajo
Dim cmpyID As Long, strWhere As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, strSQL As String
   
  CheckBPDataTaxNumber = False
      
  strWhere = "((taxNumber='" & taxNumber & "') OR (taxNumber='SI" & taxNumber & "'))"
  
  If DCount("*", "tblPtr_partners", strWhere) <> 0 Then
    MsgBox "Poslovni partner  e obstaja v bazi. Dodajanje ni mogo e!"
    Exit Function
  End If
    
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappPtr_DURS_addNew1"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappPtr_DURS_addNew2"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  CheckBPDataTaxNumber = True
  
End Function

Public Sub RefreshDefaultValues()
Dim ctl As control
  
  For Each ctl In Forms!frmCmpyMain!frmCmpyDefaultValues.Form.Controls
        If ctl.ControlType = acComboBox Or ctl.ControlType = acListBox Then
          ctl.Requery
          ctl.Requery
        End If
  Next ctl
  Set ctl = Nothing
  
End Sub

============== START FILE: modCR_FVI.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Function verifyInvoice(invID As Long, showAlertMessage As Boolean, isReversal As Boolean, isPostChange As Boolean, archiveDb As Boolean)  'archive=true potrjevanje iz arhiva
'On Error Resume Next
Dim db As Database
Dim qryName As String, qryNameVAT As String, crTableName As String
Dim qdf As DAO.QueryDef, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset
Dim FVIFolder As String
Dim cmpyTaxNumber As String, str1 As String
Dim invDataFile As String, invRespFile As String
Dim strVat As String
Dim s0, s1, s2, S3
Dim wsh As Object, strAlertMessage As String
Dim waitOnReturn As Boolean: waitOnReturn = True
Dim windowStyle As Integer: windowStyle = 1
Dim frm As Form, frmName As String

  If archiveDb Then
    qryName = "qrySalesCRFVI_Invoice"
    qryNameVAT = "qrySalesCRFVI_InvoiceVAT"
    crTableName = "tblSalesCR_Invoices"
  Else
    qryName = "qryCRFVI_Invoice"
    qryNameVAT = "qryCRFVI_InvoiceVAT"
    crTableName = "tblCR_crInvoices"
  End If
    
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    Set qdf = db.QueryDefs(qryName)
    qdf("invID") = invID
    Set rs = qdf.OpenRecordset()
    
    If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Function

    If rs("isWriteOff") = True Then
      GoTo CLOSE_DB                                                   'odpis ne sme biti potrjen
    End If
    
    If IsNull(rs("crInvoiceNumber")) Then
      MsgBox "Manjka  tevilka ra una! Ra una ni mo no dav no potrditi.", vbExclamation
      GoTo CLOSE_DB                                                   'manjka stevilka racuna
    End If
    
    If rs("errorCode") = "E000" And Not isPostChange Then              'ra un je  e bil potrjen, ponovno ga lahko potrdimo samo pri naknadnem spreminjanju
      MsgBox "Ra una je  e bil dav no potrjen!", vbExclamation
      GoTo CLOSE_DB
    End If
    
    If IsNull(rs("employeeTaxNumber")) Then
      MsgBox "Ra una ni mo no dav no potrditi. Manjka dav na  tevilka natakarja!", vbExclamation
      GoTo CLOSE_DB
    End If

    FVIFolder = "c:\Companies\Applications\FVI\"
    
    If IsNumeric(rs("cmpyTaxNumber")) Then
      cmpyTaxNumber = rs("cmpyTaxNumber")
    Else
      cmpyTaxNumber = Right(rs("cmpyTaxNumber"), 8)
    End If
    
    invDataFile = "Invoice" & Mid(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 7) & "_SentData.txt"
    invRespFile = "Invoice" & Mid(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 7) & "_FursResponse.txt"
    Open FVIFolder & invDataFile For Output As #1
    'Header
    str1 = cmpyTaxNumber & "-0000-0000-" & Format(Right(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 4), "0000") & "-"
    
    Print #1, "MessageID=" & str1 & Right((CDbl(Now()) * 10000000000#), 12)                         'R_1.0
    Print #1, "DateTime=" & Format(Now, "yyyy-mm-ddThh:mm:ss")                                      'R_2.0  as po iljanja ra una
    
    'Invoice R_3.0
    Print #1, "TaxNumber=" & cmpyTaxNumber                                                          'R_3.1
    Print #1, "IssueDateTime=" & Format(rs("crInvoiceCreatedAt"), "yyyy-mm-ddThh:nn:ss")            'R_3.2
    Print #1, "NumberingStructure=" & "B"                                                           'R_3.3
    
    'InvoiceIdentifier 'R_3.4
    Print #1, "InvoiceIdentifier=" & Replace(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), "-", "/")

    If Not IsNull(rs("bpTaxNumber")) Then
      Print #1, "CustomerVATNumber=" & rs("bpTaxNumber")                                             'R_3.5
    End If
    
    Print #1, "InvoiceAmount=" & XMLNumber(rs("discountedTotal"))                                    'R_3.6
    Print #1, "PaymentAmount=" & XMLNumber(rs("discountedTotal"))                                    'R_3.8
    
    'TaxesPerSeller R_3.9
    Set qdf = db.QueryDefs(qryNameVAT)
    qdf.Parameters("invID") = invID
    Set rs1 = qdf.OpenRecordset()
    
    If Not rs1.EOF Then
      strVat = ""
      rs1.MoveFirst
      Do Until rs1.EOF
        If strVat = "" Then
            strVat = XMLNumber(100 * rs1("vatRate")) & "/" & XMLNumber(rs1("vatBase")) & "/" & XMLNumber(rs1("vatAmount"))
        Else
            strVat = strVat & "*" & XMLNumber(100 * rs1("vatRate")) & "/" & XMLNumber(rs1("vatBase")) & "/" & XMLNumber(rs1("vatAmount"))
        End If
        rs1.MoveNext
       Loop
      Print #1, "TaxesPerSeller_VAT_0=" & strVat
    End If
    rs1.Close
      
    Print #1, "OperatorTaxNumber=" & rs("employeeTaxNumber")                                        'R_3.10
     
    'naknadno po iljanje ra una
    If rs("sendingCount") > 0 Then
        Print #1, "SubsequentSubmit= True"
        If rs("errorCode") <> "E000" Then Print #1, "SpecialNotes=Napaka pri predhodnem po iljanju: " & rs("errorCode")
    Else
        Print #1, "SubsequentSubmit=False"                                                          'R_3.13
    End If
    
    'storno
    If isReversal Then
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_BusinessPremiseID_0=" & Left(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 2)
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_ElectronicDeviceID_0=" & Mid(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 4, 2)
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_InvoiceNumber_0=" & CLng(Mid(rs("relatedInvoiceNumber"), 7))
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_IssueDateTime_0=" & Format(rs("relatedInvoiceCreatedAt"), "yyyy-mm-ddThh:nn:ss")
        Print #1, "SpecialNotes=" & rs("remark")                                                                            'R_3.20
    End If
    
    'naknadno spreminjanje ra una - mo no je samo dodati/spremeniti kupca
    If isPostChange Then
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_BusinessPremiseID_0=" & Left(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 2)
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_ElectronicDeviceID_0=" & Mid(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 4, 2)
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_InvoiceNumber_0=" & CLng(Mid(rs("crInvoiceNumber"), 7))
        Print #1, "ReferenceInvoice_IssueDateTime_0=" & Format(rs("crInvoiceCreatedAt"), "yyyy-mm-ddThh:nn:ss")
        Print #1, "SpecialNotes=Naknadna sprememba kupca"                                                                    'R_3.20
    End If
    
    Close #1

    'po lje ra un preko blackbox-a
    
    Open FVIFolder & "FursInvoice.bat" For Output As #1
    
    Print #1, """" & FVIFolder & "FiscalSlo.exe" & """" & " " & invDataFile & " " & invRespFile & " " & rs("certificateFileName") & " " & rs("certificatePassword") & " INVOICE 10"
    Close #1
      
    If DLookup("verifyInvoices", "tblCR_CashRegisters", "cashRegisterID=" & rs("cashRegisterID")) Then
      rs.Close
      Set rs = Nothing
      Set wsh = VBA.CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
      wsh.Run FVIFolder & "FursInvoice.bat", windowStyle, waitOnReturn
    Else
      Beep
      MsgBox ("Izbrana blagajna ne potrjuje ra unov!")
      Kill FVIFolder & invDataFile
      rs.Close
      Set rs = Nothing
      db.Close
      Set db = Nothing
      Exit Function
    End If

    On Error GoTo err_exit:
    Open FVIFolder & invRespFile For Input As #2
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("SELECT * FROM " & crTableName & " WHERE crInvoiceID=" & invID & ";")
    
    s0 = ""
    s1 = ""
    s2 = ""
    S3 = ""
    strAlertMessage = ""
    rs.Edit
        Do While Not EOF(2)
          Line Input #2, s0
          s1 = Split(s0, "=")
          s2 = s1(1)
          S3 = s1(0)
            Select Case S3
            Case "Error" 'koda napake
              If s2 = "E000" Then
                rs("errorCode") = s2
              Else
                rs("errorCode") = Left(s2, 4)
                strAlertMessage = "Pri overjanju ra una na FURSu je pri lo do napake: " & rs("errorCode") & " - " & Right(s2, Len(s2) - 6)
              End If
            Case "UniqueInvoiceID" 'UniqueInvoiceID EOR
              rs("EOR") = s2
            Case "ProtectedID" 'ProtectedID ZIO
              rs("ZOI") = s2
              rs("QRCode") = GetCode(rs("ZOI"), cmpyTaxNumber, Format(rs("crInvoiceCreatedAt"), "yymmddyynnss"))
              rs("BarCode1") = Code128$("41" & Mid(rs("QRCode"), 1, 30))
              rs("BarCode2") = Code128$("42" & Mid(rs("QRCode"), 31))
          End Select
        Loop
    
    rs("sendingCount") = rs("sendingCount") + 1
    rs.Update
    Close #2
    rs.Close
    Set rs = Nothing
    db.Close
    Set db = Nothing
    
    If showAlertMessage And strAlertMessage <> "" Then
      Beep
      MsgBox (strAlertMessage)
    Else
      Kill FVIFolder & invDataFile
      Kill FVIFolder & invRespFile
    End If
      
    frmName = "frmCRInvoices"
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
      Set frm = Forms(frmName)
      Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm!frmCRInvoicesSub.Form, "crInvoiceID", invID)
    End If
  
    Exit Function
    
err_exit:
On Error Resume Next
    Close #2
    
    Beep
    MsgBox "Napaka med potrjevanjem ra una na FURSu."
    
CLOSE_DB:

    If hasValue(rs) Then
      rs.Close
      Set rs = Nothing
    End If
    
    db.Close
    Set db = Nothing

End Function

Function CreateMsgID(str As String) As String
Dim i As Integer, i1 As Integer
  If Not IsNull(str) Then
    i = 1
    i1 = 36 - Len(str)
    If i1 <= 0 Then
      CreateMsgID = Left(str, 36)
    Else
      CreateMsgID = str
      Do Until i > i1
        CreateMsgID = 0 & CreateMsgID
        i = i + 1
      Loop
    End If
  Else
    CreateMsgID = ""
  End If
End Function

Function Dodaj_nicle_nizu_spredaj(A As String, stevilo_nicel As Integer) As String
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim niz As String
    
    i = 1
    niz = A
    
    Do Until i > stevilo_nicel
        niz = "0" & niz
        i = i + 1
    Loop
    
    Dodaj_nicle_nizu_spredaj = niz
 
End Function
 
Function Dodaj_nicle_nizu_zadaj(A As String, stevilo_nicel As Integer) As String
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim niz As String
    
    i = 1
    niz = A
    
    Do Until i > stevilo_nicel
        niz = niz & "0"
        i = i + 1
    Loop
    
    Dodaj_nicle_nizu_zadaj = niz
 
End Function
 
Function Vsota_nizov(A As String, b As String) As String
    Dim a_niz As String
    Dim b_niz As String
    Dim Vsota As String
    Dim Prenos, i As Integer
        
    a_niz = A
    b_niz = b
    'najprej izena im dol ini stringov tako, da kraj emu spredaj dodam ni le
    If Len(a_niz) < Len(b_niz) Then a_niz = Dodaj_nicle_nizu_spredaj(a_niz, Len(b_niz) - Len(a_niz))
    If Len(b_niz) < Len(a_niz) Then b_niz = Dodaj_nicle_nizu_spredaj(b_niz, Len(a_niz) - Len(b_niz))
    
    Prenos = 0
    i = Len(a_niz)
    Vsota = ""
    
    Do Until i < 1
        Vsota = (Val(Mid(a_niz, i, 1)) + Val(Mid(b_niz, i, 1)) + Prenos) Mod 10 & Vsota
        If (Val(Mid(a_niz, i, 1)) + Val(Mid(b_niz, i, 1)) + Prenos) > (Val(Mid(a_niz, i, 1)) + Val(Mid(b_niz, i, 1)) + Prenos) Mod 10 Then Prenos = 1 Else Prenos = 0
        i = i - 1
    Loop
    
    If Prenos > 0 Then Vsota_nizov = Prenos & Vsota Else Vsota_nizov = Vsota
    
End Function

Function Produkt_nizov(A As String, b As String) As String
    Dim a_niz As String
    Dim b_niz As String
    Dim vmesna_vsota As String
    Dim produkt As String
    Dim Prenos, i, j, dolzina_a, dolzina_b As Integer
    
    a_niz = A
    b_niz = b
    dolzina_a = Len(A)
    dolzina_b = Len(b)
    
    produkt = "0"
    j = 1
    
    Do Until j > dolzina_b
        i = dolzina_a
        vmesna_vsota = ""
        Prenos = 0
        Do Until i < 1
            vmesna_vsota = (Val(Mid(a_niz, i, 1)) * Val(Mid(b_niz, j, 1)) + Prenos) Mod 10 & vmesna_vsota
            Prenos = (Val(Mid(a_niz, i, 1)) * Val(Mid(b_niz, j, 1)) + Prenos) \ 10
            i = i - 1
        Loop
        vmesna_vsota = Dodaj_nicle_nizu_zadaj(vmesna_vsota, dolzina_b - j)
        If Prenos > 0 Then vmesna_vsota = Prenos & vmesna_vsota
        produkt = Vsota_nizov(vmesna_vsota, produkt)
        j = j + 1
    Loop
    
    Produkt_nizov = produkt
 
End Function
 
Function Potenca(A As String, n As Integer) As String
    Dim i As Integer
    Dim produkt As String
    
    i = 2
    produkt = A
    
    Do Until i > n
        produkt = Produkt_nizov(produkt, A)
        i = i + 1
    Loop
    
    If n = 0 Then Potenca = "1" Else Potenca = produkt
End Function

Function HexToDec(A As String) As String
    Dim vmesna_vsota As String
    Dim Pozicija As String
    Dim i, dolzina_a As Integer
   
    i = 1
    dolzina_a = Len(A)
    vmesna_vsota = "0"
    
    Do Until i > dolzina_a
        Pozicija = Mid(A, i, 1)
        If Pozicija = "A" Or Pozicija = "a" Then Pozicija = "10"
        If Pozicija = "B" Or Pozicija = "b" Then Pozicija = "11"
        If Pozicija = "C" Or Pozicija = "c" Then Pozicija = "12"
        If Pozicija = "D" Or Pozicija = "d" Then Pozicija = "13"
        If Pozicija = "E" Or Pozicija = "e" Then Pozicija = "14"
        If Pozicija = "F" Or Pozicija = "f" Then Pozicija = "15"
        vmesna_vsota = Vsota_nizov(Produkt_nizov(Pozicija, Potenca("16", dolzina_a - i)), vmesna_vsota)
        i = i + 1
    Loop
    HexToDec = vmesna_vsota
End Function

Function GetCode(A As String, b As String, c As String) As String
Dim koda As String
Dim i, Vsota, dolzina As Integer
koda = HexToDec(A) & b & c
dolzina = Len(koda)
i = 1
Vsota = 0
Do Until i > dolzina
    Vsota = Vsota + Val(Mid(koda, i, 1))
    i = i + 1
Loop
Vsota = Vsota Mod 10
GetCode = koda & Vsota
i = Len(GetCode)
If i < 60 Then
  Do Until i = 60
    GetCode = "0" & GetCode
    i = i + 1
  Loop
End If
End Function

Public Function Code128$(chaine$)
  'V 1.1.0
  'Parametres : une chaine
  'Parameters : a string
  'Retour : * une chaine qui, affich e avec la police CODE128.TTF, donne le code barre
  '         * une chaine vide si param tre fourni incorrect
  'Return : * a string which give the bar code when it is dispayed with CODE128.TTF font
  '         * an empty string if the supplied parameter is no good
  Dim i%, checksum&, mini%, dummy%, tableB As Boolean
  Code128$ = ""
  If Len(chaine$) > 0 Then
  'V rifier si caract res valides
  'Check for valid characters
    For i% = 1 To Len(chaine$)
      Select Case Asc(Mid$(chaine$, i%, 1))
      Case 32 To 126, 198
      Case Else
        i% = 0
        Exit For
      End Select
    Next
    'Calculer la chaine de code en optimisant l'usage des tables B et C
    'Calculation of the code string with optimized use of tables B and C
    Code128$ = ""
    tableB = True
    If i% > 0 Then
      i% = 1 'i% devient l'index sur la chaine / i% become the string index
      Do While i% <= Len(chaine$)
        If tableB Then
          'Voir si int ressant de passer en table C / See if interesting to switch to table C
          'Oui pour 4 chiffres au d but ou   la fin, sinon pour 6 chiffres / yes for 4 digits at start or end, else if 6 digits
          mini% = IIf(i% = 1 Or i% + 3 = Len(chaine$), 4, 6)
          GoSub testnum
          If mini% < 0 Then 'Choix table C / Choice of table C
            If i% = 1 Then 'D buter sur table C / Starting with table C
              Code128$ = chr$(205)
            Else 'Commuter sur table C / Switch to table C
              Code128$ = Code128$ & chr$(199)
            End If
            tableB = False
          Else
            If i% = 1 Then Code128$ = chr$(204) 'D buter sur table B / Starting with table B
          End If
        End If
        If Not tableB Then
          'On est sur la table C, essayer de traiter 2 chiffres / We are on table C, try to process 2 digits
          mini% = 2
          GoSub testnum
          If mini% < 0 Then 'OK pour 2 chiffres, les traiter / OK for 2 digits, process it
            dummy% = Val(Mid$(chaine$, i%, 2))
            dummy% = IIf(dummy% < 95, dummy% + 32, dummy% + 100)
            Code128$ = Code128$ & chr$(dummy%)
            i% = i% + 2
          Else 'On n'a pas 2 chiffres, repasser en table B / We haven't 2 digits, switch to table B
            Code128$ = Code128$ & chr$(200)
            tableB = True
          End If
        End If
        If tableB Then
          'Traiter 1 caract re en table B / Process 1 digit with table B
          Code128$ = Code128$ & Mid$(chaine$, i%, 1)
          i% = i% + 1
        End If
      Loop
      'Calcul de la cl  de contr le / Calculation of the checksum
      For i% = 1 To Len(Code128$)
        dummy% = Asc(Mid$(Code128$, i%, 1))
        dummy% = IIf(dummy% < 127, dummy% - 32, dummy% - 100)
        If i% = 1 Then checksum& = dummy%
        checksum& = (checksum& + (i% - 1) * dummy%) Mod 103
      Next
      'Calcul du code ASCII de la cl  / Calculation of the checksum ASCII code
      checksum& = IIf(checksum& < 95, checksum& + 32, checksum& + 100)
      'Ajout de la cl  et du STOP / Add the checksum and the STOP
      Code128$ = Code128$ & chr$(checksum&) & chr$(206)
    End If
  End If
  Exit Function
testnum:
  'si les mini% caract res   partir de i% sont num riques, alors mini%=0
  'if the mini% characters from i% are numeric, then mini%=0
  mini% = mini% - 1
  If i% + mini% <= Len(chaine$) Then
    Do While mini% >= 0
      If Asc(Mid$(chaine$, i% + mini%, 1)) < 48 Or Asc(Mid$(chaine$, i% + mini%, 1)) > 57 Then Exit Do
      mini% = mini% - 1
    Loop
  End If
Return
End Function

============== START FILE: modCR_Invoice.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Function AddPaymentAmount(meansOfPaymentID As Long, qty As Double)
Dim frm As Form, strSQL As String, db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, strPaymentMenasID  As String

  Set frm = Forms.frmCRInvoice.Form
  Call PaymentDifference(frm, qty)

  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoicePayments")
  
  rs.AddNew
  rs("crInvoiceID") = frm!crInvoiceID
  rs("meansOfPaymentID") = meansOfPaymentID
  rs("paymentAmount") = Round(qty, 2)
  rs.Update
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
  frm!frmCRInvoice_Payment.Requery
  frm!frmCRInvoice_Summary.Requery

End Function

Public Sub PaymentDifference(frm As Form, qty As Double)
Dim strSQL As String, db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, paymentMenasID  As Long

  frm!frmCRInvoice_Summary.Form.Requery
  If Round(frm!frmCRInvoice_Summary!Difference - qty, 2) <> 0 Then
    strSQL = "SELECT tblAccPmt_MeansOfPayments.meansOfPaymentID " & _
             "FROM tblAccPmt_MeansOfPayments INNER JOIN tblAccPmt_MeansOfPaymentGroups ON tblAccPmt_MeansOfPayments.meansOfPaymentGroupID = tblAccPmt_MeansOfPaymentGroups.meansOfPaymentGroupID " & _
             "WHERE (companyID=crCompanyID()) AND (tblAccPmt_MeansOfPaymentGroups.meansOfPaymentGroupType= 'isCash');"
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
    paymentMenasID = rs("meansOfPaymentID")
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoicePayments")
    rs.AddNew
    rs("crInvoiceID") = frm!crInvoiceID
    rs("meansOfPaymentID") = paymentMenasID
    rs("paymentAmount") = Round(frm!frmCRInvoice_Summary!Difference - qty, 2)
    rs.Update
    rs.Close
    Set rs = Nothing
    db.Close
    Set db = Nothing
  
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub PaymentSequence(invID As Long)
Dim strSQL As String, db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, i As Integer, iMax As Integer

  strSQL = "SELECT crInvoiceID, sequence FROM tblCR_crInvoicePayments " & _
           "WHERE (crInvoiceID=" & invID & ") AND (deletedAt Is Null) " & _
           "ORDER BY paymentAmount DESC;"

  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  iMax = rs.RecordCount
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
  rs.MoveFirst
  For i = 1 To iMax
    rs.Edit
    rs("sequence") = i
    rs.Update
    If Not i = iMax Then rs.MoveNext
  Next i
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
End Sub

Public Function CreateInvoiceNumber(crInvoiceID As Long, isWriteOff As Boolean)
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset, newNumber As Long, invPrefix As String
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoices", dbOpenDynaset)
  rs.FindFirst "crInvoiceID=" & crInvoiceID
  
  If rs.NoMatch Then Exit Function
  If Not IsNull(rs("crInvoiceNumber")) Then Exit Function
  
  Set rs1 = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_LastInvoiceNumber", dbOpenDynaset)

  rs1.MoveFirst
  rs1.Edit
    If isWriteOff Then
      newNumber = rs1("lastWriteOffNumber") + 1
      rs1("lastWriteOffNumber") = newNumber
    Else
      newNumber = rs1("lastInvoiceNumber") + 1
      rs1("lastInvoiceNumber") = newNumber
    End If
  rs1.Update
  rs1.Close
  Set rs1 = Nothing
  
  invPrefix = DLookup("cashRegisterName", "qryCRHome_CurrentValues")
  
  rs.Edit
  rs("crInvoiceNumber") = invPrefix & "-" & Format$(newNumber, "0000000")
  rs("crInvoiceCreatedAt") = Now()
  If isWriteOff Then
    rs("isWriteOff") = True
  Else
    rs("isWriteOff") = False
  End If
  rs.Update
  
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
End Function

Public Function printInvoice(invID As Long)
'On Error Resume Next
Dim db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, frmName As String, frm As Form, strPrinterName As String, strReportName As String
Dim defaultPrinter As Printer, prtPrinter As Printer, isWriteOff As Boolean, resp As Integer

  frmName = "frmCRInvoice"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    strPrinterName = Forms(frmName)!InvoicePrinterName.Column(2)
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
  Else
    frmName = "frmCRInvoices"
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
      strPrinterName = Forms(frmName)!InvoicePrinterName.Column(2)
    Else
      strPrinterName = Forms!frmCRHome!frmCRHome_SettingsWaiterSettings.Form!invoicePrinterID.Column(2)
    End If
  End If
    
  Set defaultPrinter = Application.Printer
 
  For Each prtPrinter In Application.Printers
    If Right(prtPrinter.DeviceName, 9) = Right(strPrinterName, 9) Then
      Set Application.Printer = prtPrinter
      Exit For
    End If
  Next prtPrinter
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoices", dbOpenDynaset)
  rs.FindFirst "(crInvoiceID)=" & invID
  rs.Edit
  rs("printingCount") = rs("printingCount") + 1
  isWriteOff = rs("isWriteOff")
  rs.Update
  
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
  On Error GoTo ERROR_HANDLING

  If isWriteOff Or IsNull(DLookup("customerID", "tblCR_crInvoices", "crInvoiceID=" & invID)) Then
    strReportName = "rptCRInvoice"
  Else
    strReportName = "rptCRInvoiceCustomer"
  End If

printInvoice:

  DoCmd.OpenReport strReportName, acViewNormal, , "crInvoiceID=" & invID
  Set Application.Printer = defaultPrinter
  
  Exit Function

ERROR_HANDLING:
  On Error GoTo -1
  On Error GoTo ERROR_HANDLING1
  Set Application.Printer = defaultPrinter
  DoCmd.OpenReport strReportName, acViewNormal, , "crInvoiceID=" & invID
  resp = MsgBox("Ne najdem izbranega tiskalnika ra unov (" & prtPrinter.DeviceName & _
                "), zato je bil ra un natisnjen na privzeti tiskalnik (" & defaultPrinter.DeviceName & ").", vbInformation)
  Exit Function
  
ERROR_HANDLING1:
  On Error GoTo -1
  On Error Resume Next
  Beep
  resp = MsgBox("Usodna napaka! Ne najdem tiskalnika ra unov. Ra un bo izdan, vendar ne bo natisnjen!", vbCritical)

End Function
Public Sub FindCustomer()
Dim f As Form, F1 As Form
  
  Set f = Forms!frmCRInvoice_Customer.Form
  Set F1 = f!frmCRInvoice_CustomerSub.Form

 
  If IsNull(f!strCustomer) Or f!strCustomer = "" Then Exit Sub

  F1.Filter = "(partnerName like '*" & f!strCustomer & "*') Or (TaxNumber like '*" & f!strCustomer & "*') "

  If Not F1.FilterOn = True Then F1.FilterOn = True
    
End Sub

Public Sub DeleteInvoice(oldInvoiceID As Long, oldInvoiceNumber As String, createCopy As Boolean)
Dim strRemark As String, strSQL As String, newInvoiceID As Long, isWriteOff As Boolean
  
  newInvoiceID = CopyInvoiceID(oldInvoiceID, "storno ra una: " & oldInvoiceNumber, -1, True)
  isWriteOff = DLookup("isWriteOff", "tblCR_crInvoices", "relatedInvoiceID=" & oldInvoiceID)
  Call CreateInvoiceNumber(newInvoiceID, isWriteOff)
  
  strRemark = "storniran z ra unom: " & DLookup("crInvoiceNumber", "tblCR_crInvoices", "crInvoiceID=" & newInvoiceID)
  
  If Not IsNull(DLookup("remark", "tblCR_crInvoices", "crInvoiceID=" & oldInvoiceID)) Then
    strRemark = Left(strRemark & " + " & DLookup("remark", "tblCR_crInvoices", "crInvoiceID=" & oldInvoiceID), 255)
  End If
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblCR_crInvoices SET remark = '" & strRemark & "', relatedInvoiceID = " & newInvoiceID & " WHERE crInvoiceID=" & oldInvoiceID & ";"
           
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  Call verifyInvoice(newInvoiceID, True, True, False, False)
  Call printInvoice(newInvoiceID)
  
  If createCopy Then
    Call CopyInvoiceID(oldInvoiceID, "kopija ra una: " & oldInvoiceNumber, 1)
  End If

End Sub

Public Function CopyInvoiceID(oldInvoiceID As Long, strRemark As String, delInv As Long, Optional copyPayments As Boolean) As Long
Dim strSQL As String, rs0 As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset, newInvoiceID As Long, strSign As String

  strSQL = "SELECT tableName, crDateID, employeeID, discount, customerID, customerData, crInvoiceID , isWriteoff " & _
           "FROM tblCR_crInvoices WHERE crInvoiceID=" & oldInvoiceID & ";"
  Set rs0 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  Set rs1 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoices")
  
  rs1.AddNew
  rs1("tableName") = rs0("tableName")
  rs1("crDateID") = rs0("crDateID")
  rs1("employeeID") = rs0("employeeID")
  rs1("discount") = rs0("discount")
  rs1("customerID") = rs0("customerID")
  rs1("customerData") = rs0("customerData")
  rs1("relatedInvoiceID") = oldInvoiceID
  rs1("remark") = Left(strRemark & IIf(Not IsNull(rs1("remark")), " + " & rs1("remark"), Null), 255)
  newInvoiceID = rs1("crInvoiceID")
  rs1.Update
  rs1.Close
  Set rs1 = Nothing
  
  If delInv = -1 Then strSign = "-1*tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.qty as q, " Else strSign = "tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.qty as q, "
  strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblCR_crInvoiceDetails (crInvoiceID, costCenterProductID, productName, qty, vatRateID, salesPrice, employeeID, orderRemark, jobID, orderedAt, ticketAt, deletedAt) " & _
           "SELECT " & newInvoiceID & " AS newId, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.costCenterProductID, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.productName, " & strSign & _
           "tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.vatRateID, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.salesPrice, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.employeeID, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.orderRemark, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.jobID, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.orderedAt, " & _
           "tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.ticketAt, tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.deletedAt " & _
           "FROM tblCR_crInvoices INNER JOIN tblCR_crInvoiceDetails ON tblCR_crInvoices.crInvoiceID = tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.crInvoiceID " & _
           "WHERE (((tblCR_crInvoiceDetails.crInvoiceID)=" & oldInvoiceID & "));"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  
  If copyPayments = 0 Then Exit Function
  If delInv = -1 Then strSign = "-1*tblCR_crInvoicePayments.paymentAmount," Else strSign = "tblCR_crInvoicePayments.paymentAmount,"
  strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblCR_crInvoicePayments (crInvoiceID, meansOfPaymentID, sequence, paymentAmount,  createdAt) " & _
           "SELECT " & newInvoiceID & " AS newId, meansOfPaymentID, sequence, " & strSign & "tblCR_crInvoicePayments.createdAt " & _
           "FROM tblCR_crInvoicePayments " & _
           "WHERE (crInvoiceID=" & oldInvoiceID & ") AND (deletedAt Is Null);"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)

  CopyInvoiceID = newInvoiceID
  
End Function


Public Sub CompanyInvoice(frmInvoice As Form)
Dim frm As Form, strWhere As String

  If Round(frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!Difference, 2) <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Znesek pla il ni enak znesku ra una!"
    Exit Sub
  End If
    
     DoCmd.OpenForm "frmCRInvoice_Customer"
     Set frm = Forms!frmCRInvoice_Customer.Form
     frm!crInvoiceID = frmInvoice!crInvoiceID
    
     Set frm = frm!frmCRInvoice_CustomerSub.Form
     strWhere = "crInvoiceID=" & frmInvoice!crInvoiceID
     frm!tableName = frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!tableName
     frm!crInvoiceNumber = frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!crInvoiceNumber
     frm!invAmount = frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!discountedTotal
     If Not IsNull(DLookup("customerID", "tblCR_crInvoices", strWhere)) Then
       frm!oldBpID = DLookup("(customerID)", "tblCR_crInvoices", strWhere)
     End If
    
     If frm!oldBpID <> 0 Then
       strWhere = "(partnerID)=" & (frm!oldBpID)
       frm!oldBpName = DLookup("bpName1", "tblPtr_Partners", strWhere)
       If Not IsNull(DLookup("bpName2", "tblPtr_Partners", strWhere)) Then frm!oldBpName = frm!oldBpName & ", " & DLookup("bpName2", "tblPtr_Partners", strWhere)
       frm!oldBpTaxNumber = DLookup("bpTaxNumber", "tblPtr_Partners", strWhere)
       frm!oldBpAddress = DLookup("bpStreet", "tblPtr_Partners", strWhere)
       frm!oldBpAddress = frm!oldBpAddress & ", " & DLookup("bpPlace", "tblPtr_Partners", strWhere)
     End If

End Sub

Public Sub CloseFormInvoice(frmInvoice As Form)
'On Error Resume Next
Dim resp As Integer

  If frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!Difference <> 0 And Not IsNull(frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!crInvoiceNumber) Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Znesek pla il ni enak znesku ra una! Ali  eli  vseeno zapreti formo?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo)
    If resp = vbYes Then
      DoCmd.Close acForm, frmInvoice.Name
    Else
      Exit Sub
    End If
  Else
    DoCmd.Close acForm, frmInvoice.Name
  End If

End Sub

Public Sub CreateVerifyPrintInvoice(frmInvoice As Form)
Dim invID As Long

  If frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!Difference <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Znesek pla il ni enak znesku ra una!"
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  invID = frmInvoice!crInvoiceID
  Call CreateInvoiceNumber(invID, frmInvoice!isWriteOff)
  Call verifyInvoice(invID, True, False, False, False)
  Call printInvoice(invID)
  DoCmd.Close acForm, frmInvoice.Name
  
End Sub

Public Sub InvoiceDiscount(frmInvoice As Form, discountAmount As Double)

  If IsNull(frmInvoice!discount) Then frmInvoice!discount = 0
  frmInvoice!discount = frmInvoice!discount + discountAmount
  If frmInvoice!discount < 0 Then frmInvoice!discount.ForeColor = 255 Else frmInvoice!discount.ForeColor = 4210752
  frmInvoice!endAmount = Round((1 - frmInvoice!discount) * frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary!undiscountedTotal, 2)
  
End Sub

Public Sub InvoiceDiscountSave(frmInvoice As Form)
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset

  If hasValue(frmInvoice!crInvoiceID) Then
    Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoices", dbOpenDynaset)
    If IsNull(frmInvoice!discount) Then frmInvoice!discount = 0
    rs.FindFirst "(crInvoiceID) = " & (frmInvoice!crInvoiceID)
    rs.Edit
    rs("discount") = Round(frmInvoice!discount, 2)
    rs.Update
    rs.Close
    Set rs = Nothing
    frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary.Requery
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub paymentType(frmInvoice As Form, selectedPaymentType As Boolean)
Dim strSQL  As String
  
  If frmInvoice!isWriteOff <> selectedPaymentType Then
    frmInvoice!isWriteOff = selectedPaymentType
    strSQL = "DELETE *, crInvoiceID FROM tblCR_crInvoicePayments " & _
             "WHERE crInvoiceID=" & frmInvoice!crInvoiceID & ";"
    Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  Else
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If frmInvoice!isWriteOff Then
    
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.BackColor = -2147483610
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.HoverColor = -2147483610
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.PressedColor = -2147483610
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.BorderStyle = 1
  
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.BackColor = 16777215
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.HoverColor = 16777215
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.PressedColor = 16777215
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.BorderStyle = 0
  
    frmInvoice!isWriteOff = True
    frmInvoice!lstMeansOfPaymentID.Requery
    frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary.Requery
  
  Else
 
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.BackColor = 16777215
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.HoverColor = 16777215
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.PressedColor = 16777215
    frmInvoice!cmdPayment.BorderStyle = 0
  
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.BackColor = -2147483610
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.HoverColor = -2147483610
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.PressedColor = -2147483610
    frmInvoice!cmdWriteOff.BorderStyle = 1
  
    frmInvoice!isWriteOff = 0
    frmInvoice!lstMeansOfPaymentID.Requery
    frmInvoice!frmCRInvoice_Summary.Requery
  
  End If
End Sub

Public Sub AddStudentCoupons(frmInvoice As Form, invID As Long, createInv As Boolean)
Dim strSQL As String, noOfStudents As Integer, frm As Form
  
  noOfStudents = NoOfStudentMeals(invID)
  
  If noOfStudents = 0 Then
    MsgBox "Na ra unu ni  tudentskih jedi, zato pla ilo z bonom ni mo no!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If Not CompleteStudentMeal(invID) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Se tevek  tudentskih jedi na ra unu ni ve kratnik vrednosti  tudentskega obroka. Avtomatsko dodajanje bonov ni mo no.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblCR_crInvoicePayments SET deletedAt = Now() WHERE crInvoiceID=" & invID & ";"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  
  Set frm = Forms!frmCRHome.Form
    
  Call AddPaymentAmount(frm!studentCouponID, noOfStudents * frm!studentCouponValue)
  
  If createInv Then Call CreateVerifyPrintInvoice(frmInvoice)
  
End Sub

Public Function NoOfStudentMeals(invID As Long) As Integer
Dim db As DAO.Database, qDef As QueryDef, rs As DAO.Recordset
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryCRInvoice_StudentMealsCount")
  qDef.Parameters("invID") = invID
  Set rs = qDef.OpenRecordset()
  
  If IsNull(rs("noOfStudents")) Then
    NoOfStudentMeals = 0
  Else
    NoOfStudentMeals = rs("noOfStudents")
  End If
  
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
End Function

Public Function CompleteStudentMeal(invID As Long) As Boolean
Dim db As DAO.Database, qDef As QueryDef, rs As DAO.Recordset, noOfStudents As Integer, frm As Form
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryCRInvoice_StudentMealsAmount")
  qDef.Parameters("invID") = invID
  Set rs = qDef.OpenRecordset()
    
  Set frm = Forms!frmCRHome.Form
  
  If (Round(rs("StudentMealsTotal"), 2) Mod Round(frm!studentMealValue, 2)) <> 0 Then
    CompleteStudentMeal = False
  Else
    CompleteStudentMeal = True
  End If
  
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing

End Function

Public Function NoOfPaymentMeans(invID As Long) As Integer
  NoOfPaymentMeans = DCount("crInvoicePaymentID", "tblCR_crInvoicePayments", "(deletedAt Is Null) And crInvoiceID=" & invID)
End Function
