Public Sub FindProduct(Optional DblClick As Boolean)
'poi  e artikel

Dim f As Form, F1 As Form
  
  Set f = Forms!frmCRProductsAdd.Form
  Set F1 = Forms!frmCRProductsAdd!frmCRProductsAdd_Products.Form
  If DblClick Then
    If Not IsNull(Right(Screen.ActiveControl.Caption, 1)) Then f!strProductName = Left(f!strProductName, Len(f!strProductName) - 1) & Right(Screen.ActiveControl.Caption, 1)
  Else
    If Not IsNull(Left(Screen.ActiveControl.Caption, 1)) Then f!strProductName = f!strProductName & Left(Screen.ActiveControl.Caption, 1)
  End If
  
  With F1.RecordsetClone
    .FindFirst "costCenterProductName like  '" & f!strProductName & "*'"
    If Not .NoMatch Then
      F1.Bookmark = .Bookmark
    End If
  End With

End Sub

Public Sub FrmCRProductsAddCmdHandling(frm As Form)
'On Error Resume Next
'ugotovi ali je na gumbu meni ali artikel
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset
  
  strSQL = "SELECT parameterID, commandName, isProduct, qryName " & _
           "FROM tblCR_frmProductAddCommands " & _
           "WHERE (costCenterID=" & crCostCenterID() & ") And (commandName='" & Right(Screen.ActiveControl.Name, 3) & "');"
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)


  If rs("isProduct") Then
    
    Call AddProduct(frm!crInvoiceID, rs("parameterID"), 1)
  
  Else
  
    If Not IsNull(frm!lastButton) And Not frm!lastButton = "" Then
      frm.Controls(frm!lastButton).BackColor = 16777215                     'bela
      frm.Controls(frm!lastButton).ForeColor = 12156969                     'modra
    End If
    
    Screen.ActiveControl.BackColor = -2147483610                            'siva
    Screen.ActiveControl.ForeColor = 255                                    'rde a
    frm!lastButton = Screen.ActiveControl.Name
    
    Set frm = frm!frmCRProductsAdd_Products.Form
    
    If IsNull(rs("qryName")) Then
      If frm.RecordSource <> "qryCRProductsAdd_All" Then frm.RecordSource = "qryCRProductsAdd_All"
      If Not IsNull(rs("parameterID")) Then
        frm.Filter = "crMenuID = " & rs("parameterID")
        frm.FilterOn = True
        frm.OrderBy = "crSequence DESC, costCenterProductName"
        frm.OrderByOn = True
      End If
    Else
      frm.RecordSource = rs("qryName")
      frm.OrderByOn = False
    End If

  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub AddProduct(crInvoiceID As Long, ccProductID As Long, qty As Double, Optional strOrderRemark As String)
'doda artikel na ra un
Dim strSQL As String, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset, rs2 As DAO.Recordset
Dim frmName As String, frm As Form

  frmName = "frmCRProductsAdd"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
  Else
    frmName = "frmCRTables"
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
  End If
   
  Call CheckTableStatus(frm)
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoiceDetails", dbOpenDynaset)
  
  strSQL = "SELECT productID, costCenterProductName, salesPrice  " & _
           "FROM tblProdCat_CostCenterProducts " & _
           "WHERE costCenterProductID= " & ccProductID & ";"
  Set rs1 = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
    
  strSQL = "SELECT TOP 1 employeeID, jobID " & _
           "FROM tblEmpTime_DailyTimeclock " & _
           "WHERE employeeID=" & Forms!frmCRTables.Form!employeeID & " " & _
           "ORDER BY jobStart DESC;"
  Set rs2 = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  rs.AddNew
  rs("crInvoiceID") = crInvoiceID
  rs("qty") = qty
  'rs("productID") = rs1("productID")
  rs("costCenterProductID") = ccProductID
  rs("productName") = rs1("costCenterProductName")
  rs("vatRateID") = DFirst("vatRateID", "tblProdCat_Products", "productID=" & rs1("productID"))
  rs("salesPrice") = rs1("salesPrice")
  rs("employeeID") = rs2("employeeID")
  rs("jobID") = rs2("jobID")
  If strOrderRemark <> "" Then rs("orderRemark") = strOrderRemark
  rs.Update
  
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  rs1.Close
  Set rs1 = Nothing
  rs2.Close
  Set rs2 = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
  Call RequeryActiveForms
  
End Sub

Public Function EditProductNoOfGlasses(invDetailID As Long, qty As String)
'urejanje postavke ra una: navodila -  t. kozarcev
  Dim strSQL As String
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblCR_crInvoiceDetails SET orderRemark='" & qty & "' WHERE crInvoiceDetailID=" & invDetailID & ";"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  
End Function

Public Function EditProductQty(invDetailID As Long, qty As Double)
'urejanje postavke ra una: spremeni koli ino

  Dim strSQL As String
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblCR_crInvoiceDetails SET qty=" & Replace(Round(CDbl(qty), 2), ",", ".") & " WHERE crInvoiceDetailID=" & invDetailID & ";"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  Call RequeryActiveForms
  
End Function

Public Sub DeleteProduct(frmProducts As Form, invDetailID As Long, isTicketed As Boolean)
Dim frm As Form, resp As Integer, tableName As String, productName As String, invID As Long, delete As Boolean
  
  Set frm = Forms!frmCRTables.Form
  invID = frmProducts!crInvoiceID
  productName = frmProducts!productName
  
  Beep
  If Not isTicketed Then
    resp = vbYes
    delete = True
  Else
    If frm!isManager Then
      resp = MsgBox("Ali naj bri em artikel " & productName & "?", vbCritical + vbYesNo)
      If resp = vbYes Then
        Call Ticket(" crInvoiceDetailID=" & invDetailID, True)
        delete = True
      End If
    Else
      tableName = "st" & frm!employeeID.Column(2)
      If frm!tableName = tableName Then
        resp = MsgBox("Artikel se  e nahaja na tvoji storno mizi. Bri e ga lahko samo mened er!", vbInformation)
        Exit Sub
      End If
      
      resp = MsgBox("Artikel " & productName & " je boniran, zato brisanje ni mogo e. Ali ga prenesem na tvojo storno mizo - " & tableName & "? " & _
      "(opomba:  e storno mize  e nima  odprte, bo avtomatsko odprta)", vbCritical + vbYesNo)
      If resp = vbYes Then
        invID = NewTableID(tableName, frm!employeeID, False, False)
        delete = False
      End If
    End If
  End If
  
  If resp = vbYes Then
    Call ExecuteDeleteProduct(invID, invDetailID, delete)
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form.Requery
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form.Requery
    frmProducts.Requery
  End If
        
End Sub

Public Sub ExecuteDeleteProduct(invID As Long, invDetailID As Long, delete As Boolean)
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset
    
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoiceDetails", dbOpenDynaset)
    
    rs.FindFirst "crInvoiceDetailID=" & invDetailID
    rs.Edit
    rs("crInvoiceID") = invID
    If delete Then rs("deletedAt") = Now()
    rs.Update
    
    rs.Close
    Set rs = Nothing
    db.Close
    Set db = Nothing
    
End Sub

Public Sub Ticket(strWhere As String, isReversal As Boolean)
On Error GoTo PROC_ERROR
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, strReportName As String, strWhere1 As String, strSQL As String
Dim defaultPrinter As Printer, prtPrinter As Printer, resp As Integer, ticketPrinterFound As Boolean

  Set db = CurrentDb()

  If isReversal Then
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("qryCRTicket_ReversalPrinters")
  Else
    Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("qryCRTicket_Printers")
  End If

  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then GoTo PROC_EXIT
  
  If isReversal Then
    strReportName = "rptCRTicket_Reversal"
  Else
    strReportName = "rptCRTicket"
  End If
      
  Set defaultPrinter = Application.Printer
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF

    strWhere1 = "(printerID=" & rs("printerID") & ") And (ticketID=" & rs("ticketID") & ") And " & strWhere
   
    If DCount("*", "qryCRTicket", strWhere1) <> 0 Then

      ticketPrinterFound = False

      For Each prtPrinter In Application.Printers
        If Right(prtPrinter.DeviceName, 9) = Right(rs("printerFullName"), 9) Then
          Set Application.Printer = prtPrinter
          ticketPrinterFound = True
          Exit For
        End If
      Next prtPrinter

      On Error GoTo PRINTING_ERR
      
      If Not ticketPrinterFound Then
        Set Application.Printer = defaultPrinter
        MsgBox "Ne najdem tiskalnika bonov: " & rs("printerFullName") & ". " & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & "Bon bo natisnjen na tiskalniku: " & defaultPrinter.DeviceName & ".", vbInformation
      End If

      DoCmd.OpenReport strReportName, acViewNormal, , strWhere1
      'DoCmd.OpenReport strReportName, acViewPreview, , strWhere1, acDialog
      On Error GoTo PROC_ERROR
      'MsgBox "tiskam " & Application.printer.DeviceName & vbCrLf & "strWhere1: " & strWhere1

    End If

CONTINUE_LOOP:
    rs.MoveNext
  
  Loop
  
TICKETING:

  If Not rs.EOF Then GoTo CONTINUE_LOOP:
  Set Application.Printer = defaultPrinter

TICKETING1:
  On Error Resume Next
  
  If Not isReversal Then
    strSQL = "UPDATE qryCRTicket SET qryCRTicket.ticketAt = Now() WHERE " & strWhere & ";"
    Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  End If

  Call RequeryActiveForms
  
PROC_EXIT:
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  Exit Sub
  
PROC_ERROR:
  MsgBox "Error: (" & err.Number & ") " & err.description, vbCritical
  resp = MsgBox("Bon ne bo natisnjen. Ali naj artikle ozna im kot bonirane?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
  If resp = vbYes Then Resume TICKETING1
  Resume PROC_EXIT

PRINTING_ERR:
  On Error GoTo -1
  On Error GoTo PRINTING_ERR1
  Set Application.Printer = defaultPrinter
  DoCmd.OpenReport strReportName, acViewNormal, , strWhere1
  MsgBox "Te ave pri tiskanju bonov na tiskalniku " & prtPrinter.DeviceName & _
                ". Bon je bil natisnjen na tiskalnik (" & defaultPrinter.DeviceName & ").", vbInformation
  GoTo TICKETING

PRINTING_ERR1:
  Beep
  MsgBox "Napaka pri tiskanju bonov. Artikli bodo bonirani, vendar bon ne bo natisnjen!", vbCritical
  GoTo TICKETING
  
End Sub

Public Function MoveProduct(qty As Double)
Dim frm As Form, db As Database, rs1 As DAO.Recordset, rs2 As DAO.Recordset, resp As Integer

  Set frm = Forms!frmCRMove.Form
  
  If qty > 0 And qty <= frm!id1.Column(2) Then
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    Set rs1 = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_TempMove1", dbOpenDynaset)
    Set rs2 = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_TempMove2", dbOpenDynaset)
    
    rs1.FindFirst "(ID)=" & (frm!id1)
    rs2.FindFirst "(ID)=" & (frm!id1)
    
    If rs2.NoMatch Then
      rs2.AddNew
      rs2("ID") = rs1("ID")
      rs2("strName") = rs1("strName")
      rs2("qty") = Round(qty, 2)
      rs2("seq1") = rs1("seq1")
      rs2("seq2") = rs1("seq2")
      rs2.Update
    Else
      rs2.Edit
      rs2("qty") = Round(qty + rs2("qty"), 2)
      rs2.Update
    End If
    
    rs1.Edit
    rs1("qty") = Round(frm!id1.Column(2) - qty, 2)
    rs1.Update
    
  Else
  
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Koli ina mora biti med 0 in " & frm!id1.Column(2) & "!", vbCritical)
    End
    
  End If
    
  rs1.Close
  Set rs1 = Nothing
  rs2.Close
  Set rs2 = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing

  frm!id1.Requery
  frm!id2.Requery
  
End Function

Public Sub RefreshCmdButtons() 'names command buttons on form frmCRProductsAdd
Dim frm As Form, strWhere As String, i As Integer, j As Integer

  DoCmd.OpenForm "frmCRProductsAdd", acDesign
  Set frm = Forms!frmCRProductsAdd.Form
  
  For j = 2 To 5
    For i = 1 To 21
      strWhere = "costCenterID=" & crCostCenterID & " And commandName = '" & j & Format(i, "00") & "'"
      If IsNull(DFirst("commandCaption", "tblCR_frmProductAddCommands", strWhere)) Then
        frm("cmd" & j & Format(i, "00")).Caption = "(prazno)"
      Else
        frm("cmd" & j & Format(i, "00")).Caption = DFirst("commandCaption", "tblCR_frmProductAddCommands", strWhere)
      End If
        
      If Not DFirst("isProduct", "tblCR_frmProductAddCommands", strWhere) Then ' e je izbran meni,  rne  rke
        frm("cmd" & j & Format(i, "00")).ForeColor = 12156969
        frm("cmd" & j & Format(i, "00")).FontUnderline = False
      Else
        frm("cmd" & j & Format(i, "00")).ForeColor = 8355711
        frm("cmd" & j & Format(i, "00")).FontUnderline = False
      End If
    Next i
  Next j

  DoCmd.Save acForm, frm.Name
  DoCmd.Close acForm, frm.Name
  
End Sub

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Function NewTableID(strTableName As String, employeeID As String, showMsg As Boolean, selectNewTable As Boolean) As Long
Dim strWhere As String, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, i As Integer, frm As Form, frmName As String

  strWhere = "IsNull(crInvoiceCreatedAt) And IsNull(deletedAt) And tableName = '" & strTableName & "'"
  Set frm = Forms!frmCRTables.Form
  
  If strTableName = "malica" Then
    If Not IsNull(DFirst("tableName", "tblCR_crInvoices", strWhere)) Then ' miza malica je laho samo ena!
      If showMsg Then i = MsgBox("Miza malica je  e odprta! Artikle dodaj na obstoje o mizo!", vbInformation)
      Exit Function
    End If
  End If
  
  If Left(strTableName, 2) = "st" Then
    If IsNull(DLookup("crInvoiceID", "tblCR_crInvoices", strWhere & " And employeeID=" & employeeID)) Then
      GoTo createNewTable
    Else
      If showMsg Then i = MsgBox("Miza " & strTableName & " je  e odprta! Odprta ima  lahko samo eno storno mizo. Artikle dodaj na obstoje o mizo!", vbInformation)
      NewTableID = DLookup("crInvoiceID", "tblCR_crInvoices", strWhere & " And employeeID=" & employeeID)
      Exit Function
    End If
  End If
  
  If Not IsNull(DFirst("tableName", "tblCR_crInvoices", strWhere)) Then
    For i = 1 To 9
       If IsNull(DFirst("tableName", "tblCR_crInvoices", Left(strWhere, Len(strWhere) - 1) & "_" & i & "'")) Then
         strTableName = strTableName & "_" & i
         GoTo createNewTable
       Else
         If i = 9 Then
           Beep
           i = MsgBox("Preve  odprtih miz z imenom " & strTableName & ". Izberi drugo mizo!", vbExclamation)
           Exit Function
         End If
       End If
    Next i
  End If
    
createNewTable:
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_crInvoices")
  rs.AddNew
  rs("crDateID") = DFirst("crDateID", "tblCR_crDates", "IsNull(closedAt)")
  rs("tableName") = strTableName
  rs("employeeID") = employeeID
  NewTableID = rs("crInvoiceID")
  rs.Update
  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
  frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Requery
  frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Requery
  If hasValue(selectNewTable) Then
    If selectNewTable = True Then
      frm!crInvoiceID = NewTableID
      frm!tableName = strTableName
      frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form!invID = NewTableID
      frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form!invID = NewTableID
    End If
  End If
  
On Error GoTo ErrorExit
  'DoCmd.Echo False
  frmName = "frmCRRestaurantPlan"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    
    DoCmd.Close acForm, frmName
    
    frmName = "frmCRMove"
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
      Set frm = Forms(frmName)
      frm!crInvoice2ID.Requery
      frm!crInvoice2ID = NewTableID
    Else
      DoCmd.OpenForm "frmCRProductsAdd"
      Forms.frmCRProductsAdd.Form!crInvoiceID = NewTableID
      Forms.frmCRProductsAdd.Form!tableName = strTableName
    End If
    
  End If
  
ErrorExit:
  DoCmd.Echo True
End Function

Public Sub DeleteTable(crInvoiceID As Long, tableName As String)
Dim frm As Form, resp As Integer, strSQL As String, printInv As Boolean

  If Not IsNull(DFirst("crInvoiceDetailID", "tblCR_crInvoiceDetails", "crInvoiceID=" & crInvoiceID & " And Not IsNull(ticketAt) And IsNull(deletedAt) And qty<>0")) Then
    printInv = True
    If Not Forms!frmCRTables.Form!isManager = True Then
      Beep
      MsgBox ("Na mizi so bonirani artikli. Mizo lahko bri e samo manager!")
      Exit Sub
    End If
  End If
  
  resp = MsgBox("Ali  eli  brisati mizo " & tableName & "?", vbCritical + vbYesNo)
  If resp <> 6 Then Exit Sub
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblCR_crInvoices SET tblCR_crInvoices.deletedAt = Now() " & _
           "WHERE crInvoiceID=" & crInvoiceID & ";"

  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  If printInv Then Call printInvoice(crInvoiceID)
  Call ResetFormTables
  
End Sub

Public Sub CheckTableStatus(frm As Form)

  If Not hasValue(frm!crInvoiceID) Then Exit Sub
  If Not TableIsOpened(frm!crInvoiceID) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Miza je zaklju ena ali brisana!"
    
    Set frm = Forms!frmCRTables.Form
    frm!crInvoiceID = Null
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form.Requery
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form.Requery
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, "frmCRProductsAdd") Then
      DoCmd.Close acForm, "frmCRProductsAdd"
    End If
    End
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Function TableIsOpened(invID As Long) As Boolean
  
  TableIsOpened = False
  If Not IsNull(DLookup("deletedAt", "tblCR_crInvoices", "crInvoiceID=" & invID)) Then Exit Function
  If Not IsNull(DLookup("crInvoiceCreatedAt", "tblCR_crInvoices", "crInvoiceID=" & invID)) Then Exit Function
  TableIsOpened = True
  
End Function

Option Compare Database

Public Sub TerminateDay()
'On Error Resume Next
Dim strPath As String
Dim strTableName As String
Dim resp As Integer, pass As String
Dim fso As Object, sourceFile As String, targetFile As String
Dim eMailSubject As String, eMailBody As String
Dim deleteCR As Boolean

  eMailSubject = ""
  eMailBody = ""
  deleteCR = False
  
  If OpenedApplications <> "" Then
    DoCmd.OpenReport "rptCRReportsOpened_Applications"
    resp = MsgBox("Obstajajo odprte aplikacije " & Left(OpenedApplications, Len(OpenedApplications) - 2) & "). Pred nadaljevanjem jih zapri. " & _
    " e ne gre druga e, ugasni ra unalnik.", vbInformation)
    resp = vbNo
    If resp = vbNo Then Exit Sub
  End If
  
On Error GoTo errorMsg1

  strPath = "c:\Companies\Applications\Data\dbGostinstvo\"
  
  If DCount("*", "tblCR_crInvoices") = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Blagajna je prazna. Zaklju ek ni mogo !", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
    
  If DCount("*", "qryCRTerminateDay_OpenedTables") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Obstajajo odprte mize! Zaklju ek ni mogo !", vbExclamation
    Exit Sub
  End If

  If DCount("*", "qryCRTerminateDay_PaymentDifference") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Obstajajo ra uni z neurejenimi pla ili! Zaklju ek ni mogo !", vbExclamation
    Exit Sub
  End If

  If DCount("*", "qryCRReportsOpened_FVIErrors") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Obstajajo dav no nepotrjeni ra uni ra uni! Ali naj vseeno zaklju im blagajno?", vbExclamation + vbYesNo)
    If resp = vbNo Then Exit Sub
  End If
  
  Beep
  resp = MsgBox("Ali  eli  zaklju iti blagajno?", vbCritical + vbYesNo)
  If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  
  pass = InputBox("Geslo:")
  If pass <> "jp1998" Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Napa no geslo!", vbExclamation)
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  Forms!frmCRHOME_terminateday.Form!status = "Kopiram podatke ..."
  
  eMailBody = "Kopiram podatke!"
    
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappCRTerminateDay_Date")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappCRTerminateDay_Invoices")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qupdCRTerminateDay_Invoices")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappCRTerminateDay_InvoiceDetails")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappCRTerminateDay_InvoicePayments")
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True

  Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
  sourceFile = strPath & "dbCashRegisterInvoices.accdb"
  targetFile = strPath & "cashRegisterArchive\dbCR_" & Format$(Now, "yyyymmddhhmmss") & ".accdb"
  Call fso.Copyfile(sourceFile, targetFile)
  
  If DLookup("sumCR", "qryCRTerminateDay_CheckExported1") = DLookup("sumCR", "qryCRTerminateDay_CheckExported2") Then
    eMailSubject = DLookup("costCenterAbb", "tblCmpy_costCenters", "costCenterID=" & DFirst("costCenterID", "qryCRHome_CurrentValues"))
    eMailSubject = eMailSubject & "-" & Format$(DLookup("openedAt", "tblCR_crDates"), "dd.mm.")
    eMailBody = Format$(DSum("paymentAmount", "tblCR_crInvoicePayments", "deletedAt Is Null"), "standard") & " EUR"
    MsgBox "Blagajna je bila uspe no zaklju ena!  e par trenutkov ...", vbInformation
    deleteCR = True
  Else
    MsgBox "Pri zaklju ku je pri lo do te av! Obvesti pisarno!", vbCritical
    eMailBody = "Pri zakljucku je prislo do tezav. Podatki prene eni v arhiv se ne ujemajo s podatki v blagajni!"
  End If
  
  On Error GoTo errorMsg2
  Call eMailCRInfo(eMailSubject, eMailBody, deleteCR)

Exit Sub

errorMsg1:
  
  Beep
  resp = MsgBox("Pri zaklju ku je pri lo do te av! Obvesti pisarno!", vbCritical)
  eMailBody = eMailBody & " Pri zakljucku je prislo do tezav! (Error: " & err.Number & " " & err.description & ")"
  Call eMailCRInfo(eMailSubject, eMailBody, deleteCR)
  
errorMsg2:
  
  Beep
  eMailBody = "Tezave pri kopiranju datoteke dbCashRegisterInvoices.accdb!(Error: " & err.Number & " " & err.description & ")"
  Call eMailCRInfo(eMailSubject, eMailBody, deleteCR)
  
End Sub

Public Function OpenedApplications() As String
'On Error Resume Next
Dim db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, strPath As String, strFileName As String, resp As Integer

  OpenedApplications = ""
  'get the first filename
  strPath = "c:\Companies\Applications\Applications\"
  strFileName = Dir(strPath, vbNormal)
  Call RunSQL("DELETE * FROM tblCR_TempOpenedApplications;")
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblCR_TempOpenedApplications", dbOpenDynaset)
  
  Do                                                      'Loop through the balance of files and tries do delete file, if deletion fails app is really opened
    If strFileName = "" Then GoTo continueTerminateDay    'check to see if you have a filename
    On Error GoTo ADD_APPNAME
    
    If Right(strFileName, 6) = "laccdb" Then
      If Left(strFileName, Len(strFileName) - 7) <> Left(Application.CurrentProject.Name, Len(Application.CurrentProject.Name) - 6) Then
        Kill strPath & strFileName
      End If
    End If
NEXT_APPNAME:
    'try for next filename
    strFileName = Dir()
  Loop
  'On Error Resume Next
  
continueTerminateDay:

  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  
  Exit Function
  
ADD_APPNAME:
  On Error GoTo -1
  rs.AddNew
  rs("appName") = Left(strFileName, Len(strFileName) - 7)
  OpenedApplications = Left(strFileName, Len(strFileName) - 7) & ", "
  rs.Update
  GoTo NEXT_APPNAME

End Function

Dim attach1 As String, attach2 As String
Dim i As Integer

  Set frm = Forms!frmCRHOME_terminateday.Form
  frm!status = "Urejam bazo ..."

  smtpServerPort = 465
  smtpAuthenticate = 1
  sendPassword = "ojv1ptuj2"
  smtpUseSSL = 1
  sendTo = "mittja.vidovic@gmail.com"
  ccTo = "dusan.sedej@siol.net"
  
  Subject = IIf(eMailSubject = "", "napaka pri zaklju ku: " & Date, eMailSubject)
  Body = eMailBody
  
  DoCmd.Close acForm, "frmCRHome"

Public Sub RefreshActiveForms()
'On Error Resume Next
Dim frmName As String, frm As Form

  frmName = "frmCRProductsAdd"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm!frmCR_TableProducts.Form.Refresh
  Else
    frmName = "frmCRTables"
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form.Refresh
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form.Refresh
    frm!frmCR_TableProducts.Form.Refresh
  End If
  
End Sub

'On Error Resume Next

  frmName = "frmCRProductsAdd"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm!frmCR_TableProducts.Form.Requery
  Else
    frmName = "frmCRTables"
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form.Requery
    frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form.Requery
    frm!frmCR_TableProducts.Form.Requery
  End If
  
End Sub

  Set frm = Forms.frmCRTables.Form

  frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form.Requery
  frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form.Requery
  frm!frmCRTables_Tables1.Form!invID = Null
  frm!frmCRTables_Tables2.Form!invID = Null
  frm!crInvoiceID = Null
  
End Sub

Public Function crInvoiceTitle(invType As Integer, deletedAt As Boolean) As String
'On Error Resume Next
Dim frm As Form

If deletedAt = True Then
  Select Case invType
    Case 0: crInvoiceTitle = "Brisan odpis  t.: "
    Case 1: crInvoiceTitle = "Brisana miza: "
    Case 2: crInvoiceTitle = "Brisan darilni bon  t.: "
  End Select
Else
  Select Case invType
    Case 0: crInvoiceTitle = "Odpis  t.: "
    Case 1: crInvoiceTitle = "Ra un  t.: "
    Case 2: crInvoiceTitle = "Darilni bon  t.: "
  End Select
End If
  
End Function

Public Function SendMail(authenticate As Boolean, sendUserName, sendUsing, smtpServer, smtpServerPort, smtpAuthenticate, sendPassword, smtpUseSSL, sendTo, ccTo, Subject, Body, Optional attachment1, Optional attachment2, Optional attachment3) As Boolean
On Error GoTo errMsg
'************************************************* **************************************************
' This Function Is Created in order to solve the Ms-Outlook problem which
' Prompt user’s confirmation during Batch e-mail process. The source of the problem is
' the Microsoft security Update
'************************************************* **************************************************

' test To Use CDO (Microsoft's Collaboration Data Objects ) instead of outlook Object

Dim imsg, iconf
Set imsg = CreateObject("CDO.Message")
Set iconf = CreateObject("CDO.Configuration")

  SendMail = True
  
  If Not IsMissing(attachment1) Then ' Include attachment if applicable.
    imsg.AddAttachment attachment1
  End If
  If Not IsMissing(attachment2) Then
    imsg.AddAttachment attachment2
  End If
  If Not IsMissing(attachment3) Then
    imsg.AddAttachment attachment3
  End If
  
  'imsg.from = """GALAXY - Janez"" <mitja@xyz.si>"
  'imsg.ReplyTo = sendUserName


  imsg.FROM = sendUserName
  imsg.To = sendTo
  If ccTo <> "" Then imsg.CC = ccTo
  imsg.Subject = Subject
  imsg.TextBody = Body

  If authenticate Then
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusername") = sendUserName
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = sendUsing
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = smtpServer
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = smtpServerPort
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = smtpAuthenticate
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendpassword") = sendPassword
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpusessl") = smtpUseSSL
  Else
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/sendusing") = 2
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserver") = "smtp.amis.net"
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpserverport") = 25
    iconf.fields.Item("http://schemas.microsoft.com/cdo/configuration/smtpauthenticate") = 0
  End If
  
  iconf.fields.Update
  Set imsg.Configuration = iconf

  imsg.send

  Exit Function

errMsg:
  SendMail = False
  MsgBox "Napaka pri po iljanju sporo ila " & Subject & " / " & sendTo & "." & vbCrLf & _
           "Error : (" & err.Number & ") " & err.description, vbInformation
  
End Function

End Function

Public Function DailyTimeClockAutoRemark(remark, jobStart, jobEnd)
Dim autoRemark

  If Not IsNull(remark) Then autoRemark = "opomba; "
  If IsNull(jobEnd) Then
    autoRemark = autoRemark & "odhod; "
  Else
    If 24 * (jobEnd - jobStart) < 1 Then
      autoRemark = autoRemark & "nizke ure; "
    Else
      If 24 * (jobEnd - jobStart) > 12 Then autoRemark = autoRemark & "visoke ure; "
    End If
  End If
  
  If Not IsEmpty(autoRemark) Then
    DailyTimeClockAutoRemark = Left(autoRemark, Len(autoRemark) - 2)
  End If
  
End Function

Public Sub AddCmpyGrpWageCalculation()
Dim pmt01 As Long, pmt02 As Long, pmt03 As String, pmt04 As String

  pmt01 = fwcMnthID()
  pmt02 = currentCompanyGroupID()
  pmt03 = SQLDate(DLookup("monthFirstDate", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalMonthID=" & pmt01))
  pmt04 = SQLDate(DLookup("monthLastDate", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalMonthID=" & pmt01))

  Call CreateQry1(309, pmt01, pmt02)                        'doda novi wage calcID
  Call RunMySQL

  Call CreateQry1(310, pmt01, pmt02)                        'doda novi payrollCalcID
  Call RunMySQL

  Call CreateQry1(311, pmt01, pmt02, pmt03, pmt04)          'doda (manjkajo e) zaposlene (employeeID) v wageCalcSubID
  Call RunMySQL

  Call CreateQry1(312, pmt01, pmt02, pmt03, pmt04)          'doda (manjkajo e) zaposlitve (employmentID) v wageCalcSubSubID
  Call RunMySQL
  
  Call CreateQry1(317, pmt01, pmt02)                        'spremeni  t. delovnih ur v mesecu v wageCalcSubID glede na za etek/konec zaposlitve
  Call RunMySQL

  Beep
  
End Sub

Public Function PersonalIncomeTax(taxBase As Double, payoutYear As Long) As Double
Dim f As Form, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  PersonalIncomeTax = 0
  
  If taxBase <= 0 Then Exit Function
  
  strSQL = "SELECT tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates.*, Round([taxRate]*([upperBound]-[lowerBound]),2) AS taxAmount " & _
           "FROM tblEmpWC_WageTaxes INNER JOIN tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates ON tblEmpWC_WageTaxes.wageTaxID = tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates.wageTaxID " & _
           "WHERE(((tblEmpWC_WageTaxes.isPersonalIncomeTax) = True) And ((tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates.fromYear) <= " & payoutYear & ") And " & _
           "((tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates.toYear) >=" & payoutYear & " Or (tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates.toYear) Is Null)) " & _
           "ORDER BY tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates.lowerBound;"

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  rs.MoveFirst
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    If taxBase > rs("upperBound") Then
      PersonalIncomeTax = PersonalIncomeTax + rs("taxAmount")
    Else
      PersonalIncomeTax = Round(PersonalIncomeTax + rs("taxRate") * (taxBase - rs("lowerBound")), 2)
      Exit Function
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
End Function

Public Function generalTaxRelief(earning As Double, payoutYear As Integer) As Double
Dim f As Form, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  strSQL = "SELECT upperBound, reliefAmount, n, k " & _
           "FROM tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefs INNER JOIN tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts ON tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefs.wageTaxReliefID = tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.wageTaxReliefID " & _
           "WHERE(((tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefs.isGeneralTaxRelief) <> 0) And " & _
           "((tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.fromYear) <= " & payoutYear & " Or (tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.fromYear) Is Null) And " & _
           "((tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.toYear) >= " & payoutYear & " Or (tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.toYear) Is Null)) " & _
           "ORDER BY tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.lowerBound;"
           
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  rs.MoveFirst
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    If earning < rs("upperBound") Then
      generalTaxRelief = rs("reliefAmount") + rs("n") + earning * rs("k")
      Exit Function
    Else
      generalTaxRelief = rs("reliefAmount")
      Exit Function
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop

End Function

Public Function youngTaxRelief(earning As Double, payoutYear As Integer) As Double
Dim f As Form, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  strSQL = "SELECT tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.reliefAmount " & _
           "FROM tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefs INNER JOIN tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts ON tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefs.wageTaxReliefID = tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefAmounts.wageTaxReliefID " & _
           "WHERE(tblEmpWC_WageTaxReliefs.age=29);"
           
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  rs.MoveFirst

End Function

Public Sub EmployeeMonthlyEarnings(earningCalcExists As Boolean, wcID As Long, mnth As Integer)
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim frm As Form, resp As Integer, strSQL As String
Dim pmt01 As Long, pmt02 As Long, pmt03 As String, pmt04 As String, strMissingEmployees
  
  Set frm = Forms("frmEmpWC")
  If DCount("*", "qryEmpWC_checkEmployeeData") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    DoCmd.OpenForm "frmEmpWC_checkEmployeeData", , , , , acDialog
  End If
  
  If earningCalcExists Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Pozor! Ali  eli  izbrisati vse podatke? " & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
    "YES - vsi podatki bodo izbrisani, tudi tisti ozna eni z 'Ne bri i'" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
    "NO - izbrisani bodo vsi podatki razen ozna enih z 'Ne bri i'" & vbCrLf & vbCrLf & _
    "CANCEL - izvedba programa bo prekinjena", vbCritical + vbYesNoCancel)
  Else
    resp = vbYes
  End If
  
  If resp = vbCancel Then End
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  If resp = vbYes Then
    Call CreateQry1(57, wcID)                                                                                       'pobri e vse obstoje e podatke iz tabele tblEmpWC_Earnings
  Else
    Call CreateQry1(58, wcID)                                                                                       'pobri e obstoje e podatke iz tabele tblEmpWC_Earnings, razen tistih, ki so ozna eni z ne bri i"
  End If
  
  Call RunMySQL
  
  pmt01 = fwcMnthID()
  pmt02 = currentCompanyGroupID()
  pmt03 = SQLDate(DLookup("monthFirstDate", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalMonthID=" & pmt01))
  pmt04 = SQLDate(DLookup("monthLastDate", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalMonthID=" & pmt01))

  Call CreateQry1(316, pmt01, pmt02, pmt03, pmt04)                                                                  'qryEmpWC_EmployeesNotAdded
  Call RunMySQL
  
  strMissingEmployees = DLookup("missingEmployees", "qryEmpWC_EmployeesNotAdded")
  If Not IsNull(strMissingEmployees) Then
    resp = MsgBox("Na obra unu manjkajo delavci: " & strMissingEmployees & "! Ali naj nadaljujem z obra unom pla ?", vbYesNo + vbInformation)
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  End If
    
  Call CreateQry1(64, wcID)                                                                                         ' bolni ke, dopusti, ...
  Call RunMySQL
  
  Call CreateQry1(318, wcID, Mid(pmt03, 1, 4), Mid(pmt03, 6, 2))                                                    ' zaslu ek  igosanih
  Call RunMySQL
    
  '****** OLD *******
  Call CreateQry1(253, frm!wageCalculationID)                                                                       ' zaslu ek delavcev, ki se ne  igosajo ...
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_Earnings_NotTCUsers_Salary"
  
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_Earnings_NotTCUsers_Costs"                                                             ' prehrana, prevoz za delavce, ki se ne  igosajo
  
  If DCount("*", "qryEmpWC_Earnings_HA_check1") <> 0 And DCount("*", "qryEmpWC_Earnings_HA_check2") <> 0 Then       ' regres
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_Earnings_HA"
  End If
  '****** OLD *******
  
  Call CreateQry1(297, frm!wageCalculationID)                                                                       ' obvezni zdr. prispevek ...
  Call RunMySQL
  
  strSQL = "UPDATE tblEmpWC_Earnings SET appliesToYear = " & currentFiscalYear() & " WHERE appliesToYear Is Null;"
  Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  Beep
  Exit Sub
  
err_exit:
  Resume Next
  MsgBox "Napaka: (" & err.Number & ") " & err.description
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
End Sub

Public Sub PayrollEarnings(frm As Form, pcID As Long, isHolidayAllowance As Integer)  'dolo itev postavk pla ilnih list
'*** pripravi dohodke delavca (postavke pla ilne liste v skladu s KP)
Dim strSQL As String, wageTypeID As Long, wcID As Long

  wcID = fwcWcID()
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
 ' Call CreateQry1(254, pcID)

Public Sub wcHours()
Dim strSQL As String, wcID As Long

   wcID = fwcWcID()

   strSQL = "DELETE tblEmpWC_wcHours FROM  " & _
            "tblEmpWC_wcHours INNER JOIN tblEmpWC_WageCalculationSubs ON  " & _
            "tblEmpWC_WageCalculationSubs.wageCalculationSubID = tblEmpWC_wcHours.wageCalculationSubID " & _
            "WHERE (tblEmpWC_WageCalculationSubs.wageCalculationID=" & wcID & ");"
   Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
   
   Call CreateQry1(286, wcID, fwcMnthID())               'odsotnost
   Call RunMySQL
   Call CreateQry1(287, wcID)                            'URE ne igosani
   Call RunMySQL
   Call CreateQry1(288, wcID, fwcMnthID())               'prazniki ne igosani
   Call RunMySQL
   
   DoCmd.SetWarnings False
   DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdEmpWC_wcHours_Absence"           'pobri e prisotnost on absence day za ne igosane
   DoCmd.OpenQuery "qdelEmpWC_wcHours_Absence"
   DoCmd.SetWarnings True
   
   Call CreateQry1(285, wcID)                            'URE  igosani
   Call RunMySQL
  
'   DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdEmpWC_wcHours_breakStart"        ' doda odmor med delom
'   DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdEmpWC_wcHours_breakEnd"

End Sub

Public Sub payrollTaxes(isHolidayAllowance As Boolean)
Dim sumWageAndHAforTaxBase As Integer

  '*** izra unaj davke
  sumWageAndHAforTaxBase = DLookup("sumWageHAforTaxBase", "tblEmpWC_WageCalculations", "wageCalculationID=fwcwcID()")
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False

  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qdelEmpWC_PayrollTaxes00_DeleteTaxes"                           'prispevki
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qdelEmpWC_PayrollTaxes00_DeleteReliefs"                         'olaj ave
 
  If isHolidayAllowance <> 0 Then
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_PayrollTaxes03_HolidayAllowanceTax_0"
  Else
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_PayrollTaxes01_FlatRateTaxes"                     'prispevki
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_PayrollTaxes02_WageIncomeTax_ReliefsOther"        'ostale olaj ave (otroci)
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_PayrollTaxes02_WageIncomeTax_ReliefsGeneral"      'splo na olaj ava
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_PayrollTaxes02_WageIncomeTax_ReliefsYoung"        'splo na olaj ava
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappEmpWC_PayrollTaxes02_WageIncomeTax_Tax"                 'dohodnina pla a
  End If
 
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  Beep

End Sub

Function CreateREK1xml(frm As Form)
Dim newLine As String, companyID As Long, pDay As Date, payrolCalcID As Long

Dim qdf As QueryDef, strXML As String
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset
Dim strIncomeType As String

  newLine = chr(13) & chr(10)
  companyID = fwcCmpyID()
  pDay = frm!frmEmpWC_PayrollCalculations.Form!payDate
  payrolCalcID = fwcPcID()
  Yr = currentFiscalYear()
  yrH = DSum("mnthHours", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalYear=" & Yr)
  
  Call CreateQry(184, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(185, , , , , payrolCalcID)

  Call CreateQry(188, , , , , payrolCalcID)

  Call CreateQry(191, , , , , payrolCalcID)

    rs.Close

  End If

  strXML = "<?xml version = '1.0' encoding='UTF-8'?>" & newLine
  strXML = strXML & "<Envelope xmlns='http://edavki.durs.si/Documents/Schemas/REK_1_6.xsd' " & _
                                "xmlns:edp='http://edavki.durs.si/Documents/Schemas/EDP-Common-1.xsd' " & _

    strXML = strXML & "</edp:Header>" & newLine
    strXML = strXML & "<edp:Signatures/>" & newLine
    
    strXML = strXML & "<body>" & newLine
    
      strXML = strXML & "<edp:bodyContent/>" & newLine
        strXML = strXML & "<REK>" & newLine
          strXML = strXML & "<REK_1>" & newLine
            strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("DocumentTypeID", "O")

                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A081S1", (rs("A081S1")))
                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A081S2", (rs("A081S2")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A081O", (rs("A081")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A081P", (rs("A081")))
                
                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A082S1", (rs("A082S")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A082O", (rs("A082")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A082P", (rs("A082")))
        
                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A083S", (rs("A083S")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A083O", (rs("A083")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A083P", (rs("A083")))
                
                
                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A084S", (rs("A084S")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A084O", (rs("A084")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A084P", (rs("A084")))
                
                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A085S", (rs("A085S")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A085O", (rs("A085")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A085P", (rs("A085")))
                
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A086O", XMLNumber(n(rs("A081")) + n(rs("A082")) + n(rs("A083")) + n(rs("A084")) + n(rs("A085"))))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A086P", XMLNumber(n(rs("A081")) + n(rs("A082")) + n(rs("A083")) + n(rs("A084")) + n(rs("A085"))))
                
                'dav ni odtegljaj
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A091O", (rs("A091")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A091P", (rs("A091")))
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A092O", "0.00")              'pla ano v tujini
                
                'olaj ave za vzdr evane dru inske  lane
                Set qdf = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qryEmpWC_REK1aChildSupport")
                'qdf("pcID") = (frm!payrollCalculationID)
                qdf("empID") = rs("employeeID")
                Set rs1 = qdf.OpenRecordset()
            
                If rs1.RecordCount > 0 Then
                  rs1.MoveFirst
                    Do While Not rs1.EOF
                      strXML = strXML & "<podo:ChildSupport>" & newLine
                        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:V01L", rs1("V01L"))
                        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:V01R", rs1("V01R"))
                        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:V01Z", XMLNumber(rs1("V01Z")))
                      strXML = strXML & "</podo:ChildSupport>" & newLine
                      rs1.MoveNext
                    Loop
                End If
                rs1.Close
                
                'prispevki za zav. dobo z pove anjem
                'If rs("A101S") <> 0 Then 'Or IsNull(rs("A101S")) Then
                '  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A101S", XMLNumber(rs("A101S")))
                '  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A101TP", rs("A101TP"))
                '  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A101OP", XMLNumber(rs("A101OP")))
                '  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A101O", XMLNumber(rs("A101O")))
                '  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:A101P", XMLNumber(rs("A101P")))
                'End If
                
                strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:D01Z", (rs("A052")))
                'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:D02", 1)
                    
                If Not frm!frmEmpWC_PayrollCalculations.Form!isHolidayAllowance Then
                    
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B01U", rs("B01h")) ' pla a ure
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B01Z", rs("B01e")) ' pla a znesek
                    
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B02U", rs("B02h"))  ' nadomestilo pla e ure
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B02Z", rs("B02e"))  ' nadomestilo pla e znesek
                    
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B04Z", rs("B04"))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B05Z", rs("B05"))
                    
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B06Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B07Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B08Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B09Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B09aZ", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B010Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B011Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B012Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B013Z", XMLNumber"0.00")
                    'strXML = strXML & "<podo:B014>" & newLine
                    'strXML = strXML & "<podo:B014R> </podo:B014R>" & newLine
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B014Z", XMLNumber("0,00"))
                    'strXML = strXML & "</podo:B014>" & newLine
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B015Z", XMLNumber("0,00"))
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B016Z", XMLNumber("0,00"))
                    'strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:B017Z", XMLNumber("0,00"))
                End If
           
               'podatki za obrazce M
               'M01 PLA A IN NADOMESTILA PLA E (razen bolni ke)
                If rs("M01h") <> "0" Then
                    strXML = strXML & "<podo:M01>" & newLine
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M01U", rs("M01h"))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M01Z", (rs("M01")))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M01OD", rs("M01OD"))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M01DO", rs("M01DO"))
                    strXML = strXML & "</podo:M01>" & newLine
                End If
                
                'M02 NADOMESTILO PLA E: bolni ke
                If rs("M02h") <> "0" Then
                    strXML = strXML & "<podo:M02>" & newLine
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M02U", rs("M02h"))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M02Z", rs("M02"))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M02LO", Yr - 1)
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M02OD", rs("M01OD"))
                    strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M02DO", rs("M01DO"))
                    strXML = strXML & "</podo:M02>" & newLine
                End If
                
                If rs("M03h") <> 0 Then
                'M03 PLA A za nadure
                  strXML = strXML & "<podo:M03>" & newLine
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M03U", rs("M03h"))
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M03Z", (rs("M03")))
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M03OD", rs("M01OD"))
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M03DO", rs("M01DO"))
                  strXML = strXML & "</podo:M03>" & newLine
                End If
                
                'M06 LETNI SKLAD UR
                strXML = strXML & "<podo:M06>" & newLine
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M06U", yrH)
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M06OD", rs("M01OD"))
                  strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("podo:M06DO", rs("M01DO"))
                strXML = strXML & "</podo:M06>" & newLine
                
              strXML = strXML & "</podo:AnalyticalDataItem>" & newLine
              rs.MoveNext
            Loop

            strXML = strXML & "</podo:AnalyticalData>" & newLine

        strXML = strXML & "</REK>" & newLine
      strXML = strXML & "</body>" & newLine
    strXML = strXML & "</Envelope>" & newLine
    
    'Napi e xml datoteko
    
    strIncomeType = "c:\Companies\Applications\XMLFiles\REK\" & GetCmpyData("cmpyShortName1", pDay, companyID) & "_" & strIncomeType & ".xml"
    
    Open strIncomeType For Output As #1
    
    Print #1, strXML
    Close #1

End Function

Function CreateREKOxml(frm As Form)
Dim newLine As String, companyID As Long, pDay As Date, payrolCalcID As Long
Dim Yr As Integer, yrH As Integer
Dim qdf As QueryDef, strXML As String
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset
Dim strIncomeType As String
Dim maticnaSt As String
Dim prispDelavca As Double
Dim mm As String

  newLine = chr(13) & chr(10)
  companyID = fwcCmpyID()
  pDay = frm!frmEmpWC_PayrollCalculations.Form!payDate
  payrolCalcID = fwcPcID()
  Yr = currentFiscalYear()
  yrH = DSum("mnthHours", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalYear=" & Yr)
  maticnaSt = DFirst("cmpyRegistrationNumber", "tblCmpy_RegistrationData", "companyID=" & companyID)
  
  Call CreateQry(184, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(185, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(186, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(187, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(188, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(189, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(190, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry(191, , , , , payrolCalcID)
  
  'Call CreateQry(59, fwcWcID(), , , , DSum("mnthHours", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "fiscalYear=" & currentFiscalYear()))
  Call CreateQry1(240, payrolCalcID)
  Call CreateQry1(241, payrolCalcID)
  
  Set qdf = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qryEmpWC_REK_O")
  Set rs = qdf.OpenRecordset()
  
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then
    rs.Close
    MsgBox "Kreiranje XML datoteke ni mo no - podatki ne obstajajo!"
    Exit Function
  End If

  mm = Left(rs("F011Start"), 2)
  
  strXML = "<?xml version = '1.0' encoding='UTF-8'?>" & newLine
  strXML = strXML & "<Envelope xmlns='http://edavki.durs.si/Documents/Schemas/REK_O_1.xsd' " & _
                               "xmlns:edp='http://edavki.durs.si/Documents/Schemas/EDP-Common-1.xsd' " & _
                               "xmlns:podo='http://edavki.durs.si/Documents/Schemas/REK_O_Analytical_1.xsd'>"

      strXML = strXML & "<edp:taxpayer xmlns:ner='http://tempuri.org/Ds_NER_1.xsd' xmlns:ds_odip='http://tempuri.org/Ds_ODIP_1.xsd'>" & newLine
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:taxNumber", Right(GetCmpyData("cmpyTaxNumber", pDay, companyID), 8))
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:taxpayerType", GetCmpyData("companyType", pDay, companyID))
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:name", GetCmpyData("cmpyShortName1", pDay, companyID))
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:address1", GetCmpyData("cmpyStreet", pDay, companyID))
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:city", GetCmpyData("cmpyPlace", pDay, companyID))
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:invalidskoPodjetje", 0)
        strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("edp:activityCode", GetCmpyData("activityCode", pDay, companyID))
      'strxml = strxml & "   "; htmlline("edp:address1", rs("F006_Address"))& newline
      strXML = strXML & "</edp:taxpayer>" & newLine
    strXML = strXML & "</edp:Header>" & newLine
    strXML = strXML & "<edp:Signatures/>" & newLine
    
    strXML = strXML & "<body>" & newLine
    
      strXML = strXML & "<edp:bodyContent/>" & newLine
        strXML = strXML & "<REK>" & newLine
          strXML = strXML & "<REK_O>" & newLine
            'Vrsta dokumenta
            strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("DocumentType", "O")
            strXML = strXML & HTMLLine("DocumentTypeName", "Original")

Public Function GetTaxRate(REKCode As String) As String
Dim wageTaxID As String
Dim strWhere As String

  strWhere = "REKCode='" & REKCode & "'"
  wageTaxID = DLookup("(wageTaxID)", "tblEmpWC_WageTaxes", strWhere)
  GetTaxRate = XMLNumber(100 * DFirst("taxRate", "tblEmpWC_WageTaxRates", "wageTaxID=" & wageTaxID))

End Function

Public Function GetTaxIBANRef(REKCode As String, DT As Date, companyID As Long)
Dim strWhere As String, bpID As String, bpIBAN As String, bpRef, bpRefInf
  
  strWhere = "REKCode='" & REKCode & "'"
  bpID = DLookup("(partnerID)", "tblEmpWC_WageTaxes", strWhere)
  bpIBAN = DLookup("IBAN", "tblPtr_Partners", "(partnerID)=" & bpID)
  bpRef = DLookup("bpRef", "tblEmpWC_WageTaxes", strWhere)
  bpRefInf = DLookup("bpRefInformation", "tblEmpWC_WageTaxes", strWhere)
  GetTaxIBANRef = bpIBAN & " " & bpRef & " " & companyTaxNumber(companyID) & bpRefInf

End Function

Public Function employmentDuration(D1 As Date, D2, Y) As Double
  
  If Year(D1) < Y Then
    D1 = CDate("01.01." & Y)
  End If
  
  
  If IsNull(D2) Then
    D2 = CDate("31.12." & Y)
  Else
    If Y < Year(D2) Then D2 = CDate("31.12." & Y)
  End If
    
  employmentDuration = ((D2 - D1 + 1) / 365)

End Function

Public Function PastServiceYears(D1, pastService, Yr) As Long
  
  PastServiceYears = Int((CDate("31.12." & Yr) - D1 + pastService) / 365)

End Function

Public Sub CreateEmployeesPasswords()
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  strSQL = "SELECT tblPtr_Partners.password " & _
           "FROM tblPtr_Partners INNER JOIN tblPtr_PartnersCmpys ON tblPtr_Partners.partnerID = tblPtr_PartnersCmpys.partnerID " & _
           "WHERE (((tblPtr_Partners.password) Is Null) AND ((tblPtr_PartnersCmpys.isEmployee)=1)); "

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do Until rs.EOF
    rs.Edit
    rs("password") = Format$(Int(100000 * Rnd()), "00000")
    rs.Update
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
End Sub

Public Function BusinessTripSubsistence(btID As Long) As Double
Dim amount

  amount = DSum("[noOfDays]*[subsistenceRate]*[subsistenceShare]", "tblEmpBT_BusinessTripSubsistences", "businessTripID=" & btID)
  
  If IsNull(amount) Then
    BusinessTripSubsistence = 0
  Else
    BusinessTripSubsistence = Round(amount, 2)
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function BusinessTripMileage(btID As Long) As Double
Dim amount
  
  amount = DSum("[distance]*[mileageRate]", "tblEmpBT_BusinessTripMileages", "businessTripID=" & btID)
  
  If IsNull(amount) Then
    BusinessTripMileage = 0
  Else
    BusinessTripMileage = Round(amount, 2)
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function BusinessTripCost(btID As Long) As Double
Dim amount
  
  amount = DSum("iif(exchangeRate=0,0,round(costInCurrency/exchangeRate,2))", "tblEmpBT_BusinessTripCosts", "businessTripID=" & btID)
  
  If IsNull(amount) Then
    BusinessTripCost = 0
  Else
    BusinessTripCost = Round(amount, 2)
  End If
    
End Function

Public Sub RequeryEmployeeReports(frm As Form)

  If (Not IsNull(frm!employeeID)) And (Not IsNull(frm!fiscalMonth)) And (Not IsNull(frm!fiscalYear)) Then
    Call CreateQry(31, frm!employeeID, frm!fiscalMonth, frm!fiscalYear)
    Call CreateQry(32, frm!employeeID, frm!fiscalMonth, frm!fiscalYear)
    Call CreateQry(33, frm!employeeID, frm!fiscalMonth, frm!fiscalYear, , frm!employeeID)
    Call CreateQry(34, frm!employeeID, frm!fiscalMonth, frm!fiscalYear)
    Call CreateQry(76, frm!employeeID, frm!fiscalYear)
    Call CreateQry1(308, frm!employeeID, frm!fiscalYear)
    
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Timeclock.Report.RecordSource = "qryEmpRpts_Timeclock"
    frm!rptEmpRpts_TimeclockTotals.Report.Requery
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence.Report.RecordSource = "qryEmpRpts_Absence"
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence1.Report.RecordSource = "qryEmpRpts_Absence1"
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Holidays.Report.Requery
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Revenue.Report.RecordSource = "qryEmpRpts_Revenue"
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Revenue!rptEmpRpts_RevenueSum.Report.RecordSource = "qryEmpRpts_RevenueSum"
    
    frm!Line1.visible = True
    frm!Line2.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Timeclock.Report.ReportHeader.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Timeclock.Report.Detail.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Timeclock.Report.ReportFooter.visible = True
    
    frm!rptEmpRpts_TimeclockTotals.Report.Detail.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_TimeclockTotals.Report.ReportFooter.visible = True
    
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence.Report.sequenceHeader.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence.Report.Detail.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence.Report.sequenceFooter.visible = True
    
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence1.Report.ReportHeader.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Absence1.Report.Detail.visible = True

    frm!rptEmpRpts_Holidays.Report.ReportHeader.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Holidays.Report.Detail.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Holidays.Report.ReportFooter.visible = True

    frm!rptEmpRpts_Revenue.Report.ReportHeader.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Revenue.Report.Detail.visible = True
    frm!rptEmpRpts_Revenue!rptEmpRpts_RevenueSum.Report.Detail.visible = True
  Else
    Beep
    MsgBox "Napaka manjka delavec, leto ali mesec", vbInformation
    End
  End If


End Sub

Option Compare Database

Sub HarmonizeCatalogues()
  
  If DCount("*", "qryGGCat_CheckVatRates") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "V Gagarinu se je pojavila nova stopnja DDV! Stopnje DDV je potrebno uskladiti ro no. Ne morem nadaljevati!", vbInformation
    End
  End If

  If DCount("*", "qryGGCat_CheckCostCenters") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "V Gagarinu se je pojavilo novo stro kovno mesto! Stro kovno mesto je potrebno uskladiti ro no. Ne morem nadaljevati!", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  If DCount("*", "qryGGCat_CheckMeansOfPayments") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "V Gagarinu se je pojavilo novo pla ilno sredstvo! Pla ilna sredstva je potrebno uskladiti ro no. Ne morem nadaljevati!", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappGGCat_ImportBPs_1")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappGGCat_ImportBPs_2")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qupdGGCat_ChangedBPs")
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  Call CreateQry(89, , , , , 1)
  Call RunMySQL
  Beep
  
End Sub

Sub ImportGagarinSalesInvoices(frm As Form)
Dim db As DAO.Database, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, ID As Long, i As Integer

  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  frm!status = "Uva am ra une ..."
  frm.Repaint
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qupdSalesGGInv_ImportStart_Invoices")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappSalesGGInv_Import_Invoices")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappSalesGGInv_ImportDocVats")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappSalesGGInv_ImportPaymentTerms")
  'DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappSalesGGInv_ImportInstalments")
  
  frm!status = "Uva am predra une ..."
  frm.Repaint
  
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qupdSalesGGInv_ImportStart_ProFormas")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappSalesGGInv_Import_ProFormas")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qdelSalesGGInv_Import_DeleteProFormas"

  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qupdSalesGGInv_ImportEnd")
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  Set db = CurrentDb                                    'paymentTerms sequence
  strSQL = "SELECT ggInvoiceID, sequence, dueDate " & _
           "From tblSalesGG_InvoicePaymentTerms " & _
           "WHERE sequence Is Null " & _
           "ORDER BY ggInvoiceID, dueDate;"
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Sub
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  ID = rs("ggInvoiceID")
  i = 0
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
  
    If rs("ggInvoiceID") = ID Then
      i = i + 1
    Else
      ID = rs("ggInvoiceID")
      i = 1
    End If
    
    rs.Edit
    rs("sequence") = i
    rs.Update
    
    rs.MoveNext
      
  Loop
  
  'instalments sequence
  
  'Call InstalmentSequence
  
End Sub

Sub ImportGagarinPurchaseInvoices()

  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qupdPurchaseInvGG_Import_Start")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappPurchaseInvGG_Import_Invoices")
  DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qappPurchaseInvGG_Import_InvoiceDetails")
  CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qupdPurchaseInvGG_Import_End").Execute
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
End Sub

Public Sub RefreshPaymentAssignements()
'On Error Resume Next
Dim db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As DAO.Recordset
Dim frm As Form, frm1 As Form
  
  Call RunSQL("DELETE * FROM tblTempRecordsToBeDeleted;")
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsUpdate"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsDelete"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblTempRecordsToBeDeleted")
  Set rs1 = db.OpenRecordset("tblAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignements", dbOpenDynaset)
  
  If rs.RecordCount <> 0 Then
    
    rs.MoveFirst
    Do While Not rs.EOF
      rs1.FindFirst "ggPaymentAssignementID=" & rs("id_integer")
      rs1.delete
      rs.MoveNext
    Loop
  
  End If
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsAdd"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  Set frm = Forms!frmAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignements.Form
  If IsNull(frm!ggPaymentAssignementID) Then
    frm.Requery
  Else
    Call StayOnRecordRequery(frm, "ggPaymentAssignementID", frm!ggPaymentAssignementID)
  End If
  
  Set frm1 = Forms!frmAccPmtJournals!frmAccPmtJournals_Journals.Form
  frm.Caption = "Razporejanje pla il (" & frm1!paymentJournalNumber & " - " & frm1!paymentJournalDate & ")"
  Beep
  
End Sub

Public Sub ExportPaymentsToGG(documentTypeID As Integer)

  If DCount("*", "qryAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsDuplicates") <> 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Izvoz ni mo en, ker obstajajo podvojeni zapisi!", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  If documentTypeID = 1 Then
    DoCmd.SetWarnings False
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsExportGGProFormas"
    DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  Else
    DoCmd.SetWarnings False
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsExportGGInvoices"
    DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccPmtGG_PaymentAssignementsExportGGInvoices"
    DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  End If
  Beep
  
End Sub

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Function GetTableFields(tableName As String) As String
Dim db As DAO.Database, td As DAO.TableDef, fld As Field

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set td = db.TableDefs(tableName)
  GetTableFields = ""
  
  For Each fld In td.fields
    
    If GetTableFields = "" Then
      GetTableFields = fld.Name
    Else
      GetTableFields = GetTableFields & "," & fld.Name
    End If
  Next

  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing

End Function

Function GetPrimaryKeyName(tableName As String) As String
Dim db As DAO.Database, td As DAO.TableDef
Dim Ind As DAO.index, i As Integer

Set db = CurrentDb
Set td = db.TableDefs(tableName)

GetPrimaryKeyName = ""

For i = 0 To td.Indexes.Count - 1
  Set Ind = td.Indexes(i)
  If Ind.Primary Then
    GetPrimaryKeyName = Mid(Ind.fields, 2)
    Exit For
  End If
Next i

db.Close
Set db = Nothing
Set td = Nothing
Set Ind = Nothing

End Function

Public Function TableExists(tblName As String) As Boolean
  If IsNull(DLookup("name", "MSysObjects", "Name='" & tblName & "'")) Then
    TableExists = False
  Else
    TableExists = True
  End If
End Function

Public Function CreatePrimaryKey(tableName As String)
Dim pkName As String, strSQL As String
Dim db As Database, qDef As QueryDef

  pkName = GetPrimaryKeyName(tableName)
  If Not pkName = "" Then
  
    strSQL = "ALTER TABLE " & tableName & " ADD PRIMARY KEY( " & pkName & ");"
    
    Set db = CurrentDb
    Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryMySQL_ActionSQL")
    qDef.SQL = strSQL
    qDef.Execute
    
    strSQL = "ALTER TABLE " & tableName & " CHANGE " & pkName & " " & pkName & " INT(11) NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT;"
    Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryMySQL_ActionSQL")
    qDef.SQL = strSQL
    qDef.Execute
    
    db.Close
    
  End If
  
End Function

Function CreateIndex(tableName As String) As String
On Error GoTo errMsg
Dim db As DAO.Database, td As DAO.TableDef, fld As Field, qDef As QueryDef, strSQL As String
Dim pkName As String

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set td = db.TableDefs(tableName)
  pkName = GetPrimaryKeyName(tableName)
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryMySQL_ActionSQL")
  
  For Each fld In td.fields
    
     If fld.Type = 1 Or fld.Type = 8 Or fld.Type = 7 Or fld.Type = 20 Then
      strSQL = "ALTER TABLE " & td.Name & " CHANGE " & fld.Name & " " & fld.Name
       If fld.Type = 1 Then
         If fld.DefaultValue = "no" Then
           strSQL = strSQL & " BIT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;"
         Else
           strSQL = strSQL & " BIT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;"
         End If
       End If
       If fld.Type = 7 Or fld.Type = 20 Then strSQL = strSQL & " DECIMAL(18,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;"
       If fld.Type = 8 Then strSQL = strSQL & " DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP;"
       
       qDef.SQL = strSQL
       qDef.Execute
    End If
    
    If (fld.Name <> pkName) And (StrComp(Right(fld.Name, 2), "ID", 0) = 0) Then
      strSQL = "ALTER TABLE " & td.Name & " ADD INDEX(" & fld.Name & ");"
      qDef.SQL = strSQL
      qDef.Execute
    End If
    
  Next

  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing
  Beep
  
errExit:
  Exit Function
    
errMsg:
  CreateIndex = tableName & " " & err.description
  MsgBox CreateIndex
  Resume Next
End Function

Function FindBitField(tableName As String) As String
On Error GoTo errMsg
Dim db As DAO.Database, td As DAO.TableDef, fld As Field, qDef As QueryDef, strSQL As String
Dim pkName As String

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set td = db.TableDefs(tableName)
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryMySQL_ActionSQL")
  
  For Each fld In td.fields

       If fld.Type = 1 Then
       
         strSQL = "UPDATE " & td.Name & " SET " & fld.Name & " = -1 WHERE " & fld.Name & "=1;"
         qDef.SQL = strSQL
         qDef.Execute

         strSQL = "ALTER TABLE " & td.Name & " CHANGE " & fld.Name & " " & fld.Name
         
         If fld.DefaultValue = "Yes" Then
           strSQL = strSQL & " TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT -1;"
         Else
           strSQL = strSQL & " TINYINT(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;"
         End If
         
         qDef.SQL = strSQL
         qDef.Execute

       End If
    
  Next

  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing
  Beep
  
errExit:
  Exit Function
    
errMsg:
  FindBitField = tableName & " " & err.description
  MsgBox FindBitField
  Resume Next
End Function

Function CurrentTS(tableName As String) As String
On Error GoTo errMsg
Dim db As DAO.Database, td As DAO.TableDef, fld As Field, qDef As QueryDef, strSQL As String
Dim pkName As String

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set td = db.TableDefs(tableName)
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryMySQL_ActionSQL")
  
  For Each fld In td.fields

 
       If fld.Type = 8 And fld.Name <> "createdAt" Then
  
         strSQL = "ALTER TABLE " & td.Name & " CHANGE " & fld.Name & " " & fld.Name & " DATETIME NULL;"
         qDef.SQL = strSQL
         qDef.Execute

       End If
    
  Next

  db.Close
  Set db = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing
  Set td = Nothing
  Beep
  
errExit:
  Exit Function
    
errMsg:
  CurrentTS = tableName & " " & err.description
  MsgBox CurrentTS
  Resume Next
End Function

Function PrintRelationships()
Dim rel As Relation, fld As Field

    For Each rel In CurrentDb.Relations
        With rel
            Debug.Print "Name: " & .Name
            Debug.Print "Attributes: " & .Attributes
            Debug.Print "Table: " & .Table
            Debug.Print "ForeignTable: " & .ForeignTable

            Debug.Print "Fields:"
            For Each fld In .fields
                Debug.Print "Field: " & fld.Name
            Next
        End With
    Next
    
End Function

Public Sub CreateAppQry(tblName As String)
Dim strSQL As String, db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset
Dim qryName As String, strTableFields As String
Dim qLine1 As String, qLine2 As String, qLine3 As String
Dim qDef As QueryDef
    
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qappMySQL_addData")

    strTableFields = GetTableFields(tblName & "1")
    qLine1 = "INSERT INTO " & tblName & "(" & strTableFields & ")" & vbCrLf
    qLine2 = "SELECT " & strTableFields & vbCrLf
    qLine3 = "FROM " & tblName & "1;"
    strSQL = qLine1 & qLine2 & qLine3
    qDef.SQL = strSQL
    qDef.Execute
  
  db.Close
End Sub

Public Sub ExportData()
Dim qDef As QueryDef, db As Database, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  Set db = CurrentDb
  strSQL = "SELECT tableName FROM tblSysTables ORDER BY exportSequence;"
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Beep: Exit Sub
    
  rs.MoveFirst
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  Do Until rs.EOF
    
    DoCmd.OpenQuery ("qexp" & Mid(rs("tableName"), 4))
    rs.MoveNext
  
  Loop
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
    
  rs.Close
  db.Close
  Beep
  
End Sub

Function QueryExists(qryName As String) As Boolean
  On Error Resume Next
  QueryExists = False
  QueryExists = Not (CurrentDb.QueryDefs(qryName) Is Nothing)
End Function

Public Sub ChangeConnection()
On Error GoTo napaka
    Dim td As DAO.TableDef
    Dim db As DAO.Database
    Dim OldStr As String, NewStr As String
    Set db = CurrentDb()
    OldStr = "*poletje*"
    NewStr = "ODBC;DRIVER={MySQL ODBC 8.0 Unicode Driver};UID=mitja;PWD=Equinox23.;DFLT_BIGINT_BIND_STR=1;PORT=3306;DATABASE=MV;SERVER=10.8.0.1;"
    For Each td In db.TableDefs
        With td
            If .Connect Like "*poletje*" Then
            ' If .Name = "tblTempAccGL_Posting" Then
              ' Debug.Print .Connect
              OldStr = .Name
                .Connect = NewStr
                .RefreshLink
                GoTo OK
            End If
        End With
napaka:
  Resume Next
  Debug.Print OldStr
OK:
    Next td
End Sub

Public Sub OpenProductsReport(RName As String, Optional PageBreak As Boolean)
Dim rpt As Report

  DoCmd.OpenReport RName, acViewReport, , "isSelected=true"
  Set rpt = Screen.ActiveReport
  
  If RName = "rptProdCatProd_ProductRecipe" Then
    If PageBreak Then
      rpt.PoductID_Footer.ForceNewPage = 2
      rpt!rptProdCatProd_ProductRecipeWhereAmI.Report.visible = True
      rpt!rptProdCatProd_ProductRecipeUnits.Report.visible = True
    Else
      rpt!rptProdCatProd_ProductRecipeWhereAmI.Report.visible = False
      rpt!rptProdCatProd_ProductRecipeUnits.Report.visible = False
    End If
  End If

End Sub

Public Function NoOfUnitUses(unitID As Long, companyID As Long) As Integer
'funckija izra una kolikokrat je uporabljena dolo ena EM
Dim strSQL As String, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset

  NoOfUnitUses = 0
  
  strSQL = "SELECT Count(tblProdCat_ProductRecipeIngredients.recipeIngredientID) AS CountOfIngredient " & _
           "FROM tblProdCat_Products INNER JOIN tblProdCat_ProductRecipeIngredients ON tblProdCat_Products.productID = tblProdCat_ProductRecipeIngredients.productID  " & _
           "WHERE (((tblProdCat_ProductRecipeIngredients.unitID)= " & unitID & ") AND ((tblProdCat_Products.companyID)=" & companyID & "));"
  
  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  NoOfUnitUses = rs("CountOfIngredient")

  strSQL = "SELECT Count(tblProdCat_ProductUnits.unitID) AS CountOfproductUnitID " & _
           "FROM tblProdCat_Products INNER JOIN tblProdCat_ProductUnits ON tblProdCat_Products.productID = tblProdCat_ProductUnits.productID " & _
           "WHERE (((tblProdCat_ProductUnits.unitID)=" & unitID & ") AND ((tblProdCat_Products.companyID)=" & companyID & "));"

  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  NoOfUnitUses = NoOfUnitUses + rs("CountOfproductUnitID")

End Function


Public Sub OpenFormRecipe(prodID As Long, isSalesProduct As Boolean)
Dim f As Form

  DoCmd.OpenForm "frmProdCatProd_Recipe", acNormal, , "productID = " & prodID, acFormEdit
  
  Set f = Forms!frmProdCatProd_Recipe.Form
  f.Caption = "Recept: " & f!productName
  If isSalesProduct = True Then
    f!prLevel = CLng(100000)
    f!recipeLevel.visible = False
    f!cmdLevelUp.visible = False
    f!cmdLevelDown.visible = False
  Else
    f!prLevel = f!recipeLevel
    f!recipeLevel.visible = True
    f!cmdLevelUp.visible = True
    f!cmdLevelDown.visible = True
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Function CopyProductID(oldProdID As Long) As Long
Dim rs1 As DAO.Recordset, rs2 As DAO.Recordset, newProdID As String, qDef As QueryDef

  Set rs1 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblProdCat_Products")
  Set rs2 = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblProdCat_Products", dbOpenDynaset, dbSeeChanges)

  rs1.FindFirst "productID=" & oldProdID
  
  If rs1.NoMatch Then
    MsgBox "Napaka!"
    Exit Function
  End If
  
  rs2.AddNew
  rs2("productName") = rs1("productName") & " (KOPIJA)"
  rs2("costCenterProductName") = rs1("costCenterProductName")
  rs2("productGroupID") = rs1("productGroupID")
  rs2("recipeLevel") = rs1("recipeLevel")
  rs2("vatRateID") = rs1("vatRateID")
  rs2("preparationQty") = rs1("preparationQty")
  rs2("baseUnitID") = rs1("baseUnitID")
  rs2("allowedInventoryDeviation") = rs1("allowedInventoryDeviation")
  rs2("ownerDepartmentID") = rs1("ownerDepartmentID")
  rs2("orderDepartmentID") = rs1("orderDepartmentID")
  rs2("partnerID") = rs1("partnerID")
  rs2("baseUnitPurchasePrice") = rs1("baseUnitPurchasePrice")
  rs2("purchasePackaging") = rs1("purchasePackaging")
  rs2("noOfBaseUnitsInPackaging") = rs1("noOfBaseUnitsInPackaging")
  rs2("showOnInventoryList") = rs1("showOnInventoryList")
  rs2("calculateInventoryDeviation") = rs1("calculateInventoryDeviation")
  rs2("salesPrice") = rs1("salesPrice")
  rs2("vatIncluded") = rs1("vatIncluded")
  rs2("crMenuID") = rs1("crMenuID")
  rs2("crSequence") = rs1("crSequence")
  rs2("ticketID") = rs1("ticketID")
  rs2("isActive") = rs1("isActive")
  rs2("costCenterID") = rs1("costCenterID")
  rs2("companyID") = rs1("companyID")
  rs1.Close
  rs2.Update
  'rs2.Bookmark = rs2.LastModified
  newProdID = DMax("productID", "tblProdCat_Products")
  rs2.Close
  
  Set qDef = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qappProdCopy_ProductIngredients")
  qDef.Parameters("oldProdID") = oldProdID
  qDef.Parameters("newProdID") = newProdID
  qDef.Execute
  
  Set qDef = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qappProdCopy_ProductInstructions")
  qDef.Parameters("oldProdID") = oldProdID
  qDef.Parameters("newProdID") = newProdID
  qDef.Execute
    
  Set qDef = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qappProdCopy_ProductUnits")
  qDef.Parameters("oldProdID") = oldProdID
  qDef.Parameters("newProdID") = newProdID
  qDef.Execute

  CopyProductID = newProdID
  
  Beep
  
End Function

Public Sub FindRelatedProducts(productID As Long, deleteCurrentData As Boolean)
Dim strSQL As String, db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, maxLevel As Integer, i As Integer

  If deleteCurrentData Then Call RunSQL("Delete * From tblTempProd_RelatedProducts;")
  
  strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblTempProd_RelatedProducts (productID) " & _
           "SELECT productID " & _
           "From tblProdCalc_Atoms " & _
           "WHERE ingredientProductID=" & productID & ";"
           
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)

Public Sub CalculateProductPurchasePrices(costCenterID As Long)

  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempProd;")
  
  Call CreateQry(16)
  Call RunMySQL

  Call CreateQry(17)
  Call RunMySQL
    
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempProd;")
End Sub

Public Sub AtomizeProducts()
'On Error Resume Next
Dim maxLevel As Integer, i As Integer

  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblProdCalc_Atoms WHERE companyID=" & currentCompanyID() & ";")
  
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempProd;")
      
  Call CreateQry(12)
  Call RunMySQL
  
  Call CreateQry(13)
  Call RunMySQL
  
  maxLevel = DMax("ingredientProductLevel", "tblTempProd")
  
  Call CreateQry(14, -1)
  Call RunMySQL
    
  For i = maxLevel To 1 Step -1
    Call CreateQry(14, i)
    Call RunMySQL
  Next i
  
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempProd WHERE ingredientProductLevel<>0;")
  
  Call CreateQry(15)
  Call RunMySQL
    
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempProd;")
  
  Beep

End Sub
