Public Sub DailyInventoryDeviations(strQryName As String, updateFieldName As String)
Dim strSQL As String, str1 As String, str2 As String
 
  str1 = "UPDATE tblTempProdCalc_InvDeviationsCalc INNER JOIN tblProdCalcDI_DailyInvDeviationCalcs ON " & _
         "(tblTempProdCalc_InvDeviationsCalc.devCalcDate = tblProdCalcDI_DailyInvDeviationCalcs.deviationCalcDate) AND " & _
         "(tblTempProdCalc_InvDeviationsCalc.productID = tblProdCalcDI_DailyInvDeviationCalcs.dailyInvProductID) " & _
         "SET tblProdCalcDI_DailyInvDeviationCalcs."
  str2 = "[baseUnitQty];"
  
  Call RunSQL("DELETE * FROM tblTempProdCalc_InvDeviationsCalc;")
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery (strQryName)
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
  strSQL = str1 & updateFieldName & " = " & str2
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  
End Sub

Public Function DefaultUnitID(grpType As Integer) As Long
  Dim strWhere As String, unitID

  strWhere = "companyID=" & currentCompanyID
  
  If grpType = 1 Then
    unitID = DLookup("goodsUnitID", "tblCmpy_DefaultValues", strWhere)
  Else
    unitID = DLookup("serviceUnitID", "tblCmpy_DefaultValues", strWhere)
  End If
  
    If Not IsNull(unitID) Then
      DefaultUnitID = unitID
    End If

End Function

Public Function LastPurchasePrice(productID As Long) ' calculates last Base Unit Purchase Price
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, qDef As QueryDef, strSQL As String

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryDocGoods_Acceptance_LastPurchasePrice")
  qDef.Parameters("prID") = productID
  Set rs = qDef.OpenRecordset()
  If rs.RecordCount <> 0 Then
    LastPurchasePrice = rs("baseUnitPurchasePrice")
  Else
    LastPurchasePrice = 0
  End If
  
  rs.Close
  db.Close
  
End Function

Public Sub SaveLastPurchasePrices() ' updates product purchase price to last Base Unit Purchase Price
Dim strSQL As String, rs As Recordset, pp

strSQL = "SELECT tblProdCat_Products.productID, tblProdCat_Products.baseUnitPurchasePrice " & _
         "FROM tblProdCat_Products " & _
         "WHERE (tblProdCat_Products.recipeLevel=0) AND tblProdCat_Products.companyID=" & currentCompanyID & ";"

Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
rs.MoveFirst

Do Until rs.EOF
  pp = LastPurchasePrice(rs("productID"))
  If Not IsNull(pp) Then
    rs.Edit
    rs("baseUnitPurchasePrice") = pp
    rs.Update
  End If
  rs.MoveNext
Loop

End Sub

Public Function PPV(productID, unitID) As Double
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim baseUnitPurchasePrice

  If IsNull(productID) Or IsNull(unitID) Then
    PPV = 0
    Exit Function
  End If
  
  If productID = 2725 Then
    PPV = 0
  End If
  
  baseUnitPurchasePrice = DLookup("baseUnitPurchasePrice", "tblProdCat_Products", "productID=" & productID)
  If Not IsNull(unitID) Then
    baseUnitPurchasePrice = Round(DLookup("noOfBaseUnitsInUnit", "tblProdCat_ProductUnits", "productID=" & productID & " AND unitID=" & unitID) * baseUnitPurchasePrice, 2)
  Else
    baseUnitPurchasePrice = 0
  End If
  
  If IsNull(baseUnitPurchasePrice) Then
    PPV = 0
  Else
    PPV = baseUnitPurchasePrice
  End If
  
  Exit Function
  
err_exit:
  PPV = 0
End Function

Public Function vatRate(vatRateID) As Double
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim vatR
  
  If IsNull(vatRateID) Then
    vatRate = 0
    Exit Function
  End If
  
  vatR = DLookup("vatRate", "tblAccVat_VatRates", "vatRateID=" & vatRateID)
  If IsNull(vatR) Then
    vatRate = 0
  Else
    vatRate = vatR
  End If
  
err_exit:
  vatRate = 0
End Function

Public Sub PostAcceptances(frm As Form)
Dim iMax As Integer, i As Integer, cmptrID As Long
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset
  
  iMax = DCount("acceptanceID", "qryAccLinkAccptsPIs_NotPostedAcceptances")
  If iMax = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Vsi prevzemi so knji eni!"
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  cmptrID = computerID()
  
  i = 0
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("qryAccLinkAccptsPIs_NotPostedAcceptances")
  rs.MoveFirst
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    i = i + 1
    frm!status = i & "/" & iMax & " ..."
    frm.Repaint
    
    Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting WHERE computerID=" & cmptrID & ";")
    
    Call CreateQry(93, rs("acceptanceID"), , , , cmptrID)
    Call RunMySQL
         
    Call CreateQry(94, rs("acceptanceID"), , , , cmptrID)
    Call RunMySQL

    Call CreatePostingMySQL
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  Beep
  frm!status = Null
  frm.Repaint
End Sub

============== START FILE: modPurchaseInvoices.txt ==============

Option Compare Database
Option Explicit

Public Function CheckDates(frm As Form)
Dim resp As Integer
  If IsNull(frm!purchaseInvoiceDate) Or IsNull(frm!vatDate) Or IsNull(frm!serviceFirstDate) Or IsNull(frm!serviceLastDate) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Vsa datumska datumska polja morajo biti izpolnjena!", vbExclamation
    End
  End If
  
  If CDate(frm!serviceFirstDate) > CDate(frm!serviceLastDate) Then
    MsgBox "Datum S2 mora biti ve ji (ali enak) od datuma S1!", vbExclamation
    End
  End If
  
  If CDate(frm!vatDate) < CDate(frm!purchaseInvoiceDate) Then
    resp = MsgBox("Datum DDV je pred datumom ra una. Ali kljub temu nadaljujem?", vbYesNo + vbExclamation)
    If resp <> vbYes Then End
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function CheckGLAccounts(frm As Form)
Dim resp As Integer, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  If IsNull(frm!creditGLAccountID) Or frm!creditGLAccountID = 0 Or IsNull(frm!templateNumber) Then
    Call PurchaseInvoiceBPidAfterUpdate(frm)
    DoCmd.RunCommand acCmdSaveRecord
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!creditGLAccountID) Or frm!creditGLAccountID = 0 Then
    Beep
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka konto dobro! Preveri dobavitelja.", vbExclamation)
    End
  End If
  
  If IsNull(frm!templateNumber) Then
    resp = MsgBox("Manjka vzorec za knji enje! Preveri dobavitelja.", vbExclamation)
    End
  End If
  
  strSQL = "SELECT templateNumber, (partnerID) as expr1, GLAccountID, companyID AS expr2 " & _
           "FROM tblPtr_bpCmpyCostGLAccounts " & _
           "WHERE (templateNumber=" & frm!templateNumber & ") AND ((partnerID)=" & (frm!partnerID) & ") AND (GLAccountID Is Null) AND (companyID=" & currentCompanyID & "); "
  
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs.RecordCount > 0 Then
    resp = MsgBox("Pri izbranem vzorcu za knji enje manjkajo konti! Popravi dobavitelja!", vbExclamation)
    End
  End If
  
  strSQL = "SELECT templateNumber, (partnerID) as expr1, costShare, (companyID) AS expr2 " & _
           "FROM tblPtr_bpCmpyCostGLAccounts " & _
           "WHERE (templateNumber=" & frm!templateNumber & ") AND (IIf(Not IsNull([partnerID]),(partnerID))=" & (frm!partnerID) & ") " & _
           "AND (costShare Is Null) AND (IIf(Not IsNull([companyID]),companyID)=" & currentCompanyID & "); "
  
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs.RecordCount > 0 Then
    resp = MsgBox("Pri izbranem vzorcu za knji enje manjkajo dele i! Popravi dobavitelja!", vbExclamation)
    End
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function IsVatOK(frm As Form) As Boolean
Dim strSQL As String

  If DCount("docID", "qryAccVat_Errors", "docID=" & frm!purchaseInvoiceID) = 0 Then
      IsVatOK = True
      frm!lstVatErrors.RowSource = ""
  Else
    IsVatOK = False
    strSQL = "SELECT Description FROM qryAccVat_Errors WHERE docID=" & frm!purchaseInvoiceID & ";"
    frm!lstVatErrors.RowSource = strSQL
  End If
  frm!lstVatErrors.visible = Not IsVatOK
  
End Function

Public Sub CreatePurchaseInvoicePaymentOrder(invID As Long, Optional frm As Form)
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, rs1 As Recordset, qDef As QueryDef
Dim payDay As Date, payAm As Double, dueDate

  DoCmd.RunCommand acCmdSaveRecord
  
  dueDate = DLookup("dueDate", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID)
  
  If IsNull(dueDate) Or dueDate < Date Then
    payDay = Date
  Else
    payDay = CDate(dueDate)
  End If
  
  payAm = DLookup("purchaseInvoiceAmount", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID)
  
  Set qDef = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qryDocAccPI_CreatePaymentOrder")
  qDef.Parameters("invID") = invID
  Set rs1 = qDef.OpenRecordset()
  
  If rs1.RecordCount = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Ne morem kreirati pla ilnega naloga. Nepopolni podatki. Program bo prekinjen.", vbExclamation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  Call NewPOID(invID, 67, "GDSV", rs1("supplierInvoiceNumber") & " (" & rs1("purchaseInvoiceNumber") & ")", payDay, payAm, _
           rs1("partnerID"), rs1("ptrName"), rs1("street"), rs1("place"), rs1("BIC"), rs1("IBAN"), _
           rs1("supplierRef"), rs1("supplierRefInformation"), rs1("creditGLAccountID"), rs1("purchaseInvoiceNumber"), invID)

  On Error Resume Next
  If hasValue(frm) Then frm!frmPurchaseInvoicePaymentOrders.Requery
  Exit Sub
  
err_exit:
  Beep
  MsgBox "Napaka pri kreiranju pla ilnega naloga: " & err.description
  Resume Next

End Sub

Public Sub DeletePurchaseInvoiceDDVO(frm As Form, invID As Long)
Dim frmName As String

  Call RunSQL("DELETE purchaseInvoiceID, * FROM tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoiceVats WHERE purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID & ";")
  
  frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoice"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    frm!frmPurchaseInvoiceVat.SetFocus
    frm!frmPurchaseInvoiceVat.Requery
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub PurchaseInvoiceBPidAfterUpdate(frm As Form)
Dim strWhere As String, i, ref

  If IsNull(frm!partnerID) Then Exit Sub
  
  i = DFirst("countryID", "tblPtr_Partners", "partnerID=" & frm!partnerID)
  If IsNull(i) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Na dobavitelju manjka dr ava!", vbCritical
  Else
    If i = currentCountryID() Then
      frm!vatIncluded = 1
    Else
      frm!vatIncluded = 0
    End If
  End If
  
  i = DFirst("vatStatusID", "tblPtr_Partners", "partnerID=" & frm!partnerID)
  If IsNull(i) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Na dobavitelju DDV status!", vbCritical
  Else
    frm!vatStatusID = i
  End If
  
  strWhere = "partnerID=" & (frm!partnerID) & " And companyID=" & currentCompanyID()

  ref = DFirst("bpRef", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere)
  If IsNull(ref) Then
    frm!supplierRef = "SI00"
  Else
    frm!supplierRef = ref
  End If
  
  i = DFirst("meansOfPaymentGroupID", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere)
  If IsNull(i) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Na dobavitelju ni izbran privzeti na in pla ila obveznosti!", vbCritical
  Else
    frm!meansOfPaymentGroupID = i
  End If
  
  i = DFirst("payablesGLAccountID", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere)
  If IsNull(i) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Na dobavitelju ni izbran privzeti konto za knji enje obveznosti v glavno knjigo!", vbCritical
  Else
    frm!creditGLAccountID = i
  End If
  
  frm!templateNumber.Requery
  frm!templateNumber = 1
  
End Sub

Public Sub PurchaseInvoiceSuppInvNumberfterUpdate(frm As Form)
Dim strWhere As String, strInvNumber, refInf As String, resp As Integer

  strWhere = "supplierinvoicenumber = '" & frm!supplierInvoiceNumber & "' And companyID=" & currentCompanyID & " And partnerID=" & (frm!partnerID)
  
  strInvNumber = DFirst("purchaseInvoiceNumber", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices", strWhere)
  
  If Not IsNull(strInvNumber) Then
    If strInvNumber <> frm!purchaseInvoiceNumber Then
      Beep
      MsgBox "Mo nost podvajanja! Preveri ra un: " & strInvNumber, vbInformation
    End If
  End If
  
  If Not IsNull(frm!supplierRefInformation) Then
    resp = MsgBox("Prepi em referenco?", vbYesNo + vbInformation)
    If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  End If
  
  strWhere = "(partnerID=" & frm!partnerID & ") AND (companyID=" & currentCompanyID & ")"
  
  If Not IsNull(DFirst("bpRefInf1", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere)) Then
    refInf = DFirst("bpRefInf1", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere) & frm!supplierInvoiceNumber
  Else
    refInf = frm!supplierInvoiceNumber
  End If
  
  If Not IsNull(DFirst("bpRefInf2", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere)) Then
    refInf = refInf & DFirst("bpRefInf2", "tblPtr_PartnersCmpys", strWhere)
  End If
  
  frm!supplierRefInformation = refInf

End Sub

Public Sub DeletePurchaseInvoice(frm As Form, invID As Long)
'On Error Resume Next
Dim resp As Integer, strSQL As String, frmName As String, docID
  
  Beep
  If DCount("*", "tblAccVat_DDVODetails", "documentID=" & invID) <> 0 Then
      MsgBox "Ra un " & frm!purchaseInvoiceNumber & " je vklju en v obra un DDV. Brisanje ni mogo e.", vbInformation
      Exit Sub
  End If
  resp = MsgBox("Ali resni no  eli  izbrisati ra un " & frm!purchaseInvoiceNumber & "?", vbCritical + vbYesNo)
  
  If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
  
    Call DeletePurchaseInvoicePaymentOrders(frm, invID)
    Call DeletePurchaseInvoicePostings(frm, invID)

    strSQL = "UPDATE tblDocGoods_Acceptances SET purchaseInvoiceID = Null WHERE purchaseInvoiceID= " & invID & ";"
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
    
    frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoice"
    If frm.Name = frmName Then DoCmd.Close acForm, frm.Name
    
    strSQL = "DELETE FROM tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices WHERE purchaseInvoiceID= " & invID & ";"
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
    
    frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoices"
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
      Set frm = Forms(frmName)
      frm.Requery
    End If

    frmName = "frmAccDocPI_PurchaseInvoiceFromAcceptance"
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
      Set frm = Forms(frmName)
      frm!lstAcceptances.Requery
    End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub DeletePurchaseInvoicePaymentOrders(frm As Form, invID As Long)
Dim resp As Integer, frmName As String, poID

  poID = DLookup("unitedPaymentOrderID", "tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders", "(parentID=" & invID & ") AND (parentTableID=67)")

  If IsNull(poID) Or poID = 0 Then
    Call DeleteChild("tblAccPmt_PaymentOrders", invID, 67)
    frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoice"
    If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
       frm!frmPurchaseInvoicePaymentOrders.Requery
    End If
  Else
    resp = MsgBox("Pla ilnega naloga ni mogo e izbrisati, ker je vklju en v zdru eno pla ilo!", vbExclamation)
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Sub DeletePurchaseInvoicePostings(frm As Form, invID As Long)
Dim frmName As String

  Call DeleteChild("tblAccGL_Postings", invID, 67)
  frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoice"
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
      frm!postingID.Requery
  End If
    
End Sub

Public Sub CreatePurchaseInvoicePosting(invID As Long, checkData As Boolean, postingType As Long, docTypeID As Long)
Dim frm As Form, resp As Integer
Dim frmName As String, strWhere As String
Dim Yr As Integer, mnth As String, glAccountID_190 As Long, glAccountID_500 As Long

  If checkData Then
  
    Set frm = Forms!frmPurchaseInvoice.Form
    DoCmd.RunCommand acCmdSaveRecord

    Call CheckGLAccounts(frm)
    Call CheckDates(frm)
    
    If IsVatOK(frm) = False Then
      Beep
      resp = MsgBox("DDV je mogo e napa en. Ali naj ra un vseeno poknji im?", vbInformation + vbYesNo)
      If resp <> vbYes Then Exit Sub
    End If
    
  End If
  
    strWhere = "purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID
    Yr = DMin("fiscalYear", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails", strWhere)
    mnth = DMin("fiscalMonth", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails", strWhere & " And fiscalYear=" & Yr)
    glAccountID_190 = FindGLAccountID("190000", True)
    glAccountID_500 = FindGLAccountID("500000", True)
    Call RunMySQL("CALL P_DocAcc_CreatePurchaseInvoicePosting(" & invID & ", " & Yr & ", " & mnth & ", " & computerID() & ", " & glAccountID_190 & ", " & glAccountID_500 & ", " & docTypeID & ");")

  Call CreatePostingMySQL
  
  frmName = "frmPurchaseInvoice"
  
  If Application.SysCmd(acSysCmdGetObjectState, acForm, frmName) Then
    Set frm = Forms(frmName)
    frm!frmAccGL_PostingSub.Requery
    frm!postingID.Requery
  End If

End Sub

Public Sub PreparePurchaseInvPostingDetails_Months(invID As Long, srvDt1 As Date, srvDt2 As Date, invAm As Double)        'costs or deferred cost, period = month
Dim totCostAmount As Double, noOfMonths As Integer, i As Integer, db As Database, rs As Recordset, mCostAmount As Double, qDef As QueryDef, costSum As Double
  
  totCostAmount = DFirst("am", "qryPurchaseInvoicePostingDetailsSub", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID)
  noOfMonths = DateDiff("m", srvDt1, srvDt2) + 1
  mCostAmount = Round(totCostAmount / noOfMonths, 2)
  costSum = 0
  
  Call RunSQL("DELETE * FROM tblTempDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePosting;")
  
  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblTempDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePosting", dbOpenDynaset)
  
  For i = 0 To noOfMonths - 1
    
    rs.AddNew
    rs("fiscalMonth") = Month(LastDayInMonth(srvDt1, i))
    rs("fiscalYear") = Year(LastDayInMonth(srvDt1, i))
    If i = noOfMonths - 1 Then
      rs("amount") = Round(totCostAmount - costSum, 2)
    Else
      rs("amount") = mCostAmount
      costSum = costSum + mCostAmount
    End If
    rs.Update
  Next i
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappPurchaseInvoicePostingDetails_MnthDays"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
End Sub

Public Sub PreparePurchaseInvPostingDetails_Days(invID As Long, srvDt1 As Date, srvDt2 As Date, invAm As Double)        'costs or deferred cost, period = day
Dim totCostAmount As Double, NoOfDays As Integer, noOfMonths As Integer, i As Integer, d As Integer, db As Database, rs As Recordset
Dim dailyCostAmount As Double, qDef As QueryDef, costSum As Double

  If DCount("*", "qryPurchaseInvoicePostingDetailsSub", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID) = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Ni podatkov.", vbInformation
  End If
  
  totCostAmount = DFirst("am", "qryPurchaseInvoicePostingDetailsSub", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & invID)
  NoOfDays = DateDiff("d", srvDt1, srvDt2) + 1
  noOfMonths = DateDiff("m", srvDt1, srvDt2) + 1
  dailyCostAmount = totCostAmount / NoOfDays
  costSum = 0
  
  Call RunSQL("DELETE * FROM tblTempDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePosting;")
  
  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblTempDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePosting", dbOpenDynaset)
  
  For i = 0 To noOfMonths - 1
    
    rs.AddNew
    
    If i = 0 Then
      NoOfDays = LastDayInMonth(srvDt1, 0) - srvDt1 + 1
    Else
      NoOfDays = Day(LastDayInMonth(srvDt1, i))
    End If
    
    rs("fiscalMonth") = Month(LastDayInMonth(srvDt1, i))
    rs("fiscalYear") = Year(LastDayInMonth(srvDt1, i))
    
    If i = noOfMonths - 1 Then
      rs("amount") = Round(totCostAmount - costSum, 2)
    Else
      rs("amount") = Round(dailyCostAmount * NoOfDays, 2)
      costSum = costSum + Round(dailyCostAmount * NoOfDays, 2)
    End If
    
    rs.Update
  
  Next i
  
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappPurchaseInvoicePostingDetails_MnthDays"
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
  
End Sub

Public Sub AutoGGPurchaseInvoicePosting()
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim rs As DAO.Recordset, iMax, i As Integer, dif As Double, ID As Long, strSQL As String, cmpyID As Long

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("SELECT purchaseInvoiceID, LEFT(qryAccPI.purchaseInvoiceNumber,12) AS purchaseInvoiceNumber, lockedAt, documentTypeID FROM qryAccPI WHERE (purchDiff=0) AND (isPosted=0) AND Not IsNull(finishedAt) GROUP BY purchaseInvoiceID, LEFT(qryAccPI.purchaseInvoiceNumber,12), lockedAt, documentTypeID;", dbOpenDynaset)
  
  iMax = rs.RecordCount
  If iMax = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Vsi ra uni so poknji eni.", vbInformation
    DoCmd.Hourglass False
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
  rs.MoveLast
  iMax = rs.RecordCount
  
  Call CreateQry1(130, cmpyID)
  Call RunMySQL
    
  rs.MoveFirst
  i = 1
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    Forms!frmPurchaseInvoices.Form!status = "GK " & i & "/" & iMax & " ..."
    Forms!frmPurchaseInvoices.Form.Repaint
    
    strSQL = "DELETE FROM tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails WHERE purchaseInvoiceID=" & rs("purchaseInvoiceID")
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
    
    If IsNull(rs("lockedAt")) Then
      strSQL = "UPDATE tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices SET lockedAt=Now() WHERE purchaseInvoiceID=" & rs("purchaseInvoiceID")
      Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
    End If
    
    Call CreateQry1(126, rs("purchaseInvoiceID"), cmpyID, currentCountryID())
    Call RunMySQL
    
    Call CreateQry1(210, rs("purchaseInvoiceID"), cmpyID)
    Call RunMySQL

    ID = DMax("purchaseInvoiceDetailID", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & rs("purchaseInvoiceID"))
    strSQL = "UPDATE tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails SET tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails.amount = tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails.amount + " & Replace(dif, ",", ".")
    strSQL = strSQL & " WHERE tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoicePostingDetails.purchaseInvoiceDetailID=" & ID
    Call RunMySQL(strSQL)

    Call CreatePurchaseInvoicePosting(rs("purchaseInvoiceID"), False, DLookup("postingTypeID", "tblDocAcc_PurchaseInvoices", "purchaseInvoiceID=" & rs("purchaseInvoiceID")), rs("documentTypeID"))
    rs.MoveNext
    i = i + 1
  Loop

  rs.Close
  Set rs = Nothing
  Beep
  
  Exit Sub
  
err_exit:
  
  MsgBox "Napaka pri knji enju ra una " & rs("purchaseInvoiceNumber"), vbCritical
  Resume Next

  
End Sub

Sub AutoLinkAcceptancePIbyLinkName(frm As Form)
  
  Call RunSQL("DELETE * FROM tblTempAccPR")
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qappAccLinkAccptsPIs_AutoLink_1"
  DoCmd.OpenQuery "qupdLinkAccptsPIs_AutoLink_2"

  DoCmd.SetWarnings True

  Beep

End Sub

Public Sub AutoLinkAcceptancePIbyAmount(frm As Form)
Dim qDef As QueryDef, db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, iMax As Integer, i As Integer
Dim rs1 As DAO.Recordset

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("qryLinkAccptsPIs_AutoLinkByAmount_PIs", dbOpenDynaset)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Beep: Exit Sub
  rs.MoveLast
  iMax = rs.RecordCount
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  i = 0
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    i = i + 1
    frm!status = i & "/" & iMax & " ..."
    frm.Repaint
    Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryLinkAccptsPIs_AutoLinkByAmount_Acceptances")
    qDef.Parameters("ptrID") = rs("partnerID")
    qDef.Parameters("fiscalMonth") = rs("fiscalMonth")
    qDef.Parameters("accAm") = rs("PIAM")
    Set rs1 = qDef.OpenRecordset()
     
    If rs1.RecordCount = 1 Then
      rs1.Edit
      rs1("linkName") = rs("lnkName")
      rs1("isLinked") = 1
      rs1.Update
    
      rs.Edit
      rs("isLinked") = 1
      rs.Update

      rs1.Close
    End If
    
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  frm!status = Null
  frm.Repaint
 
  rs.Close

End Sub

'Sub LoadImages(control, ByRef image)
'    ' Callbackname in XML File "loadImage"
'
'    ' Loads an image with transparency to the ribbon
'    ' Modul basGDIPlus is required
'    ' Laed ein Bild mit Transparenz in das Ribbon
'    ' Modul basGDIPlus wird dafuer benoetigt
'
'    Dim strImage        As String
'    Dim strPicture      As String
'
'    strImage = CStr(control)
'    strPicture = getPic(strImage)

Sub GetEnabled(control As IRibbonControl, ByRef enabled)
    ' Callbackname in XML File "getEnabled"
    
    ' To set the property "enabled" to a Ribbon Control
    ' For further information see: http://www.accessribbon.de/en/index.php?Downloads:12
    ' Setzen der Enabled Eigenschaft eines Ribbon Controls
    ' Weitere Informationen: http://www.accessribbon.de/index.php?Downloads:12

    Select Case control.ID
        
        Case "btn0"
            ' Button
            ' In Tab:   tab1
            ' In Group: grp0
            enabled = True

        Case Else
            enabled = True

    End Select

End Sub

Sub GetVisible(control As IRibbonControl, ByRef visible)
    ' Callbackname in XML File "getVisible"
    
    ' To set the property "visible" to a Ribbon Control
    ' For further information see: http://www.accessribbon.de/en/index.php?Downloads:12
    ' Setzen der Visible Eigenschaft eines Ribbon Controls
    ' Weitere Informationen: http://www.accessribbon.de/index.php?Downloads:12

    Select Case control.ID
        
        Case "tab0"
            ' Tab:   tab0
            visible = True
        Case "btn0"
            ' Button
            ' In Tab:   tab1
            ' In Group: grp0
            visible = True
        Case "grp0"
            ' Group:    grp0
            ' In Tab:   tab1
            visible = True
        Case "grp1"
            ' Group:    grp1
            ' In Tab:   tab1
            visible = True
        Case "grp2"
            ' Group:    grp2
            ' In Tab:   tab1
            visible = True
        Case "tab1"
            ' Tab:   tab1
            visible = True

        Case Else
            visible = True

    End Select

End Sub

'Button

Sub OnActionButton(control As IRibbonControl)
    ' Callbackname in XML File "onAction"

    ' Callback for event button click
    ' Callback fuer Button Click
    
    Select Case control.ID
        
        Case "btn0"
            ' In Tab:   tab1
            ' In Group: grp0
            MsgBox "Button """ & control.ID & """ clicked" & vbCrLf & _
                   "Es wurde auf Button """ & control.ID & """ im Ribbon geklickt", _
                   vbInformation
      
        Case Else
            MsgBox "Button """ & control.ID & """ clicked" & vbCrLf & _
                           "Es wurde auf Button """ & control.ID & """ im Ribbon geklickt", _
                           vbInformation
    End Select

End Sub

    ' Callback fuer Command Button Click

    MsgBox "Button ""Help"" clicked" & vbCrLf & _
                           "Es wurde auf Button ""Hilfe"" geklickt", _
                           vbInformation
    CancelDefault = True

End Sub

' DynamicMenu

Sub GetContent(control As IRibbonControl, _
               ByRef XMLString)

    ' Sample for a Ribbon XML "getContent" Callback
    ' See also http://www.accessribbon.de/en/index.php?Access_-_Ribbons:Callbacks:dynamicMenu_-_getContent
    '     and: http://www.accessribbon.de/en/index.php?Access_-_Ribbons:Ribbon_XML___Controls:Dynamic_Menu

    ' Beispiel fuer einen Ribbon XML - "getContent" Callback
    ' Siehe auch: http://www.accessribbon.de/index.php?Access_-_Ribbons:Callbacks:dynamicMenu_-_getContent
    '       und : http://www.accessribbon.de/?Access_-_Ribbons:Ribbon_XML___Controls:Dynamic_Menu

    Select Case control.ID
        

        Case Else
            XMLString = getXMLForDynamicMenu()
    End Select
 
End Sub


' Helper Function
' Hilfsfunktionen

Public Function getXMLForDynamicMenu() As String
    
    ' Creates a XML String for DynamicMenu CallBack - getContent
    
    ' Erstellt den Inhalt fuer das DynamicMenu im Callback getContent
    
    Dim lngDummy    As Long
    Dim strDummy    As String
    Dim strContent  As String
    
    Dim Items(4) As ItemsVal
    Items(0).ID = "btnDy1"
    Items(0).label = "Item 1"
    Items(0).imageMso = "_1"
    Items(1).ID = "btnDy2"
    Items(1).label = "Item 2"
    Items(1).imageMso = "_2"
    Items(2).ID = "btnDy3"
    Items(2).label = "Item 3"
    Items(2).imageMso = "_3"
    Items(3).ID = "btnDy4"
    Items(3).label = "Item 4"
    Items(3).imageMso = "_4"
    Items(4).ID = "btnDy5"
    Items(4).label = "Item 5"
    Items(4).imageMso = "_5"
    
    strDummy = "<menu xmlns=""http://schemas.microsoft.com/office/2009/07/customui"">" & vbCrLf
    
        For lngDummy = LBound(Items) To UBound(Items)
            strContent = strContent & _
                "<button id=""" & Items(lngDummy).ID & """" & _
                " label=""" & Items(lngDummy).label & """" & _
                " imageMso=""" & Items(lngDummy).imageMso & """" & _
                " onAction=""OnActionButton""/>" & vbCrLf
        Next
 

    strDummy = strDummy & strContent & "</menu>"
    getXMLForDynamicMenu = strDummy

End Function

Public Function getTheValue(strTag As String, strValue As String) As String
    ' *************************************************************
    ' Created from     : Avenius
    ' Parameter        : Input String, SuchValue String
    ' Date created     : 05.01.2008
    '
    ' Sample:
    ' getTheValue("DefaultValue:=Test;Enabled:=0;Visible:=1", "DefaultValue")
    ' Return           : "Test"
    ' *************************************************************
      
   On Error Resume Next
      
   Dim workTb()     As String
   Dim Ele()        As String
   Dim myVariabs()  As String
   Dim i            As Integer

      workTb = Split(strTag, ";")
      
      ReDim myVariabs(LBound(workTb) To UBound(workTb), 0 To 1)
      For i = LBound(workTb) To UBound(workTb)
         Ele = Split(workTb(i), ":=")
         myVariabs(i, 0) = Ele(0)
         If UBound(Ele) = 1 Then
            myVariabs(i, 1) = Ele(1)
         End If
      Next
      
      For i = LBound(myVariabs) To UBound(myVariabs)
         If strValue = myVariabs(i, 0) Then
            getTheValue = myVariabs(i, 1)
         End If
      Next
      
End Function

Public Function getAppPath() As String
    Dim strDummy As String
    strDummy = CurrentProject.Path
    If Right(strDummy, 1) <> "\" Then strDummy = strDummy & "\"
    getAppPath = strDummy
End Function

Public Function getPic(strFullPath As String) As String
    Dim strResult As String
    
    If InStrRev(strFullPath, "\") > 0 Then
        strResult = Mid(strFullPath, InStrRev(strFullPath, "\") + 1)
    Else
        strResult = ""
    End If
   
    getPic = strResult
End Function

Public Sub PostGagarinInvoiceOLD(invID As Long, invDate As Date, Optional mnth)
Dim cmptrID As Long, docTypeID As Long, diff As Double, ID As Long, strSQL As String

  cmptrID = computerID()
  
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting WHERE tblTempAccGL_Posting.computerID=" & cmptrID)
  
  Select Case docTypeID
    Case 2 To 3:
      
      Call CreateQry(114, invID, , , , cmptrID) '120
      Call RunMySQL
      

        Call CreateQry(115, invID, , , , SQLDate(LastDayInMonth(invDate, 1)), cmptrID) '260
        Call RunMySQL

      
      Call CreateQry(116, invID, currentCompanyID(), , , cmptrID) '760
      Call RunMySQL
    
    Case 18:
      
'      Call CreateQry(180, mnth, , , , SQLDate(LastDayInMonth(CDate("01." & mnth & "." & currentFiscalYear()), 0)), FindGLAccountID("165001"), cmptrID) '165 kreditne kartice
'      Call RunMySQL
'
'      Call CreateQry(181, mnth, , , , SQLDate(LastDayInMonth("01." & mnth & "." & currentFiscalYear(), 0)), FindGLAccountID("145000"), cmptrID) '145
'      Call RunMySQL
      
    Case 19:
      
      Call CreateQry(177, invID, currentCompanyID(), , , cmptrID) '120
      Call RunMySQL
      
      Call CreateQry(178, invID, , , , FindGLAccountID("145000"), cmptrID) '145
      Call RunMySQL
     
    Case 22:
      
      Call CreateQry(177, invID, currentCompanyID(), , , cmptrID) '120
      Call RunMySQL
      

        Call CreateQry(115, invID, , , , SQLDate(LastDayInMonth(invDate, 1)), cmptrID) '260
        Call RunMySQL
      
      Call CreateQry(200, invID, , , , FindGLAccountID("760101"), cmptrID) '760
      Call RunMySQL
      
    Case Else
      Exit Sub
  
  End Select
  
      diff = Round(DLookup("diff", "qryTourSales_PostingDiff"), 2)
      
      If Abs(diff) < 1 Then
        If diff <> 0 Then
          ID = DLookup("maxId", "qryTourSales_PostingDiff")
          strSQL = "UPDATE tblTempAccGL_Posting SET creditAmount = creditAmount+" & Replace(diff, ",", ".") & " WHERE ID=" & ID
          Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
        End If
      Else
        strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblTourSales_PostingDiff_PostLog ( ggInvoiceID, diff ) SELECT " & invID & ", " & Replace(diff, ",", ".") & ";"
        Call RunSQL(strSQL)
        Exit Sub
      End If
      
  Call CreatePostingMySQL
  
End Sub

Public Sub PostGagarinInvoice(invID As Long, docTypeID As Long, Optional mnth, Optional Yr)
Dim cmptrID As Long, companyID As Long, partnerID As Long, ccID As Long, countryID As Long, countryGroupID As Long, GLAccountID As Long, postType As String, diff As Double, strSQL As String, ID

  cmptrID = computerID()
  Call RunMySQL("DELETE FROM tblTempAccGL_Posting WHERE tblTempAccGL_Posting.computerID=" & cmptrID)
  
  Select Case docTypeID
    Case Is < 4:                'docTypeid = 2 in 3:  turizem booking in ostalo
      
      partnerID = DLookup("partnerID", "tblTourSales_ggInvoices", "ggInvoiceID=" & invID)
      ID = DLookup("countryID", "tblCmpy_Companies", "isTourism=1 And partnerID=" & partnerID)
  
      If IsNull(ID) Then
        countryID = 1
      Else
        countryID = DLookup("countryID", "tblCmpy_Companies", "isTourism=1 And partnerID=" & partnerID)
      End If
        
      countryGroupID = DLookup("countryGroupID", "tblShrdGeo_Countries", "countryID=" & countryID)
      
      Call CreateQry(205, invID, , , , cmptrID) '120
      Call RunMySQL
       
      Call CreateQry(206, invID, , , , cmptrID, countryGroupID) '760 slovenija
      Call RunMySQL
    
    Case 18:
      
      companyID = currentCompanyID()
      
      Call CreateQry1(180, companyID, Yr, mnth, docTypeID, SQLDate(LastDayInMonth("01." & mnth & "." & Yr, 0)), FindGLAccountID("165001"), cmptrID)  '165 kreditne kartice
      Call RunMySQL

      Call CreateQry1(237, companyID, Yr, mnth, docTypeID, SQLDate(LastDayInMonth("01." & mnth & "." & Yr, 0)), FindGLAccountID("415002"), cmptrID)  '165 kreditne kartice-provizija
      Call RunMySQL
      
      Call CreateQry1(181, companyID, Yr, mnth, docTypeID, SQLDate(LastDayInMonth("01." & mnth & "." & Yr, 0)), FindGLAccountID("145000"), cmptrID)  '145 prefakturiranje
      Call RunMySQL

    Case 19:                             'smu arske vozovnice TRR -> saldakonti
      
      Call CreateQry(205, invID, , , , cmptrID) '120
      Call RunMySQL

      Call CreateQry(178, , , , , invID, FindGLAccountID("145000"), cmptrID) '145
      Call RunMySQL

    Case 22:
        
      'dolo itev konta za prihodke
      ccID = DLookup("costCenterID", "tblTourSales_ggInvoices", "ggInvoiceID=" & invID)
      If ccID = 137 Then      ' e gre za smu arsko vozovnico
        partnerID = DLookup("partnerID", "tblTourSales_ggInvoices", "ggInvoiceID=" & invID)
        countryID = DLookup("countryID", "tblPtr_Partners", "partnerID=" & partnerID)
        countryGroupID = DLookup("countryGroupID", "tblShrdGeo_Countries", "countryID=" & countryID)

        Select Case countryGroupID
          Case 1: GLAccountID = FindGLAccountID("760002") 'SLO
          Case 2: GLAccountID = FindGLAccountID("761102") 'EU
          Case 3: GLAccountID = FindGLAccountID("761202") 'tujina
        End Select
      Else
        GLAccountID = FindGLAccountID("760009")
      End If
 
      Call CreateQry(205, invID, , , , cmptrID) '120
      Call RunMySQL
        
      Call CreateQry(235, , , , , invID, FindGLAccountID("260000"), cmptrID) '260
      Call RunMySQL
       
      Call CreateQry(200, , , , , invID, GLAccountID, cmptrID) '760
      Call RunMySQL
        
      Case Else
        MsgBox "Napa en tip dokumenta:" & docTypeID & "!"
        Exit Sub
  
  End Select
  
'      diff = Round(DLookup("diff", "qryTourSales_PostingDiff"), 2)
'
'      If Abs(diff) < 1 Then
'        If diff <> 0 Then
'          ID = DLookup("maxId", "qryTourSales_PostingDiff")
'          strSQL = "UPDATE tblTempAccGL_Posting SET creditAmount = creditAmount+" & Replace(diff, ",", ".") & " WHERE ID=" & ID
'          Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
'        End If
'      Else
'        strSQL = "INSERT INTO tblTourSales_PostingDiff_PostLog ( ggInvoiceID, diff ) SELECT " & invID & ", " & Replace(diff, ",", ".") & ";"
'        Call RunSQL(strSQL)
'        Exit Sub
'      End If
      
  Call CreatePostingMySQL
  
End Sub

Public Sub PostGGInvoices(frm As Form, documentTypeID As Long)
Dim qDef As QueryDef, rs As DAO.Recordset, iMax As Integer, i As Integer
  
  Call CreateQry1(234, currentCompanyID())
  
  Set qDef = CurrentDb.QueryDefs("qryTourSales_ggInvoicesNotPosted")
  Set rs = qDef.OpenRecordset
  
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Ne obstajajo ra uni za knji enje.", vbInformation
    DoCmd.Hourglass False
    End
  End If
  
  rs.MoveLast
  
  iMax = rs.RecordCount
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  i = 0
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
  
    frm!status = i + 1 & "/" & iMax
    frm.Repaint
    Call PostGagarinInvoice(rs("ggInvoiceID"), documentTypeID, False)
    rs.MoveNext
    i = i + 1
  
  Loop
  
  frm!status = Null

End Sub
Public Sub PostGGInvoicesOLD(frm As Form)
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset, iMax As Integer, i As Integer
  
  strSQL = "SELECT ggInvoiceID, ggInvoiceNumber, invoiceDate " & _
           "FROM qrySalesGG_Invoices_CmpyYr " & _
           "WHERE (postingID Is Null) AND (invoiceDate Is Not Null) AND (partnerName Is Not Null) AND (serviceLastDate Is Not Null) AND (costCenterAbb Is Not Null) AND (post<>'');"
  
  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Ne obstajajo ra uni za knji enje.", vbInformation
    DoCmd.Hourglass False
    End
  End If
  
  rs.MoveLast
  
  iMax = rs.RecordCount
  
  rs.MoveFirst
  i = 0
  
  Do While Not rs.EOF
  
    frm!status = i + 1 & "/" & iMax
    frm.Repaint
    Call PostGagarinInvoiceOLD(rs("ggInvoiceID"), rs("invoiceDate"), False)
    rs.MoveNext
    i = i + 1
  
  Loop
  
  frm!status = Null

End Sub

Public Function currentCompanyName(fullName As Boolean, Optional cmpyID) As String
On Error GoTo err_exit

  If IsMissing(cmpyID) Then cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
  
  If fullName Then
    currentCompanyName = DFirst("cmpyName", "tblCmpy_Companies", "companyID=" & cmpyID) & ", D : " & DFirst("cmpyTaxNumber", "tblCmpy_Companies", "companyID=" & cmpyID)
  Else
    currentCompanyName = DFirst("cmpyName", "tblCmpy_Companies", "companyID=" & cmpyID)
  End If
  
  Exit Function
err_exit:
  currentCompanyName = "CMPY NAME ERROR"
  
End Function

Public Function currentFiscalMonthID() As Long
'On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim m As Integer

  If currentFiscalYear() = Year(Date) Then
    m = Month(Date)
  Else
    m = 12
  End If
  
  currentFiscalMonthID = DFirst("fiscalMonthID", "tblAccFP_FiscalMonths", "(fiscalMonth=" & m & ") AND (fiscalYear=" & currentFiscalYear() & ")")
  
  Exit Function
  
err_exit:

  currentFiscalMonthID = 0
  
End Function

Public Sub PopulateTblSet(tblName As String, tblSetName As String, pkeyName As String)
Dim strSQL As String

  strSQL = "DELETE " & tblSetName & ".*, " & tblSetName & "." & pkeyName & _
           " FROM " & tblName & " RIGHT JOIN " & tblSetName & " ON " & tblName & "." & pkeyName & " = " & tblSetName & "." & pkeyName & _
           " WHERE (((" & tblName & "." & pkeyName & ") Is Null));"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)
  
  strSQL = "INSERT INTO " & tblSetName & "(" & pkeyName & ")SELECT " & tblName & "." & pkeyName & _
           " FROM " & tblName & " LEFT JOIN " & tblSetName & " ON " & tblName & "." & pkeyName & " = " & tblSetName & "." & pkeyName & _
           " WHERE ((( " & tblSetName & "." & pkeyName & ") Is Null));"
  Call RunSQL(strSQL)

End Sub

Public Sub SetSelected(tblName As String, Optional pkeyName As String, Optional pkeyValue As String, Optional OnlyDelete As Boolean)

  Call RunSQL("UPDATE " & tblName & " SET isSelected = 0 WHERE isSelected=True;")
  If OnlyDelete Then Exit Sub
  Call RunSQL("UPDATE " & tblName & " SET isSelected = True WHERE " & pkeyName & "=" & pkeyValue & ";")

End Sub

Public Function SaveAllCurrentSettings()
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset

  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblShrdApp_qrys")

  rs.MoveFirst

  Do Until rs.EOF
    If Not rs("isLocal") Then
        Call CreateQry(rs("qryID"))
    End If
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop

End Function

Public Function CreateQry(qryID As Long, Optional othr1, Optional othr2, Optional othr3, Optional othr4, Optional pmt01, Optional pmt02, Optional pmt03, Optional pmt04) As String
Dim strSQL As String, cmpyID As Long, Yr As Long, ccID As Long, cmpyGrpID As Long, countryID As Long
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, qDef As QueryDef

  cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
  Yr = currentFiscalYear()
  ccID = currentCostCenterID()
  cmpyGrpID = currentCompanyGroupID()
  countryID = currentCountryID()
  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblShrdApp_qrys", dbOpenDynaset)
  rs.FindFirst "qryID=" & qryID
  
  If Not rs.NoMatch Then
  
    Set qDef = db.QueryDefs(rs("qryName"))
    strSQL = rs("qrySQL") & vbCrLf
    
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereCmpy")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereCmpy") & cmpyID & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereYear")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereYear") & Yr & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereCC")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereCC") & ccID & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereCmpyGrp")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereCmpyGrp") & cmpyGrpID & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereCountry")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereCountry") & countryID & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereOther1")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereOther1") & IIf(hasValue(othr1), othr1, "") & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereOther2")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereOther2") & IIf(hasValue(othr2), othr2, "") & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereOther3")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereOther3") & IIf(hasValue(othr3), othr3, "") & ") "
    If Not IsNull(rs("whereOther4")) Then strSQL = strSQL & rs("whereOther4") & IIf(hasValue(othr4), othr4, "") & ") "
    strSQL = strSQL & vbCrLf & rs("qryOrderBy")

    If Not IsMissing(pmt01) Then
      strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt01", pmt01)
    End If
    
    If Not IsMissing(pmt02) Then
      strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt02", pmt02)
    End If
    
    If Not IsMissing(pmt03) Then
      strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt03", pmt03)
    End If
    
    If Not IsMissing(pmt04) Then
      strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt04", pmt04)
    End If
    
    qDef.SQL = strSQL
    
    'Debug.Print strSQL
  End If

End Function

Public Function CreateQry1(qryID As Long, Optional pmt01, Optional pmt02, Optional pmt03, Optional pmt04, Optional pmt05, Optional pmt06, Optional pmt07, Optional pmt08, Optional pmt09, Optional pmt10) As String
Dim db As Database, rs As DAO.Recordset, qDef As QueryDef, strSQL As String

  
  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("tblShrdApp_qrys", dbOpenDynaset)
  rs.FindFirst "qryID=" & qryID

  If rs.NoMatch Then
    MsgBox "Query qryID=:" & qryID & " ne obstaja!", vbInformation
    End
  End If
  
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs(rs("qryName"))
  strSQL = rs("qrySQL")
              
  If IsMissing(pmt01) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt01", pmt01)
  End If
  
  If IsMissing(pmt02) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt02", pmt02)
  End If
          
    If IsMissing(pmt03) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt03", pmt03)
  End If
          
  If IsMissing(pmt04) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt04", pmt04)
  End If
          
  If IsMissing(pmt05) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt05", pmt05)
  End If
          
  If IsMissing(pmt06) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt06", pmt06)
  End If
  If IsMissing(pmt07) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt07", pmt07)
  End If
  
  If IsMissing(pmt08) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt08", pmt08)
  End If
          
    If IsMissing(pmt09) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt09", pmt09)
  End If
          
  If IsMissing(pmt10) Then
    GoTo create_qry
  Else
    strSQL = Replace(strSQL, "pmt10", pmt10)
  End If

create_qry:
    qDef.SQL = strSQL
    'Debug.Print strSQL

End Function

'***************** Code Start ***************
Public Function SetProperties(strPropName As String, varPropType As Variant, varPropValue As Variant) As Integer

    On Error GoTo Err_SetProperties

    Dim db As DAO.Database, prp As DAO.Property

    Set db = CurrentDb
    db.Properties(strPropName) = varPropValue
    SetProperties = True
    Set db = Nothing

Exit_SetProperties:
    Exit Function

Err_SetProperties:
    If err = 3270 Then    'Property not found
        Set prp = db.CreateProperty(strPropName, varPropType, varPropValue)
        db.Properties.Append prp
        Resume Next
    Else
        SetProperties = False
        MsgBox "SetProperties", err.Number, err.description
        Resume Exit_SetProperties
    End If
End Function
'***************** Code End ***************

Public Function HTMLLine(TagName As String, content) As String
  HTMLLine = UTF8Encode("<" & TagName & ">" & content & "</" & TagName & ">")
  HTMLLine = Replace(HTMLLine, "&", "&amp;") & chr(13) & chr(10)        'Chr(13) & Chr(10)= go to new line
End Function

Public Function XMLNumber(d) As String
  If IsNull(d) Then d = 0
  XMLNumber = Replace(Format$(d, "###########0.00"), ",", ".")
End Function

Public Function XMLDate(d As Date) As String
  XMLDate = Format(Year(d), "0000") & "-" & Format(Month(d), "00") & "-" & Format(Day(d), "00")
End Function

Public Function SQLDate(d As Date) As String
  SQLDate = Format(Year(d), "0000") & "-" & Format(Month(d), "00") & "-" & Format(Day(d), "00")
End Function

Function UTF8Encode(ByVal sStr)

Dim l As Integer, lChar
Dim sUtf8 As String

  If IsNull(sStr) Then
    UTF8Encode = ""
    Exit Function
  End If
  
  For l = 1 To Len(sStr)
    lChar = AscW(Mid(sStr, l, 1))
    
    If lChar < 128 Then
      sUtf8$ = sUtf8$ + Mid(sStr, l, 1)
    ElseIf ((lChar > 127) And (lChar < 2048)) Then
      sUtf8$ = sUtf8$ + chr(((lChar \ 64) Or 192))
      sUtf8$ = sUtf8$ + chr(((lChar And 63) Or 128))
    Else
      sUtf8$ = sUtf8$ + chr(((lChar \ 144) Or 234))
      sUtf8$ = sUtf8$ + chr((((lChar \ 64) And 63) Or 128))
      sUtf8$ = sUtf8$ + chr(((lChar And 63) Or 128))
    End If
  Next l
  
  UTF8Encode = sUtf8$

End Function

Public Function RunSQL(strSQL As String)
  DoCmd.SetWarnings False
  DoCmd.RunSQL (strSQL)
  DoCmd.SetWarnings True
End Function

Public Function RunMySQL(Optional strSQL As String)
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim db As Database, qDef As QueryDef

  Set db = CurrentDb()
  Set qDef = db.QueryDefs("qryMySQL_ActionSQL")
  If strSQL <> "" Then qDef.SQL = strSQL
  qDef.Execute
  db.Close

  Exit Function
err_exit:
  
  MsgBox "Napaka: (" & err.Number & ") " & err.description
  Resume Next
  
End Function

  CurrentCurrency = 1
End Function

Public Function YearDays(d As Date) As Integer
  
  If IsNull(d) Then YearDays = 365
  If (Year(d) Mod 4) = 0 Then YearDays = 366 Else YearDays = 365

End Function

Function AlignRight(d As Double, koliko As Byte) As String
On Error Resume Next
Dim x As String, i As Integer
  x = "0"
  For i = 1 To koliko
  x = x & "0"
  Next
  RSet x = Format(d, "Standard")
  AlignRight = x
End Function

'I left a bunch of development code in here in case anyone decides to go down the same paths I did.
'Created by Stephen Lebans with help from Chris Bergmans

'Production version of GetLineNumberForm
'Works in Form or SubForm mode
'Set controlsource of unbound Text box to
'= RowNum([Form])
'Type exactly as above

Public Function RowNum(frm As Form) As Variant
On Error GoTo Err_RowNum
    
    With frm.RecordsetClone
        .Bookmark = frm.Bookmark
        RowNum = .AbsolutePosition + 1
    End With
    
Exit_RowNum:
    Exit Function
    
Err_RowNum:
    If err.Number <> 3021& Then  'Ignore "No bookmark" at new row.

    End If
    RowNum = Null
    Resume Exit_RowNum
End Function

Public Function NewDocumentNumber(strTableName As String, numberFieldName As String, docPrefix As String, docYear As Long, whereID As Long, isCmpy As Boolean) As String
Dim lng As Integer, strWhere As String
  
  lng = Len(docPrefix)
  
  If isCmpy Then
    strWhere = "(companyID=" & whereID
  Else
    strWhere = "(costCenterID=" & whereID
  End If
  
  strWhere = strWhere & ") And Left(" & numberFieldName & "," & lng + 2 & ") = '" & docPrefix & Right(docYear, 2) & "' And (fiscalYear=" & docYear & ")"
  docPrefix = docPrefix & Right(docYear, 2) & "-"
  
  If DCount("*", strTableName, strWhere) = 0 Then
    NewDocumentNumber = docPrefix & "00001"
  Else
    NewDocumentNumber = docPrefix & Format(Int(DMax("Right(" & numberFieldName & ",5)", strTableName, strWhere)) + 1, "00000")
  End If

End Function

Public Function LastDayInMonth(ByVal AnyDate As Date, i As Integer) As Date
Dim mnth As Integer, Yr As Integer

' ************************************************** **********
' Returns the last day of AnyDate month if i=0, last day of next month if i=1, ...
' *****************************

  mnth = Month(AnyDate)
  Yr = Year(AnyDate) + Int((mnth + i) / 12)
  mnth = (Month(AnyDate) + i) Mod 12

  If mnth = 0 Then mnth = 1 Else mnth = mnth + 1

  LastDayInMonth = DateSerial(Yr, mnth, 0)
  
End Function

End Sub

Public Function GetBPName1(bpData)
  
  If IsNull(bpData) Then GetBPName1 = Null: Exit Function
  
  If Len(bpData) <> 0 Then
    GetBPName1 = Mid(bpData, 1, InStr(1, bpData, ";") - 1)
  Else
    GetBPName1 = Null
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function GetBPName2(bpData)
Dim startPosition As Integer, endPosition As Integer

  If Len(bpData) <> 0 Then
    startPosition = InStr(1, bpData, ";") + 1
    endPosition = InStr(startPosition, bpData, ";") + 1
    GetBPName2 = Mid(bpData, startPosition, endPosition - startPosition - 1)
    If Left(GetBPName2, 1) = " " Then
      GetBPName2 = Mid(GetBPName2, 2)
    End If
  Else
     GetBPName2 = Null
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function GetBPStreet(bpData)
Dim startPosition As Integer, endPosition As Integer

  If Len(bpData) <> 0 Then
    startPosition = InStr(InStr(1, bpData, ";") + 1, bpData, ";") + 1
    endPosition = InStr(startPosition, bpData, ";") + 1
    GetBPStreet = Mid(bpData, startPosition, endPosition - startPosition - 1)
  Else
    GetBPStreet = Null
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function GetBPPlace(bpData)
Dim startPosition As Integer, endPosition As Integer
  
  If Len(bpData) <> 0 Then
    startPosition = InStr(InStr(InStr(1, bpData, ";") + 1, bpData, ";") + 1, bpData, ";") + 1
    GetBPPlace = Mid(bpData, startPosition)
  Else
    GetBPPlace = Null
  End If
  
End Function

Public Function GetCmpyShortName(dat As Date, Optional companyID As Long)
Dim cmpyID As Long, strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  If Not IsMissing(companyID) And Not companyID = 0 Then
    cmpyID = companyID
  Else
    cmpyID = currentCompanyID()
  End If

   strSQL = "SELECT cmpyShortName1, cmpyShortName2, cmpyStreet, cmpyPlace, cmpyTaxNumber " & _
            "FROM tblCmpy_RegistrationData " & _
            "WHERE (((companyID)=" & cmpyID & ") AND ((CLng(CDate('" & dat & "'))) Between CLng([validFrom]) And IIf(IsNull([validTo]),2147483647,CLng([validTo]))));"
   
   Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
   If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then Exit Function
   
   GetCmpyShortName = rs("cmpyShortName1") & ", " & rs("cmpyTaxNumber")

End Function

Public Function GetCmpyData(fieldName As String, dat As Date, Optional companyID As Long)
On Error Resume Next
Dim cmpyID As String, strSQL As String, db As DAO.Database, rs As DAO.Recordset

  If hasValue(companyID) Then
    cmpyID = companyID
  Else
    cmpyID = currentCompanyID
  End If
  
   strSQL = "SELECT " & fieldName & " " & _
            "FROM tblCmpy_RegistrationData " & _
            "WHERE (((companyID)=" & cmpyID & ") AND ((CLng(CDate('" & dat & "'))) Between CLng([validFrom]) And IIf(IsNull([validTo]),2147483647,CLng([validTo]))));"
   
   Set db = CurrentDb()
   Set rs = db.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
   
   GetCmpyData = rs(fieldName)
   
   rs.Close
   Set rs = Nothing
   db.Close
   Set db = Nothing
   
End Function

Public Sub StayOnRecordRequery(frm As Form, IDName As String, ID As Long)      'stay on record after requery
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim id1, strFltr As String

  DoCmd.Echo False
  strFltr = frm.Filter
  frm.Requery
  If strFltr <> "" Then
    frm.Filter = strFltr
    If frm.FilterOn Then frm.FilterOn = True
  End If
    
  With frm.RecordsetClone
    .FindFirst IDName & "=" & ID

    If Not .NoMatch Then
      frm.Bookmark = .Bookmark
    End If
    
  End With

err_exit:
  DoCmd.Echo True
End Sub

Public Function hasValue(myExpression As Variant) As Boolean
Dim V As Variant
On Error GoTo Err_Handler
    
    hasValue = False
    
    If IsNull(myExpression) Then GoTo exit_Main
    If IsEmpty(myExpression) Then GoTo exit_Main
    If myExpression = "" Then GoTo exit_Main
    If myExpression Is Nothing Then GoTo exit_Main

    hasValue = True
    
    ' the assignment statement below will trigger error 2427 if the expression has no value.
    V = myExpression.Value
    
    GoTo exit_Main
Err_Handler:
    If err.Number = 2427 Then
      hasValue = False
    Else
      hasValue = True
      
      
    End If
    Resume Next
exit_Main:
End Function

Function LinkTable(dbName As String, sourceTblName As String, destTblName As String, deleteCurrentTable) As String
'Create an attached table in the current database from a table in a different MDB file.
On Error Resume Next

    If deleteCurrentTable And (Not IsNull(DLookup("(Type=6) AND (Name", "MSysObjects", "Name='" & destTblName & "')"))) Then
      DoCmd.DeleteObject acTable, destTblName
    End If

    On Error GoTo PROC_ERR

    DoCmd.TransferDatabase acLink, "Microsoft Access", dbName, acTable, sourceTblName, destTblName
    
PROC_EXIT:
  Exit Function

PROC_ERR:
  'Call GlobalErrHandler("LinkTable(" & strBaseTableName & ")", err.Number, err.description)
  
End Function

Public Function dbComputerName() As String
Dim strSQL As String, rs As DAO.Recordset

  strSQL = "SELECT tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers_1.computerName AS dbComputerName " & _
           "FROM tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers INNER JOIN tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers AS tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers_1 " & _
           "ON tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers.dbComputerID = tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers_1.computerID " & _
           "WHERE (((tblCmpyBusinessPremiseComputers.computerName)='" & currentComputerName() & "'));"

  Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset(strSQL)
  If rs.RecordCount = 0 Then dbComputerName = "": Exit Function
  
  dbComputerName = rs("dbComputerName")
End Function

Public Sub ContinousFormNavigation(KeyCode As Integer, frm As Form)
  
  If KeyCode = 40 Then
    If frm.CurrentRecord < frm.RecordsetClone.RecordCount Then
      DoCmd.GoToRecord , , acNext
    Else
      If frm.AllowAdditions Then
        If frm.CurrentRecord = frm.RecordsetClone.RecordCount Then
          DoCmd.GoToRecord , , acNext
        Else
          Beep
          KeyCode = 0
        End If
      Else
        Beep
        KeyCode = 0
      End If
    End If
  End If
  
  If KeyCode = 38 Then
    If frm.CurrentRecord > 1 Then
      DoCmd.GoToRecord , , acPrevious
    Else
     Beep
     KeyCode = 0
    End If
  End If
  
End Sub

Public Function GetDbName(dbFullName As String) As String
Dim iMax As Integer, iStart As Integer, tempChr As String, i As Integer

  iMax = Len(dbFullName)
  
  For i = 1 To iMax
    
    tempChr = Mid(dbFullName, i, 1)
    
    If tempChr = "\" Then
      iStart = i
    End If
  
  Next i

  For i = iStart To iMax
    
    tempChr = Mid(dbFullName, i + 1, 1)
    
    If tempChr = "\" Then
      Exit Function
    Else
      GetDbName = GetDbName & tempChr
    End If
    
  Next i

End Function

Public Function CreateReportPDF(docName As String, rptName As String, Optional strWhere As String) As String
On Error GoTo err_exit
Dim fileName As String
    
  fileName = "c:\Companies\Applications\PDFFiles\" & docName & ".pdf"
    
  DoCmd.OpenReport rptName, acViewPreview, , strWhere, acHidden
  DoCmd.OutputTo acOutputReport, rptName, acFormatPDF, fileName
  DoCmd.Close acReport, rptName, acSaveNo
  CreateReportPDF = docName
 
  Exit Function

err_exit:
  Resume Next
  MsgBox "napaka pri kreiranju poro ila :" & err.description
  End
  
End Function

Public Function SearchQueries(strTableName As String)
   Dim qdf As DAO.QueryDef
   Dim strSQL As String
   On Error GoTo ErrorHandler
   
   For Each qdf In CurrentDb.QueryDefs
       Application.Echo True, qdf.Name
       strSQL = qdf.SQL
       If InStr(1, strSQL, strTableName) > 0 Then
           Debug.Print qdf.Name
       End If
   Next qdf
   
   Set qdf = Nothing
   Beep
   MsgBox "Search Completed"
   
   Exit Function
ErrorHandler:
   If err.Number = 3258 Then
       strSQL = ""
       Resume
   End If
End Function

Function CompactFile(sourceFile As String)
On Error GoTo PROC_EXIT

Dim fso As Object, targetFile As String

  targetFile = Left(sourceFile, Len(sourceFile) - 6) & ".laccdb"
  If FileExists(targetFile) Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Ne morem stisniti in popraviti baze " & targetFile & ", ker je v uporabi. Preveri,  e so vse blagajne zaprte!", vbCritical
    Exit Function
  End If
  
  targetFile = Left(sourceFile, Len(sourceFile) - 6) & "1.accdb"
  If FileExists(targetFile) Then
    Kill (targetFile)
  End If
  
  Set fso = CreateObject("Scripting.FileSystemObject")
  Call fso.Copyfile(sourceFile, targetFile)
  
  Kill (sourceFile)
  
  Application.CompactRepair targetFile, sourceFile

PROC_EXIT:
  Exit Function

PROC_ERR:
  Resume Next
  MsgBox "Napaka (" & err.Number & ") - " & err.description

End Function

Function FileExists(ByVal strFile As String, Optional bFindFolders As Boolean) As Boolean
    'Purpose:   Return True if the file exists, even if it is hidden.
    'Arguments: strFile: File name to look for. Current directory searched if no path included.
    '           bFindFolders. If strFile is a folder, FileExists() returns False unless this argument is True.
    'Note:      Does not look inside subdirectories for the file.
    'Author:    Allen Browne. http://allenbrowne.com June, 2006.
    Dim lngAttributes As Long

    'Include read-only files, hidden files, system files.
    lngAttributes = (vbReadOnly Or vbHidden Or vbSystem)

    If bFindFolders Then
        lngAttributes = (lngAttributes Or vbDirectory) 'Include folders as well.
    Else
        'Strip any trailing slash, so Dir does not look inside the folder.
        Do While Right$(strFile, 1) = "\"
            strFile = Left$(strFile, Len(strFile) - 1)
        Loop
    End If

    'If Dir() returns something, the file exists.
    On Error Resume Next
    FileExists = (Len(Dir(strFile, lngAttributes)) > 0)
End Function

Sub GetControls()
On Error Resume Next

    Dim frm As Form
    Dim rs As Recordset
    Dim oCtrl As control

    Set frm = Forms!frmCRRestaurantPlan_Joe03.Form
    Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("tblFrmControls")
    
    For Each oCtrl In frm.Controls
      rs.AddNew
      rs("frmName") = frm.Name
      rs("controlName") = oCtrl.Name
      If oCtrl.ControlType = acTextBox Then
        rs("controlCaption") = oCtrl.Properties("DataSheetCaption")
      Else
        rs("controlCaption") = oCtrl.Caption
      End If
      rs("controlType") = oCtrl.ControlType
      rs("top") = oCtrl.Top
      rs("left") = oCtrl.Left
      rs("width") = oCtrl.Width
      rs("Height") = oCtrl.Height
      rs.Update
      
    Next
    Beep
End Sub

End Function

Public Sub OpenScan(tblID As Long, recID As Long)
Dim db As Database, rs As Recordset, totalDocuments As Integer, i As Integer

  Set db = CurrentDb
  Set rs = db.OpenRecordset("SELECT scanName FROM tblDoc_Scans WHERE recordTableID= " & tblID & " AND recordID=" & recID)
  
  totalDocuments = rs.RecordCount
  
  If totalDocuments = 0 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Pripeti dokumenti ne obstajajo.", vbInformation
    Exit Sub
  End If
  
  rs.MoveLast
  totalDocuments = rs.RecordCount
    
  rs.MoveFirst
  Do While Not rs.EOF
    FollowHyperlink ("https://www.skifun.eu/manager/actPreviewPdf.php?file=" & rs("scanName") & "&filename= " & rs("scanName"))
    rs.MoveNext
  Loop
  
  If totalDocuments > 1 Then
    Beep
    MsgBox "Obstaja ve  pripetih dokumentov: " & totalDocuments, vbInformation
  End If

End Sub

Public Sub ttttxxx()
Dim strSQL As String, rs As Recordset

Set rs = CurrentDb.OpenRecordset("00tables_cmpyID")
rs.MoveFirst
Do While Not rs.EOF
  
  rs.Edit
  rs("OK") = 1
  rs.Update
  
  strSQL = "ALTER TABLE `" & rs("tblName") & "` ADD FOREIGN KEY (`companyID`) REFERENCES `tblCmpy_Companies`(`companyID`) ON DELETE RESTRICT ON UPDATE CASCADE;"
  Call RunMySQL(strSQL)
  
  rs.MoveNext
Loop

End Sub
